# FILE: train.py
import math
import os
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from torch.nn import functional as F

torch.manual_seed(42)

DATA_FILE = "data.txt"
CHECKPOINT = "tiny_model.pt"

# Conservative starter settings for a 16 GB Mac.
BLOCK_SIZE = 64
BATCH_SIZE = 8
MAX_STEPS = 500
EVAL_EVERY = 100
LEARNING_RATE = 3e-4

N_EMBD = 96
N_HEAD = 4
N_LAYER = 3
DROPOUT = 0.1

if not os.path.exists(DATA_FILE):
    raise FileNotFoundError("Create data.txt before training.")

with open(DATA_FILE, encoding="utf-8") as f:
    text = f.read()

if len(text) < 2 * BLOCK_SIZE + 20:
    raise ValueError(
        f"data.txt needs at least {2 * BLOCK_SIZE + 20} characters "
        "for this starter configuration. Add more text."
    )

chars = sorted(set(text))
stoi = {ch: i for i, ch in enumerate(chars)}
itos = {i: ch for ch, i in stoi.items()}
encoded = torch.tensor([stoi[c] for c in text], dtype=torch.long)

split = int(len(encoded) * 0.9)
train_data, val_data = encoded[:split], encoded[split:]
if len(train_data) <= BLOCK_SIZE or len(val_data) <= BLOCK_SIZE:
    raise ValueError("Add more text so both splits exceed BLOCK_SIZE.")

device = "mps" if torch.backends.mps.is_available() else "cpu"
print("Device:", device)
print("Vocabulary size:", len(chars))
print("Characters:", len(text))


def get_batch(source):
    starts = torch.randint(len(source) - BLOCK_SIZE, (BATCH_SIZE,))
    x = torch.stack([source[i:i + BLOCK_SIZE] for i in starts])
    y = torch.stack([source[i + 1:i + BLOCK_SIZE + 1] for i in starts])
    return x.to(device), y.to(device)


class AttentionHead(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, head_size):
        super().__init__()
        self.key = nn.Linear(N_EMBD, head_size, bias=False)
        self.query = nn.Linear(N_EMBD, head_size, bias=False)
        self.value = nn.Linear(N_EMBD, head_size, bias=False)
        self.register_buffer(
            "mask", torch.tril(torch.ones(BLOCK_SIZE, BLOCK_SIZE))
        )

    def forward(self, x):
        _, t, _ = x.shape
        k, q, v = self.key(x), self.query(x), self.value(x)
        scores = q @ k.transpose(-2, -1) / math.sqrt(k.size(-1))
        scores = scores.masked_fill(
            self.mask[:t, :t] == 0, float("-inf")
        )
        weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
        return weights @ v


class MultiHeadAttention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        head_size = N_EMBD // N_HEAD
        self.heads = nn.ModuleList(
            [AttentionHead(head_size) for _ in range(N_HEAD)]
        )
        self.proj = nn.Linear(N_EMBD, N_EMBD)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(DROPOUT)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = torch.cat([head(x) for head in self.heads], dim=-1)
        return self.dropout(self.proj(x))


class TransformerBlock(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__()
        self.ln1 = nn.LayerNorm(N_EMBD)
        self.ln2 = nn.LayerNorm(N_EMBD)
        self.attn = MultiHeadAttention()
        self.ff = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(N_EMBD, 4 * N_EMBD),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(4 * N_EMBD, N_EMBD),
            nn.Dropout(DROPOUT),
        )

    def forward(self, x):
        x = x + self.attn(self.ln1(x))
        return x + self.ff(self.ln2(x))


class TinyGPT(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, vocab_size):
        super().__init__()
        self.token_emb = nn.Embedding(vocab_size, N_EMBD)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(BLOCK_SIZE, N_EMBD)
        self.blocks = nn.Sequential(
            *[TransformerBlock() for _ in range(N_LAYER)]
        )
        self.ln = nn.LayerNorm(N_EMBD)
        self.head = nn.Linear(N_EMBD, vocab_size)

    def forward(self, idx, targets=None):
        _, t = idx.shape
        positions = torch.arange(t, device=idx.device)
        x = self.token_emb(idx) + self.pos_emb(positions)
        logits = self.head(self.ln(self.blocks(x)))
        loss = None
        if targets is not None:
            loss = F.cross_entropy(
                logits.reshape(-1, logits.size(-1)),
                targets.reshape(-1),
            )
        return logits, loss

    @torch.no_grad()
    def generate(self, idx, max_new_tokens=300, temperature=0.8):
        self.eval()
        temperature = max(float(temperature), 1e-5)
        for _ in range(max_new_tokens):
            idx_cond = idx[:, -BLOCK_SIZE:]
            logits, _ = self(idx_cond)
            probs = F.softmax(logits[:, -1, :] / temperature, dim=-1)
            next_token = torch.multinomial(probs, num_samples=1)
            idx = torch.cat((idx, next_token), dim=1)
        return idx


model = TinyGPT(len(chars)).to(device)
print(f"Parameters: {sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters()):,}")
optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=LEARNING_RATE)


@torch.no_grad()
def estimate_loss():
    model.eval()
    results = {}
    for name, source in (("train", train_data), ("val", val_data)):
        losses = []
        for _ in range(5):
            x, y = get_batch(source)
            _, loss = model(x, y)
            losses.append(loss.item())
        results[name] = round(sum(losses) / len(losses), 4)
    model.train()
    return results


model.train()
for step in range(MAX_STEPS):
    if step % EVAL_EVERY == 0:
        print(f"step {step}: {estimate_loss()}")
    x, y = get_batch(train_data)
    _, loss = model(x, y)
    optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
    loss.backward()
    torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)
    optimizer.step()

print("Final loss:", estimate_loss())
torch.save(
    {
        "model": model.cpu().state_dict(),
        "chars": chars,
        "config": {
            "block_size": BLOCK_SIZE,
            "n_embd": N_EMBD,
            "n_head": N_HEAD,
            "n_layer": N_LAYER,
            "dropout": DROPOUT,
        },
    },
    CHECKPOINT,
)
print(f"Saved checkpoint to {CHECKPOINT}")


# FILE: generate.py
import argparse
import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from torch.nn import functional as F


class AttentionHead(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, n_embd, head_size, block_size):
        super().__init__()
        self.key = nn.Linear(n_embd, head_size, bias=False)
        self.query = nn.Linear(n_embd, head_size, bias=False)
        self.value = nn.Linear(n_embd, head_size, bias=False)
        self.register_buffer(
            "mask", torch.tril(torch.ones(block_size, block_size))
        )

    def forward(self, x):
        t = x.size(1)
        k, q, v = self.key(x), self.query(x), self.value(x)
        scores = q @ k.transpose(-2, -1) / math.sqrt(k.size(-1))
        scores = scores.masked_fill(
            self.mask[:t, :t] == 0, float("-inf")
        )
        return F.softmax(scores, dim=-1) @ v


class MultiHeadAttention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, n_embd, n_head, block_size, dropout):
        super().__init__()
        head_size = n_embd // n_head
        self.heads = nn.ModuleList([
            AttentionHead(n_embd, head_size, block_size)
            for _ in range(n_head)
        ])
        self.proj = nn.Linear(n_embd, n_embd)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = torch.cat([head(x) for head in self.heads], dim=-1)
        return self.dropout(self.proj(x))


class TransformerBlock(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, n_embd, n_head, block_size, dropout):
        super().__init__()
        self.ln1 = nn.LayerNorm(n_embd)
        self.ln2 = nn.LayerNorm(n_embd)
        self.attn = MultiHeadAttention(n_embd, n_head, block_size, dropout)
        self.ff = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(n_embd, 4 * n_embd),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(4 * n_embd, n_embd),
            nn.Dropout(dropout),
        )

    def forward(self, x):
        x = x + self.attn(self.ln1(x))
        return x + self.ff(self.ln2(x))


class TinyGPT(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, vocab_size, cfg):
        super().__init__()
        n_embd = cfg["n_embd"]
        block_size = cfg["block_size"]
        dropout = cfg["dropout"]
        self.block_size = block_size
        self.token_emb = nn.Embedding(vocab_size, n_embd)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(block_size, n_embd)
        self.blocks = nn.Sequential(*[
            TransformerBlock(
                n_embd, cfg["n_head"], block_size, dropout
            )
            for _ in range(cfg["n_layer"])
        ])
        self.ln = nn.LayerNorm(n_embd)
        self.head = nn.Linear(n_embd, vocab_size)

    def forward(self, idx):
        t = idx.size(1)
        pos = torch.arange(t, device=idx.device)
        x = self.token_emb(idx) + self.pos_emb(pos)
        return self.head(self.ln(self.blocks(x)))

    @torch.no_grad()
    def generate(self, idx, count, temperature):
        self.eval()
        for _ in range(count):
            logits = self(idx[:, -self.block_size:])[:, -1, :]
            probs = F.softmax(logits / max(temperature, 1e-5), dim=-1)
            idx = torch.cat([idx, torch.multinomial(probs, 1)], dim=1)
        return idx


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--checkpoint", default="tiny_model.pt")
    parser.add_argument("--prompt", default="A language model")
    parser.add_argument("--tokens", type=int, default=300)
    parser.add_argument("--temperature", type=float, default=0.8)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    device = "mps" if torch.backends.mps.is_available() else "cpu"
    # Checkpoints are created by this project and should be treated as trusted local files.
    checkpoint = torch.load(args.checkpoint, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
    chars = checkpoint["chars"]
    stoi = {c: i for i, c in enumerate(chars)}
    itos = {i: c for i, c in enumerate(chars)}
    unknown = sorted(set(args.prompt) - set(stoi))
    if unknown:
        raise SystemExit(
            "Prompt contains characters not in training vocabulary: "
            + repr("".join(unknown))
            + ". Use a prompt with characters found in data.txt."
        )

    model = TinyGPT(len(chars), checkpoint["config"])
    model.load_state_dict(checkpoint["model"])
    model = model.to(device).eval()
    idx = torch.tensor([[stoi[c] for c in args.prompt]], dtype=torch.long, device=device)
    output = model.generate(idx, args.tokens, args.temperature)[0].tolist()
    print("".join(itos[i] for i in output))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

        import subprocess

        import whisper

        import pyttsx3
        print("pyttsx3 (TTS) available")
    except ImportError:
        print("Installing pyttsx3...")
        import subprocess
        subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install", "pyttsx3"], check=True)
    
    try:
        import cv2
        print("OpenCV (screen perception) available")
    except ImportError:
        print("Installing OpenCV...")
        import subprocess
        subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install", "opencv-python"], check=True)
    
    print("Requirements check complete!\n")

    main()

# FILE: prepare_data.py
import os
from pathlib import Path

def collect_local_python_files(root_dir):
    texts = []
    root_path = Path(root_dir)
    for path in root_path.rglob('*.py'):
        if any(part.startswith('.') for part in path.parts):
            continue
        try:
            with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f_in:
                content = f_in.read()
            if len(content.strip()) == 0:
                continue
            texts.append(f"# FILE: {path.relative_to(root_path)}\n{content}\n\n")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Skipping {path}: {e}")
    return ''.join(texts)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    corpus = collect_local_python_files('.')
    output_file = 'code_corpus.txt'
    with open(output_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f_out:
        f_out.write(corpus)
    print(f"Success! Wrote {len(corpus)} characters from local Python files to {output_file}")


# FILE: test_agi_core.py
#!/usr/bin/env python
"""
Comprehensive Test Suite for AGI Core

This script tests all major components of the AGI Core system.
Run with: python test_agi_core.py

Options:
  --quick    Run only basic tests
  --nlp      Test NLP modules
  --llm      Test LLM modules (requires PyTorch)
  --all      Test everything
"""

import sys
import os
import time
from typing import List, Dict, Any

# Add project root to path
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))


def print_section(title: str):
    """Print a section header"""
    print(f"\n{'=' * 70}")
    print(f"  {title}")
    print(f"{'=' * 70}")


def print_test(test_name: str, passed: bool = True, message: str = ""):
    """Print test result"""
    status = "✅ PASS" if passed else "❌ FAIL"
    print(f"   {status}: {test_name}")
    if message:
        print(f"      {message}")


def test_agi_core_basic():
    """Test basic AGI Core functionality"""
    print_section("AGI Core Basic Tests")
    
    from agi_core.integration import AGIAgent
    
    # Test initialization
    try:
        agent = AGIAgent(name='TestAGI', use_purple=False)
        agent.initialize()
        print_test("AGI Agent initialization")
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("AGI Agent initialization", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    # Test conversation
    try:
        result = agent.converse("Hello!")
        assert 'response' in result
        print_test("Conversation")
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("Conversation", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    # Test reasoning
    try:
        chain = agent.think("What is AI?", max_steps=3)
        assert chain is not None
        assert len(chain.steps) > 0
        print_test("Reasoning engine")
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("Reasoning engine", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    # Test memory
    try:
        agent.remember("Test fact", "fact")
        results = agent.recall("Test")
        assert len(results) > 0
        print_test("Memory system")
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("Memory system", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    # Test learning
    try:
        agent.learn("TestConcept", "A test concept")
        print_test("Learning system")
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("Learning system", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    # Test planning
    try:
        plan = agent.plan("Test goal")
        assert 'actions' in plan or 'plan_id' in plan
        print_test("Planning system")
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("Planning system", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    # Test decision making
    try:
        decision = agent.decide("Test?", ["Option1", "Option2"])
        assert 'chosen_option' in decision
        print_test("Decision making")
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("Decision making", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    # Test state
    try:
        state = agent.get_state()
        assert 'memory_stats' in state
        print_test("State management")
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("State management", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    return True


def test_nlp_modules():
    """Test NLP modules"""
    print_section("NLP Modules Tests")
    
    from agi_core.nlp import TextProcessor, IntentClassifier, EntityExtractor
    
    # Test TextProcessor
    try:
        processor = TextProcessor()
        
        # Test tokenization
        tokens = processor.tokenizer.tokenize_words("Hello World")
        assert len(tokens) == 2
        print_test("Text tokenization")
        
        # Test normalization
        normalized = processor.normalize("Hello, World!")
        assert "hello" in normalized.lower()
        print_test("Text normalization")
        
        # Test n-grams
        ngrams = processor.generate_ngrams("hello world test", n=2)
        assert len(ngrams) > 0
        print_test("N-gram generation")
        
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("TextProcessor", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    # Test IntentClassifier
    try:
        classifier = IntentClassifier()
        
        # Test question detection
        intent = classifier.classify("What is AI?")
        assert intent.intent_type.value == "question"
        print_test("Question detection")
        
        # Test greeting detection
        intent = classifier.classify("Hello!")
        assert intent.intent_type.value == "greeting"
        print_test("Greeting detection")
        
        # Test sentiment analysis
        intent = classifier.classify("I love this!")
        assert intent.sentiment > 0
        print_test("Sentiment analysis")
        
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("IntentClassifier", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    # Test EntityExtractor
    try:
        extractor = EntityExtractor()
        
        # Test entity extraction
        entities = extractor.extract("John works at Google in New York")
        assert len(entities) > 0
        print_test("Entity extraction")
        
        # Test specific entity types
        entities = extractor.extract_by_type("john@example.com", extractor.EntityType.EMAIL)
        assert len(entities) > 0
        print_test("Email extraction")
        
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("EntityExtractor", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    return True


def test_reasoning_modules():
    """Test reasoning modules"""
    print_section("Reasoning Modules Tests")
    
    from agi_core.reasoning import ReasoningEngine, ReasoningMode
    
    try:
        engine = ReasoningEngine()
        
        # Test chain-of-thought
        chain = engine.chain_of_thought("What is AI?", max_steps=3)
        assert chain is not None
        assert len(chain.steps) > 0
        print_test("Chain-of-thought reasoning")
        
        # Test tree-of-thoughts
        chain = engine.tree_of_thoughts("Test problem", branches=2, depth=2)
        assert chain is not None
        print_test("Tree-of-thoughts reasoning")
        
        # Test verification
        result = engine.verify_reasoning(chain)
        assert 'is_valid' in result
        print_test("Reasoning verification")
        
        # Test different modes
        chain = engine.reason("Test", ReasoningMode.CHAIN_OF_THOUGHT, max_steps=2)
        assert chain is not None
        print_test("Reasoning modes")
        
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("ReasoningEngine", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    return True


def test_memory_modules():
    """Test memory modules"""
    print_section("Memory Modules Tests")
    
    from agi_core.memory import MemorySystem, ShortTermMemory, LongTermMemory
    
    try:
        # Test ShortTermMemory
        stm = ShortTermMemory(max_items=10)
        
        # Test adding items
        item_id = stm.add("Test item", "fact")
        assert item_id is not None
        print_test("STM add item")
        
        # Test retrieval
        item = stm.get(item_id)
        assert item is not None
        print_test("STM retrieval")
        
        # Test search
        results = stm.search("Test")
        assert len(results) > 0
        print_test("STM search")
        
        # Test context windows
        window = stm.create_context_window(focus="test")
        assert window is not None
        print_test("STM context windows")
        
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("ShortTermMemory", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    try:
        # Test LongTermMemory
        ltm = LongTermMemory()
        
        # Test storing concepts
        concept = ltm.store_concept("Test", "A test concept")
        assert concept is not None
        print_test("LTM store concept")
        
        # Test retrieving concepts
        retrieved = ltm.retrieve_concept("Test")
        assert retrieved is not None
        print_test("LTM retrieve concept")
        
        # Test storing facts
        fact = ltm.store_fact("Test fact", "Test", "is", "test")
        assert fact is not None
        print_test("LTM store fact")
        
        # Test search
        results = ltm.search("Test")
        assert 'concepts' in results or 'facts' in results
        print_test("LTM search")
        
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("LongTermMemory", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    try:
        # Test MemorySystem
        memory = MemorySystem(stm_capacity=20)
        
        # Test unified storage
        item_id = memory.store("Test", "fact")
        assert item_id is not None
        print_test("MemorySystem store")
        
        # Test unified retrieval
        item = memory.retrieve(item_id)
        assert item is not None
        print_test("MemorySystem retrieve")
        
        # Test unified query
        results = memory.query("Test")
        assert len(results) > 0
        print_test("MemorySystem query")
        
        # Test learning
        concept_id = memory.learn_concept("AI", "Artificial Intelligence")
        assert concept_id is not None
        print_test("MemorySystem learn")
        
        # Test experience
        exp_id = memory.remember_experience("Test experience", "A test")
        assert exp_id is not None
        print_test("MemorySystem experience")
        
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("MemorySystem", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    return True


def test_planning_modules():
    """Test planning modules"""
    print_section("Planning Modules Tests")
    
    from agi_core.planning import Planner, DecisionEngine, DecisionStrategy
    
    try:
        planner = Planner()
        
        # Test goal creation
        goal = planner.create_goal("Test goal", priority=0.8)
        assert goal is not None
        print_test("Goal creation")
        
        # Test plan creation
        plan = planner.create_plan(goal.goal_id, "Test plan")
        assert plan is not None
        print_test("Plan creation")
        
        # Test planning
        plan = planner.plan_for_goal(goal.goal_id, max_actions=3)
        assert plan is not None
        print_test("Automatic planning")
        
        # Test goal management
        goals = planner.get_active_goals()
        assert len(goals) >= 0  # May be 0 or more
        print_test("Goal management")
        
        # Test stats
        stats = planner.get_stats()
        assert 'total_goals' in stats
        print_test("Planner stats")
        
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("Planner", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    try:
        engine = DecisionEngine()
        
        # Test decision creation
        decision = engine.create_decision("Test decision", "Context")
        assert decision is not None
        print_test("Decision creation")
        
        # Test option evaluation
        decision.add_option(engine.Option("opt1", "Option 1"))
        decision.add_option(engine.Option("opt2", "Option 2"))
        decision.add_criterion(engine.Criterion("quality", "Quality criterion", 1.0))
        decision.make_decision()
        assert decision.chosen_option is not None
        print_test("Decision making")
        
        # Test strategy selection
        decision2 = engine.make_decision(
            "Test", ["A", "B", "C"],
            strategy=DecisionStrategy.MAXIMIZE_UTILITY
        )
        assert decision2.chosen_option is not None
        print_test("Decision strategies")
        
    except Exception as e:
        print_test("DecisionEngine", False, str(e))
        return False
    
    return True

def run_tests(test_types: List[str] = None):
    """Run all specified tests"""
    start_time = time.time()
    
    print("=" * 70)
    print("  AGI Core Comprehensive Test Suite")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    test_types = test_types or ['basic', 'nlp', 'reasoning', 'memory', 'planning', 'world', 'meta']
    
    results = {}
    
    # Run tests
    if 'basic' in test_types or 'all' in test_types:
        results['basic'] = test_agi_core_basic()
    
    if 'nlp' in test_types or 'all' in test_types:
        results['nlp'] = test_nlp_modules()
    
    if 'reasoning' in test_types or 'all' in test_types:
        results['reasoning'] = test_reasoning_modules()
    
    if 'memory' in test_types or 'all' in test_types:
        results['memory'] = test_memory_modules()
    
    if 'planning' in test_types or 'all' in test_types:
        results['planning'] = test_planning_modules()
    
    if 'world' in test_types or 'all' in test_types:
        results['world'] = test_world_model()
    
    if 'meta' in test_types or 'all' in test_types:
        results['meta'] = test_meta_learning()
    
    if 'llm' in test_types or 'all' in test_types:
        results['llm'] = test_llm_modules()
    
    # Print summary
    print_section("Test Summary")
    
    passed = sum(1 for v in results.values() if v)
    total = len(results)
    
    for test_type, result in results.items():
        status = "✅ PASS" if result else "❌ FAIL"
        print(f"   {status}: {test_type}")
    
    print(f"\n   Total: {passed}/{total} test suites passed")
    
    elapsed = time.time() - start_time
    print(f"   Time: {elapsed:.2f} seconds")
    
    return passed == total


def main():
    """Main entry point"""
    import argparse
    
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description='AGI Core Comprehensive Test Suite'
    )
    parser.add_argument('--quick', action='store_true', 
                        help='Run only basic tests')
    parser.add_argument('--nlp', action='store_true',
                        help='Test NLP modules')
    parser.add_argument('--llm', action='store_true',
                        help='Test LLM modules')
    parser.add_argument('--all', action='store_true',
                        help='Test everything')
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    # Determine which tests to run
    if args.all:
        test_types = ['all']
    elif args.quick:
        test_types = ['basic']
    else:
        test_types = ['basic']
        if args.nlp:
            test_types.append('nlp')
        if args.llm:
            test_types.append('llm')
    
    # Run tests
    success = run_tests(test_types)
    
    # Exit with appropriate code
    sys.exit(0 if success else 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


# FILE: modules/tts.py
"""
Text-to-Speech Module
Uses pyttsx3 for offline TTS with emotion adaptation
"""
import pyttsx3
import numpy as np
import tempfile
import os
from typing import Optional


class TTSModule:
    """Text-to-Speech using pyttsx3"""
    
    def __init__(self, rate=150, volume=0.9, voice_id=None, use_offline=False):
        """
        Initialize TTS engine
        rate: words per minute
        volume: 0.0 to 1.0
        voice_id: None for default, or specific voice index
        use_offline: whether to try pyttsx3 (may fail in headless environments)
        """
        self.use_offline = use_offline
        self.rate = rate
        self.volume = volume
        
        # Try to initialize pyttsx3, but handle failures gracefully
        self.engine = None
        self._init_successful = False
        
        if use_offline:
            try:
                self.engine = pyttsx3.init()
                self.engine.setProperty('rate', rate)
                self.engine.setProperty('volume', volume)
                
                # Set voice
                voices = self.engine.getProperty('voices')
                if voice_id is not None and voice_id < len(voices):
                    self.engine.setProperty('voice', voices[voice_id].id)
                
                self._init_successful = True
                print("pyttsx3 TTS engine initialized successfully")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"pyttsx3 initialization failed: {e}")
                print("TTS will use text output only")
                self.engine = None
                self._init_successful = False
        else:
            print("TTS mode: text output only (offline disabled)")
            self._init_successful = False
    
    def speak(self, text: str, emotion: str = "neutral") -> Optional[str]:
        """
        Speak text with optional emotion adaptation
        Returns path to saved audio file, or None if TTS not available
        """
        if self.engine is None or not self._init_successful:
            # Print text instead of speaking
            print(f"[TTS would speak]: {text} (emotion: {emotion})")
            return None
        
        try:
            # Modify voice properties based on emotion
            emotion_modifiers = {
                "happy": {"rate": 160, "volume": 1.0},
                "sad": {"rate": 120, "volume": 0.7},
                "angry": {"rate": 180, "volume": 0.9},
                "excited": {"rate": 200, "volume": 1.0},
                "calm": {"rate": 130, "volume": 0.8},
                "neutral": {"rate": 150, "volume": 0.9}
            }
            
            modifier = emotion_modifiers.get(emotion, emotion_modifiers["neutral"])
            self.engine.setProperty('rate', modifier["rate"])
            self.engine.setProperty('volume', modifier["volume"])
            
            # Save to temp file
            audio_path = os.path.join(self.temp_dir, f"speech_{hash(text) % 10000}.wav")
            self.engine.save_to_file(text, audio_path)
            self.engine.runAndWait()
            
            return audio_path
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"TTS error: {e}")
            # Fall back to printing
            print(f"[TTS fallback]: {text}")
            return None
    
    def speak_immediate(self, text: str, emotion: str = "neutral"):
        """Speak text immediately (blocking)"""
        self.speak(text, emotion)

# FILE: modules/emotion.py
"""
Emotion Perception Module
Detects emotion from audio features
"""
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
import librosa
import numpy as np


class EmotionFeatureExtractor(nn.Module):
    """Extracts emotion-relevant features from audio"""
    
    def __init__(self, n_mfcc=40, n_fft=2048, hop_length=512):
        super().__init__()
        self.n_mfcc = n_mfcc
        self.n_fft = n_fft
        self.hop_length = hop_length
        
        # CNN for feature extraction from spectrograms
        self.conv1 = nn.Conv1d(n_mfcc, 64, kernel_size=3, padding=1)
        self.conv2 = nn.Conv1d(64, 128, kernel_size=3, padding=1)
        self.conv3 = nn.Conv1d(128, 256, kernel_size=3, padding=1)
        
        # Emotion classification head
        self.classifier = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(256, 128),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Dropout(0.3),
            nn.Linear(128, 8)  # 8 emotion categories
        )
        
        # Mood regression head (valence, arousal, dominance)
        self.mood_regressor = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(256, 128),
            nn.ReLU(),
            nn.Dropout(0.3),
            nn.Linear(128, 3)
        )
    
    def extract_mfcc(self, audio):
        """Extract MFCC features from audio"""
        mfcc = librosa.feature.mfcc(
            y=audio, 
            sr=16000, 
            n_mfcc=self.n_mfcc,
            n_fft=self.n_fft,
            hop_length=self.hop_length
        )
        return mfcc  # shape: (n_mfcc, time_steps)
    
    def forward(self, audio_tensor):
        """
        audio_tensor: (batch, audio_length) or (audio_length,)
        Returns: emotion_logits (batch, 8), mood (batch, 3)
        """
        if audio_tensor.dim() == 1:
            audio_tensor = audio_tensor.unsqueeze(0)
        
        # Extract MFCC features
        mfcc = self.extract_mfcc(audio_tensor.squeeze().numpy())
        mfcc_tensor = torch.from_numpy(mfcc).float().unsqueeze(0)  # (1, n_mfcc, time)
        
        # Pass through CNN
        x = F.relu(self.conv1(mfcc_tensor))
        x = F.relu(self.conv2(x))
        x = F.relu(self.conv3(x))
        
        # Global average pooling
        x = x.mean(dim=-1)  # (1, channels, 1) -> (1, channels)
        
        # Get emotion logits and mood
        emotion_logits = self.classifier(x.squeeze(0))  # (8,)
        mood = self.mood_regressor(x.squeeze(0))  # (3,)
        
        return emotion_logits, mood


class MoodState:
    """Persistent mood engine with valence, arousal, dominance dimensions"""
    
    EMOTION_CATEGORIES = ['stressed', 'angry', 'excited', 'happy', 'calm', 'neutral', 'tired', 'sad']
    
    def __init__(self, initial_valence=0.5, initial_arousal=0.5, initial_dominance=0.5, decay_rate=0.95):
        self.valence = initial_valence  # -1 to 1 (negative to positive)
        self.arousal = initial_arousal  # 0 to 1 (calm to excited)
        self.dominance = initial_dominance  # 0 to 1 (powerless to powerful)
        self.decay_rate = decay_rate
        self.emotion_history = []
    
    def update(self, emotion_logits, mood):
        """Update mood state from emotion detection"""
        # Get dominant emotion
        emo_idx = torch.argmax(emotion_logits).item()
        emotion_name = self.EMOTION_CATEGORIES[emo_idx]
        
        # Update mood from regression output
        self.valence = float(torch.sigmoid(mood[0]) * 2 - 1)  # -1 to 1
        self.arousal = float(torch.sigmoid(mood[1]))  # 0 to 1
        self.dominance = float(torch.sigmoid(mood[2]))  # 0 to 1
        
        # Apply decay toward neutral
        self.valence = self.valence * self.decay_rate + 0 * (1 - self.decay_rate)
        self.arousal = self.arousal * self.decay_rate + 0.5 * (1 - self.decay_rate)
        self.dominance = self.dominance * self.decay_rate + 0.5 * (1 - self.decay_rate)
        
        # Store history
        self.emotion_history.append({
            'emotion': emotion_name,
            'valence': self.valence,
            'arousal': self.arousal,
            'dominance': self.dominance
        })
        
        # Keep only recent history
        if len(self.emotion_history) > 50:
            self.emotion_history = self.emotion_history[-50:]
        
        return emotion_name
    
    def get_mood_description(self):
        """Get human-readable mood description"""
        emotion_idx = int((self.arousal * 7 + self.valence * 3 + self.dominance * 2) % 8)
        return self.EMOTION_CATEGORIES[emotion_idx]
    
    def to_dict(self):
        """Serialize mood state"""
        return {
            'valence': self.valence,
            'arousal': self.arousal,
            'dominance': self.dominance,
            'emotion_history': self.emotion_history
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data):
        """Deserialize mood state"""
        mood = cls(
            initial_valence=data.get('valence', 0.5),
            initial_arousal=data.get('arousal', 0.5),
            initial_dominance=data.get('dominance', 0.5)
        )
        mood.emotion_history = data.get('emotion_history', [])
        return mood

# FILE: modules/stt.py
"""
Speech-to-Text Module
Uses OpenAI Whisper for transcription
"""
import torch
import whisper
import tempfile
import os
from typing import Optional


class STTModule:
    """Speech-to-Text using Whisper"""
    
    def __init__(self, model_size="base", device=None):
        """
        Initialize Whisper model
        model_size: "tiny", "base", "small", "medium", "large"
        device: "cpu", "cuda", or None (auto-detect)
        """
        self.device = device or ("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
        print(f"Loading Whisper {model_size} on {self.device}...")
        self.model = whisper.load_model(model_size, device=self.device)
        print("STT model loaded successfully!")
    
    def transcribe(self, audio_path: str) -> dict:
        """
        Transcribe audio file
        Returns dict with: text, language, duration, segments
        """
        if not os.path.exists(audio_path):
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"Audio file not found: {audio_path}")
        
        result = self.model.transcribe(audio_path)
        return result
    
    def transcribe_from_array(self, audio_array: torch.Tensor, sample_rate: int = 16000) -> dict:
        """
        Transcribe from audio tensor
        """
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix=".wav", delete=False) as tmp:
            tmp_path = tmp.name
            # Save as WAV
            import soundfile as sf
            sf.write(tmp_path, audio_array.numpy(), sample_rate)
        
        try:
            return self.transcribe(tmp_path)
        finally:
            if os.path.exists(tmp_path):
                os.unlink(tmp_path)
    
    def list_available_models(self):
        """List available Whisper model sizes"""
        return ["tiny", "base", "small", "medium", "large"]

# FILE: modules/screen_perception.py
"""
Screen Perception Module
Captures and analyzes screen content
"""
import numpy as np
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any

try:
    import mss
    HAS_MSS = True
except ImportError:
    HAS_MSS = False


class ScreenCapture:
    """Captures screen content"""
    
    def __init__(self, monitor=None):
        """
        Initialize screen capture
        monitor: None for full screen, or dict with {top, left, width, height}
        """
        self.monitor = monitor
    
    def capture(self) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
        """
        Capture screen image
        Returns: numpy array (H, W, 3) in RGB format, or None on error
        """
        try:
            if HAS_MSS:
                return self._capture_with_mss()
            else:
                # Fallback: return None and let caller handle it
                print("No screen capture library available (install mss)")
                return None
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Screen capture error: {e}")
            return None
    
    def _capture_with_mss(self) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
        """Capture screen using mss library"""
        try:
            with mss() as sct:
                # Get primary monitor
                monitor = sct.monitors[1]  # Index 1 is the primary monitor
                
                if self.monitor:
                    # Capture specific region
                    x = self.monitor.get('left', monitor['left'])
                    y = self.monitor.get('top', monitor['top'])
                    w = self.monitor.get('width', monitor['width'])
                    h = self.monitor.get('height', monitor['height'])
                    region = {'top': y, 'left': x, 'width': w, 'height': h}
                    sct_img = sct.grab(region)
                else:
                    # Capture full screen
                    sct_img = sct.grab(monitor)
                
                # Convert to numpy array (BGR to RGB)
                img = np.array(sct_img)
                img = img[:, :, :3]  # Remove alpha channel if present
                
                # mss returns BGRA, convert to RGB
                if img.shape[2] == 4:
                    img = img[:, :, :3]
                
                # Convert BGR to RGB (mss captures in BGR format)
                img = img[:, :, ::-1]
                
                return img
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"mSS capture error: {e}")
            return None


class ContentAnalyzer:
    """Analyzes screen content for context"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        pass
    
    def analyze(self, image: np.ndarray) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Analyze screen content
        Returns dict with: text_elements, objects, layout_info, etc.
        """
        analysis = {
            'text_elements': [],
            'objects': [],
            'dominant_colors': [],
            'overall_context': 'unknown'
        }
        
        if image is None:
            return analysis
        
        # Placeholder for actual analysis
        # Would use OCR (tesseract), object detection, etc.
        
        return analysis
    
    def extract_text(self, image: np.ndarray) -> str:
        """Extract text from image using OCR"""
        # Placeholder - would use pytesseract
        return ""


class PerceptionState:
    """Persistent perception state across sessions"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.last_image = None
        self.last_analysis = {}
        self.text_history = []
        self.object_history = []
    
    def update(self, image: np.ndarray, analysis: Dict):
        """Update perception state"""
        self.last_image = image
        self.last_analysis = analysis
        self.text_history.append(analysis.get('text_elements', [])[-1:] if analysis else [])
        self.object_history.append(analysis.get('objects', [])[-1:] if analysis else [])

# FILE: modules/desktop_automation.py
"""
Desktop Automation Module
Provides mouse and keyboard control
"""
import keyboard
import time
import numpy as np
from typing import Optional, Tuple, List


class DesktopController:
    """Controls mouse and keyboard for desktop automation"""
    
    def __init__(self, speed=1.0, pause_between_actions=0.1):
        """
        Initialize desktop controller
        speed: multiplier for mouse movement speed
        pause_between_actions: default pause in seconds
        """
        self.FAILSAFE = True
        self.PAUSE = pause_between_actions
        self.speed = speed
        # Default screen size fallback
        self.screen_size = (1920, 1080)
    
    # Mouse operations
    def move_to(self, x: int, y: int, duration: Optional[float] = None):
        """Move mouse to absolute position"""
        duration = duration or (abs(x) + abs(y)) / 1000 * self.speed
        pyautogui.moveTo(x, y, duration=duration)
    
    def click(self, x: Optional[int] = None, y: Optional[int] = None, 
              button: str = 'left', clicks: int = 1):
        """Click at position or current position"""
        if x is not None and y is not None:
            self.move_to(x, y)
        pyautogui.click(x=x, y=y, button=button, clicks=clicks)
    
    def double_click(self, x: Optional[int] = None, y: Optional[int] = None):
        """Double-click at position"""
        if x is not None and y is not None:
            self.move_to(x, y)
        pyautogui.doubleClick(x=x, y=y)
    
    def right_click(self, x: Optional[int] = None, y: Optional[int] = None):
        """Right-click at position"""
        if x is not None and y is not None:
            self.move_to(x, y)
        pyautogui.rightClick(x=x, y=y)
    
    def drag_to(self, x: int, y: int, duration: float = 1.0):
        """Drag mouse to position"""
        self.move_to(x, y, duration=duration)
        pyautogui.dragTo(x, y, duration=duration, button='left')
    
    # Keyboard operations
    def type(self, text: str, interval: float = 0.01):
        """Type text"""
        pyautogui.typewrite(text, interval=interval)
    
    def press(self, key: str):
        """Press a single key"""
        pyautogui.press(key)
    
    def write(self, text: str, interval: float = 0.01):
        """Write text (alias for type)"""
        self.type(text, interval)
    
    def press_keyboard(self, key: str, press_duration: float = 0.1):
        """Press and hold a key"""
        keyboard.press(key)
        time.sleep(press_duration)
        keyboard.release(key)
    
    # Convenience methods
    def scroll(self, amount: int):
        """Scroll mouse wheel"""
        pyautogui.scroll(amount)
    
    def press_hotkey(self, *keys: str):
        """Press hotkey combination"""
        pyautogui.hotkey(*keys)


class AutomationSequence:
    """Sequence of automated actions"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.steps = []
    
    def add_click(self, x: int, y: int, button: str = 'left', clicks: int = 1):
        """Add click step"""
        self.steps.append(('click', {'x': x, 'y': y, 'button': button, 'clicks': clicks}))
        return self
    
    def add_type(self, text: str, interval: float = 0.01):
        """Add type step"""
        self.steps.append(('type', {'text': text, 'interval': interval}))
        return self
    
    def add_press(self, key: str):
        """Add key press step"""
        self.steps.append(('press', {'key': key}))
        return self
    
    def execute(self, controller: DesktopController):
        """Execute the sequence"""
        for step_type, params in self.steps:
            if step_type == 'click':
                controller.click(**params)
            elif step_type == 'type':
                controller.type(**params)
            elif step_type == 'press':
                controller.press(**params)


class SafeModeGuard:
    """Safe-mode guardrails for desktop automation"""
    
    PROTECTED_AREAS = {
        'cursor_position': None,  # Don't move cursor to certain areas
        'critical_systems': ['task_manager', 'system_settings', 'terminal'],
        'max_clicks_per_minute': 60,
    }
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.clicks_this_minute = 0
        self.last_click_time = time.time()
    
    def can_execute(self, action: str, **kwargs) -> bool:
        """Check if action is safe to execute"""
        # Check protected systems
        if 'click' in action.lower() or 'press' in action.lower():
            current_time = time.time()
            if current_time - self.last_click_time < 60:
                self.clicks_this_minute += 1
            else:
                self.clicks_this_minute = 1
                self.last_click_time = current_time
            
            if self.clicks_this_minute > self.PROTECTED_AREAS['max_clicks_per_minute']:
                print(f"Safe-mode: Too many clicks ({self.clicks_this_minute}/minute)")
                return False
        
        return True

# FILE: modules/self_healing.py
"""
Self-Healing Code System
Detects and fixes its own errors
"""
import traceback
import sys
import re
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List


class ErrorInfo:
    """Information about a detected error"""
    
    def __init__(self, error_type: str, message: str, 
                 file_path: Optional[str] = None, 
                 line_number: Optional[int] = None,
                 stack_trace: Optional[str] = None):
        self.error_type = error_type
        self.message = message
        self.file_path = file_path
        self.line_number = line_number
        self.stack_trace = stack_trace or traceback.format_stack()
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'error_type': self.error_type,
            'message': self.message,
            'file_path': self.file_path,
            'line_number': self.line_number,
            'stack_trace': self.stack_trace
        }


class SelfHealingCode:
    """Detects and attempts to fix its own errors"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.error_history = []
        self.fix_attempts = 0
        self.max_fix_attempts = 3
    
    def capture_error(self, error: Exception, context: str = "") -> ErrorInfo:
        """Capture and format error information"""
        error_info = ErrorInfo(
            error_type=type(error).__name__,
            message=str(error),
            file_path=getattr(error, '__file__', None),
            line_number=getattr(error, '__line__', None),
            stack_trace=traceback.format_exc()
        )
        self.error_history.append(error_info)
        return error_info
    
    def analyze_error(self, error_info: ErrorInfo) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Analyze error and suggest fix
        Returns dict with: fix_type, fix_code, confidence, explanation
        """
        fix_suggestions = {
            'RuntimeError': self._fix_runtime_error,
            'ValueError': self._fix_value_error,
            'TypeError': self._fix_type_error,
            'NameError': self._fix_name_error,
            'ImportError': self._fix_import_error,
        }
        
        fixer = fix_suggestions.get(error_info.error_type)
        if fixer:
            return fixer(error_info)
        
        return self._general_error_fix(error_info)
    
    def _fix_runtime_error(self, error: ErrorInfo) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Fix runtime errors"""
        msg = error.message.lower()
        
        if 'division' in msg and 'zero' in msg:
            return {
                'fix_type': 'check_division',
                'fix_code': 'Add zero check before division',
                'confidence': 0.8,
                'explanation': 'Detected potential division by zero'
            }
        
        if 'index' in msg and 'out of range' in msg:
            return {
                'fix_type': 'check_bounds',
                'fix_code': 'Add bounds checking before array access',
                'confidence': 0.7,
                'explanation': 'Detected potential index out of range'
            }
        
        return None
    
    def _fix_value_error(self, error: ErrorInfo) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Fix value errors"""
        msg = error.message.lower()
        
        if 'shape' in msg:
            return {
                'fix_type': 'check_shape',
                'fix_code': 'Verify tensor/shape dimensions match',
                'confidence': 0.75,
                'explanation': 'Detected shape mismatch'
            }
        
        return None
    
    def _fix_type_error(self, error: ErrorInfo) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Fix type errors"""
        return {
            'fix_type': 'add_type_check',
            'fix_code': 'Add type checking or conversion',
            'confidence': 0.6,
            'explanation': 'Detected type mismatch'
        }
    
    def _fix_name_error(self, error: ErrorInfo) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Fix name errors (undefined variables)"""
        return {
            'fix_type': 'define_variable',
            'fix_code': 'Ensure variable is defined before use',
            'confidence': 0.8,
            'explanation': 'Detected undefined variable'
        }
    
    def _fix_import_error(self, error: ErrorInfo) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Fix import errors"""
        return {
            'fix_type': 'install_and_import',
            'fix_code': 'Install missing package and re-import',
            'confidence': 0.7,
            'explanation': 'Detected missing import'
        }
    
    def _general_error_fix(self, error: ErrorInfo) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """General error fix fallback"""
        return {
            'fix_type': 'general_debug',
            'fix_code': 'Review error message and apply appropriate fix',
            'confidence': 0.4,
            'explanation': 'General error - manual review recommended'
        }
    
    def attempt_fix(self, error: Exception, context: str = "") -> bool:
        """
        Attempt to automatically fix an error
        Returns True if fix was applied successfully
        """
        self.fix_attempts += 1
        if self.fix_attempts > self.max_fix_attempts:
            # Don't spam the message
            if self.fix_attempts == self.max_fix_attempts + 1:
                print("Max fix attempts reached, stopping")
            return False
        
        error_info = self.capture_error(error, context)
        suggestion = self.analyze_error(error_info)
        
        if suggestion and suggestion['confidence'] > 0.6:
            print(f"Attempting fix ({suggestion['fix_type']}): {suggestion['explanation']}")
            # In a real implementation, we'd apply the fix
            # For now, just log the suggestion
            print(f"  Suggested fix: {suggestion['fix_code']}")
            return True
        
        return False
    
    def get_last_error(self) -> Optional[ErrorInfo]:
        """Get the most recent error info"""
        if self.error_history:
            return self.error_history[-1]
        return None


class CodeRecoverySystem:
    """Full self-healing system for AI code"""
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.healer = SelfHealingCode()
        self.restart_on_failure = True
        self.max_recursions = 5
    
    def handle_exception(self, error: Exception, context: str = "", 
                        callback=None) -> bool:
        """
        Handle exception with auto-fix attempt
        callback: optional function to call with fix suggestion
        Returns True if error was handled (fixed or acknowledged)
        """
        error_info = self.healer.capture_error(error, context)
        print(f"\nError caught: {error_info.error_type}: {error_info.message}")
        print(f"Context: {context}")
        
        # Attempt auto-fix
        auto_fixed = self.healer.attempt_fix(error, context)
        
        # Call callback if provided
        if callback:
            callback(error_info, auto_fixed)
        
        return auto_fixed
    
    def recover_and_continue(self, code_func, *args, **kwargs):
        """
        Run a function with error recovery
        If error occurs, attempt fix and retry
        """
        try:
            return code_func(*args, **kwargs)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error occurred, attempting recovery...")
            self.handle_exception(e, f"In {code_func.__name__}")
            
            # Could retry here if needed
            if self.restart_on_failure:
                print("Restarting after error...")
            return None

"""

Voice-to-voice conversation AI with emotion awareness, screen perception,

import sys

class EmotionAwareAI:
    """Main AI class integrating all capabilities"""
    
    def __init__(self, 
                 model_name="base",
                 emotion_model=None,
                 use_gpu=False):
        
        self.healer = SelfHealingCode()
        self.running = False
        
        # Initialize core components
        print("Initializing AI components...")
        
        # STT
        self.stt = STTModule(model_size=model_name)
        
        # TTS
        self.tts = TTSModule(rate=150, volume=0.9)
        
        # Emotion perception
        if emotion_model is None:
            self.emotion_extractor = EmotionFeatureExtractor(n_mfcc=40)
        else:
            self.emotion_extractor = emotion_model
        
        self.mood_state = MoodState()
        
        # Screen perception
        self.screen_capture = ScreenCapture()
        self.content_analyzer = ContentAnalyzer()
        self.perception_state = PerceptionState()
        
        # Desktop automation
        self.desktop = DesktopController(speed=1.0)
        self.safe_mode = SafeModeGuard()
        
        # AI model
        self.vocab_size = 50257  # GPT-2 vocab size example
        self.cfg = {
            "n_embd": 768,
            "n_head": 12,
            "n_layer": 12,
            "block_size": 512,
            "dropout": 0.1
        }
        self.model = self._init_ai_model()
        
        print("AI system ready!")
    
    def _init_ai_model(self):
        """Initialize the core AI model"""
        from ai_core.model import EmotionAwareGPT
        model = EmotionAwareGPT(self.vocab_size, self.cfg)
        return model
    
    def process_speech_input(self, audio_path: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Process speech input and extract text + emotion"""
        # Transcribe
        stt_result = self.stt.transcribe(audio_path)
        text = stt_result.get('text', '')
        
        # Detect emotion from audio
        try:
            emotion_logits, mood = self.emotion_extractor.extract_mfcc(
                torch.randn(16000)  # placeholder
            )
            # In real implementation, use actual audio
            emotion_name = 'neutral'
            confidence = 0.5
        except Exception as e:
            emotion_name = 'neutral'
            confidence = 0.5
            print(f"Emotion detection error: {e}")
        
        # Update mood state
        if 'mood' in dir():
            detected_emotion = self.mood_state.update(
                emotion_logits, mood
            )
        else:
            detected_emotion = emotion_name
        
        return {
            'text': text,
            'emotion': detected_emotion,
            'mood': self.mood_state.get_mood_description(),
            'valence': self.mood_state.valence,
            'arousal': self.mood_state.arousal,
            'confidence': confidence
        }
    
    def generate_response(self, user_text: str, mood: str) -> str:
        """Generate AI response based on input and mood"""
        # In a real implementation, this would use the EmotionAwareGPT model
        # For now, use template-based response with mood adaptation
        
        mood_responses = {
            "stressed": "I can hear you're feeling stressed. Take a deep breath. How can I help?",
            "angry": "I sense your frustration. Let me help you with that. What seems to be the problem?",
            "excited": "Wow, you sound excited! Tell me more about what's got you so pumped!",
            "happy": "That's great to hear! What's making you so happy?",
            "calm": "I can sense you're in a calm state. How can I assist you today?",
            "neutral": "I'm listening. What's on your mind?",
            "tired": "You sound tired. Maybe you should rest. Is there anything I can help with before you go?",
            "sad": "I'm sorry you're feeling down. I'm here if you want to talk or need help with anything."
        }
        
        # Get base response for mood
        base_response = mood_responses.get(mood, mood_responses["neutral"])
        
        # Adapt based on user text content
        response = base_response
        
        # Simple keyword-based adaptation
        user_lower = user_text.lower()
        if any(word in user_lower for word in ['help', 'assist', 'support']):
            if mood in ["stressed", "angry", "sad"]:
                response = "I'm here to help you. What specifically do you need assistance with?"
            else:
                response = "I'd be happy to help you with that! What do you need?"
        
        if any(word in user_lower for word in ['thank', 'thanks']):
            response = "You're welcome! Is there anything else I can help you with?"
        
        return response
    
    def perceive_screen(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Capture and analyze screen content"""
        # Capture screen
        image = self.screen_capture.capture()
        
        if image is not None:
            # Analyze content
            analysis = self.content_analyzer.analyze(image)
            
            # Extract text
            extracted_text = self.content_analyzer.extract_text(image)
            
            # Update perception state
            self.perception_state.update(image, analysis)
            
            return {
                'image': image,
                'analysis': analysis,
                'extracted_text': extracted_text,
                'mood': self.mood_state.get_mood_description()
            }
        
        return {'analysis': {}, 'extracted_text': ''}
    
    def execute_safe_action(self, action: str, **kwargs) -> bool:
        """Execute action with safe-mode guardrails"""
        if not self.safe_mode.can_execute(action, **kwargs):
            print("Safe-mode: Action blocked for safety")
            return False
        
        # Execute the action
        try:
            if action == "click":
                self.desktop.click(**kwargs)
            elif action == "type":
                self.desktop.type(**kwargs)
            elif action == "press":
                self.desktop.press(**kwargs)
            elif action == "drag":
                self.desktop.drag_to(**kwargs)
            elif action == "scroll":
                self.desktop.scroll(**kwargs)
            elif action == "hotkey":
                self.desktop.press_hotkey(**kwargs)
            else:
                print(f"Unknown action: {action}")
                return False
            
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Action execution error: {e}")
            # Attempt self-healing
            self.healer.attempt_fix(e, f"action:{action}")
            return False
    
    def converse(self, user_text: str) -> str:
        """Main conversation loop - generate response"""
        # Detect mood from text content (simplified)
        mood = self.mood_state.get_mood_description()
        
        # Generate response
        response = self.generate_response(user_text, mood)
        
        # Update mood based on conversation
        # In a real system, this would use the emotion extractor
        
        return response
    
    def start_voice_loop(self):
        """Start the voice conversation loop"""
        print("Starting voice conversation loop...")
        print("Say 'exit' or 'quit' to stop")
        
        self.running = True
        
        while self.running:
            try:
                # In a real implementation, this would capture from microphone
                # For now, simulate with text input
                try:
                    user_input = input("\nYou: ")
                except EOFError:
                    print("\nAI: End of input. Goodbye!")
                    self.running = False
                    break
                
                if user_input.lower() in ['exit', 'quit', 'goodbye']:
                    print("AI: Goodbye! Have a great day!")
                    self.running = False
                    break
                
                # Generate response
                response = self.converse(user_input)
                print(f"AI: {response}")
                
                try:
                    # Speak response
                    self.tts.speak_immediate(response)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                
            except KeyboardInterrupt:
                print("\nAI: Interrupted. Goodbye!")
                self.running = False
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error: {e}")
                # Attempt self-healing
                try:
                    self.healer.attempt_fix(e, "voice_loop")
                except Exception:
                    pass
                time.sleep(0.5)


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Emotion-Aware AI Assistant')
    parser.add_argument('--model', type=str, default='base', 
                        help='Whisper model size (tiny, base, small, medium, large)')
    parser.add_argument('--voice', action='store_true',
                        help='Enable voice input/output')
    parser.add_argument('--text', action='store_true',
                        help='Text-only mode')
    parser.add_argument('--emotion', action='store_true',
                        help='Enable emotion detection')
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    # Create AI instance
    ai = EmotionAwareAI(
        model_name=args.model,
        use_gpu=False  # Set to True if GPU available
    )
    
    if args.text:
        # Text-only mode
        print("Text mode active. Type 'exit' to quit.")
        while True:
            try:
                user_input = input("\nYou: ")
                if user_input.lower() in ['exit', 'quit']:
                    break
                response = ai.converse(user_input)
                print(f"AI: {response}")
            except EOFError:
                break
            except KeyboardInterrupt:
                break
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Error: {e}")
                break
    else:
        # Voice mode
        try:
            ai.start_voice_loop()
        except EOFError:
            pass
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error: {e}")
            pass


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# FILE: ai_core/model.py
"""
Core AI Model - Transformer-based LLM
"""
import math
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from torch.nn import functional as F


class AttentionHead(nn.Module):
    """Single attention head with causal masking"""
    
    def __init__(self, n_embd, head_size, block_size):
        super().__init__()
        self.key = nn.Linear(n_embd, head_size, bias=False)
        self.query = nn.Linear(n_embd, head_size, bias=False)
        self.value = nn.Linear(n_embd, head_size, bias=False)
        self.register_buffer(
            "mask", torch.tril(torch.ones(block_size, block_size))
        )

    def forward(self, x):
        t = x.size(1)
        k, q, v = self.key(x), self.query(x), self.value(x)
        scores = q @ k.transpose(-2, -1) / math.sqrt(k.size(-1))
        scores = scores.masked_fill(
            self.mask[:t, :t] == 0, float("-inf")
        )
        return F.softmax(scores, dim=-1) @ v


class MultiHeadAttention(nn.Module):
    """Multiple attention heads in parallel"""
    
    def __init__(self, n_embd, n_head, block_size, dropout):
        super().__init__()
        head_size = n_embd // n_head
        self.heads = nn.ModuleList([
            AttentionHead(n_embd, head_size, block_size)
            for _ in range(n_head)
        ])
        self.proj = nn.Linear(n_embd, n_embd)
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)

    def forward(self, x):
        x = torch.cat([head(x) for head in self.heads], dim=-1)
        return self.dropout(self.proj(x))


class TransformerBlock(nn.Module):
    """Transformer block with attention and feedforward"""
    
    def __init__(self, n_embd, n_head, block_size, dropout):
        super().__init__()
        self.ln1 = nn.LayerNorm(n_embd)
        self.ln2 = nn.LayerNorm(n_embd)
        self.attn = MultiHeadAttention(n_embd, n_head, block_size, dropout)
        self.ff = nn.Sequential(
            nn.Linear(n_embd, 4 * n_embd),
            nn.GELU(),
            nn.Linear(4 * n_embd, n_embd),
            nn.Dropout(dropout),
        )

    def forward(self, x):
        x = x + self.attn(self.ln1(x))
        return x + self.ff(self.ln2(x))


class EmotionAwareGPT(nn.Module):
    """GPT model with emotion awareness"""
    
    def __init__(self, vocab_size, cfg):
        super().__init__()
        n_embd = cfg["n_embd"]
        block_size = cfg["block_size"]
        dropout = cfg["dropout"]
        n_layer = cfg["n_layer"]
        
        self.block_size = block_size
        self.token_emb = nn.Embedding(vocab_size, n_embd)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(block_size, n_embd)
        self.emotion_emb = nn.Embedding(8, n_embd)  # 8 emotion categories
        
        self.blocks = nn.Sequential(*[
            TransformerBlock(n_embd, cfg["n_head"], block_size, dropout)
            for _ in range(n_layer)
        ])
        self.ln = nn.LayerNorm(n_embd)
        self.head = nn.Linear(n_embd, vocab_size)

    def forward(self, idx, emotion_id=None):
        t = idx.size(1)
        pos = torch.arange(t, device=idx.device)
        x = self.token_emb(idx) + self.pos_emb(pos)
        
        # Add emotion context if provided
        if emotion_id is not None:
            x = x + self.emotion_emb(emotion_id)
        
        x = self.blocks(x)
        return self.head(self.ln(x))

    @torch.no_grad()
    def generate(self, idx, count, temperature=0.8, emotion_id=None):
        self.eval()
        for _ in range(count):
            logits = self(idx[:, -self.block_size:], emotion_id)[:, -1, :]
            probs = F.softmax(logits / max(temperature, 1e-5), dim=-1)
            idx = torch.cat([idx, torch.multinomial(probs, 1)], dim=1)
        return idx

def start_conversation(agent: AGIAgent):
    """Start an interactive conversation with the AGI"""
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("  Conversation Mode")
    print("  Type 'quit' or 'exit' to return to menu")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    while True:
        try:
            user_input = input("\nYou: ").strip()
            
            if user_input.lower() in ['quit', 'exit', 'bye', 'goodbye']:
                print(f"{agent.name}: Goodbye!")
                break
            
            result = agent.converse(user_input)
            
            print(f"\n{agent.name}: {result['response']}")
            
            # Show additional info if verbose
            if result.get('intent'):
                print(f"  [Intent: {result['intent'].get('type', 'unknown')}, Confidence: {result['intent'].get('confidence', 0):.1f}]")
            if result.get('mood'):
                print(f"  [Mood: {result['mood'].get('emotion', 'unknown')}]")
            
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            print(f"\n{agent.name}: Conversation ended.")
            break
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error: {e}")


def test_reasoning(agent: AGIAgent):
    """Test the reasoning engine"""
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("  Reasoning Engine Test")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    problems = [
        "What is the meaning of life?",
        "How do I solve a quadratic equation?",
        "Explain the concept of artificial intelligence",
        "If all Bloops are Razzies and all Razzies are Lazzies, are all Bloops Lazzies?"
    ]
    
    for problem in problems:
        print(f"\nProblem: {problem}")
        chain = agent.think(problem, max_steps=5)
        
        print(f"  Conclusion: {chain.get_conclusion()}")
        print(f"  Confidence: {chain.confidence:.2f}")
        print(f"  Steps: {len(chain.steps)}")
        
        time.sleep(0.5)


def test_memory(agent: AGIAgent):
    """Test the memory system"""
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("  Memory System Test")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    # Store some information
    print("\nStoring information...")
    agent.remember("The capital of France is Paris", "fact")
    agent.remember("Artificial intelligence is the simulation of human intelligence in machines", "definition")
    agent.learn("AI", "Artificial Intelligence - the field of computer science focused on creating intelligent machines")
    
    # Recall information
    print("\nRecalling information...")
    results = agent.recall("France")
    print(f"  Found {len(results)} results about France")
    for result in results:
        print(f"    - {result}")
    
    results = agent.recall("AI")
    print(f"  Found {len(results)} results about AI")
    for result in results:
        print(f"    - {result}")
    
    # Show memory stats
    state = agent.get_state()
    print(f"\nMemory Stats:")
    print(f"  STM items: {state['memory_stats']['stm']['total_items']}")
    print(f"  LTM concepts: {state['memory_stats']['ltm']['total_concepts']}")


def test_planning(agent: AGIAgent):
    """Test the planning system"""
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("  Planning System Test")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    goals = [
        "Write a research paper on AI",
        "Learn to play the piano",
        "Build a website"
    ]
    
    for goal in goals:
        print(f"\nGoal: {goal}")
        plan = agent.plan(goal)
        
        if 'error' not in plan:
            print(f"  Plan ID: {plan['plan_id']}")
            print(f"  Number of actions: {len(plan['actions'])}")
            for i, action in enumerate(plan['actions'][:3]):  # Show first 3 actions
                print(f"    {i+1}. {action['name']}: {action['description'][:50]}...")
        else:
            print(f"  Error: {plan['error']}")

        ["Python", "JavaScript", "Java", "C++"]

def show_state(agent: AGIAgent):
    """Show the current AGI state"""
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("  AGI State")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    state = agent.get_state()
    
    print("\nMemory System:")
    print(f"  STM: {state['memory_stats']['stm']['total_items']} items")
    print(f"  LTM: {state['memory_stats']['ltm']['total_concepts']} concepts")
    print(f"  Episodic: {state['memory_stats']['episodic']['total_experiences']} experiences")
    
    print("\nPlanning System:")
    print(f"  Total goals: {state['planning_stats']['total_goals']}")
    print(f"  Active goals: {state['planning_stats']['active_goals']}")
    
    print("\nWorld Model:")
    print(f"  Entities: {state['world_model_stats']['entities']}")
    print(f"  Relationships: {state['world_model_stats']['relationships']}")
    
    print("\nMeta Learning:")
    print(f"  Learning experiences: {state['meta_learning_stats']['total_experiences']}")
    print(f"  Success rate: {state['meta_learning_stats']['success_rate']:.1%}")
    
    if state.get('mood'):
        print("\nMood:")
        print(f"  Emotion: {state['mood']['emotion']}")
        print(f"  Valence: {state['mood']['valence']:.2f}")
        print(f"  Arousal: {state['mood']['arousal']:.2f}")


def run_demo(agent: AGIAgent):
    """Run a comprehensive demo of AGI capabilities"""
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("  AGI Core Demo")
    print("=" * 70)
    
    print("\n1. Conversation")
    print("-" * 70)
    result = agent.converse("Hello! What is artificial intelligence?")
    print(f"Q: Hello! What is artificial intelligence?")
    print(f"A: {result['response']}")
    
    print("\n2. Reasoning")
    print("-" * 70)
    chain = agent.think("If all humans are mortal and Socrates is human, is Socrates mortal?")
    print(f"Q: If all humans are mortal and Socrates is human, is Socrates mortal?")
    print(f"A: {chain.get_conclusion()}")
    print(f"  Confidence: {chain.confidence:.2f}")
    
    print("\n3. Memory")
    print("-" * 70)
    agent.learn("AGI", "Artificial General Intelligence - AI with human-like cognitive abilities")
    results = agent.recall("AGI")
    print(f"Learned: AGI = Artificial General Intelligence")
    print(f"Recall: {results[0] if results else 'No results'}")
    
    print("\n4. Planning")
    print("-" * 70)
    plan = agent.plan("Write a book")
    print(f"Goal: Write a book")
    print(f"Plan: {len(plan.get('actions', []))} steps created")
    
    print("\n5. Decision Making")
    print("-" * 70)
    decision = agent.decide("Which is better, tea or coffee?", ["Tea", "Coffee"])
    print(f"Q: Which is better, tea or coffee?")
    print(f"A: {decision['chosen_option']}")
    
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("  Demo Complete!")
    print("=" * 70)


def main():
    """Main entry point"""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=f"{AGI_NAME} - Artificial General Intelligence Core"
    )
    parser.add_argument('--version', action='store_true', help='Show version and exit')
    parser.add_argument('--demo', action='store_true', help='Run demo and exit')
    parser.add_argument('--conversation', action='store_true', help='Start conversation mode')
    parser.add_argument('--no-purple', action='store_true', help='Disable Purple AI integration')
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    # Show version
    if args.version:
        print_header()
        print(f"Version: {__version__}")
        print(f"AGI Name: {AGI_NAME}")
        sys.exit(0)
    
    # Initialize AGI Agent
    print_header()
    print("Initializing AGI Core...")
    
    agent = AGIAgent(use_purple=not args.no_purple)
    agent.initialize()
    
    print(f"{agent.name} ready!")
    
    # Run demo if requested
    if args.demo:
        run_demo(agent)
        sys.exit(0)
    
    # Start conversation if requested
    if args.conversation:
        start_conversation(agent)
        sys.exit(0)
    
    # Interactive menu
    while True:
        try:
            print_menu()
            choice = input("Enter your choice (1-9): ").strip()
            
            if choice == '1':
                start_conversation(agent)
            elif choice == '2':
                test_reasoning(agent)
            elif choice == '3':
                test_memory(agent)
            elif choice == '4':
                test_planning(agent)
            elif choice == '5':
                test_decision_making(agent)
            elif choice == '6':
                show_state(agent)
            elif choice == '7':
                concept = input("Enter concept name: ").strip()
                definition = input("Enter definition: ").strip()
                agent.learn(concept, definition)
                print(f"Learned: {concept}")
            elif choice == '8':
                run_demo(agent)
            elif choice == '9':
                print(f"\n{agent.name}: Shutting down...")
                agent.shutdown()
                print("Goodbye!")
                break
            else:
                print("Invalid choice. Please enter a number between 1 and 9.")
            
        except KeyboardInterrupt:
            print(f"\n{agent.name}: Shutting down...")
            agent.shutdown()
            print("Goodbye!")
            break
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error: {e}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
