# FILE: agi_core/reasoning/reasoning_engine.py
"""
Reasoning Engine - Core Chain-of-Thought and Multi-Step Reasoning

This module provides the foundation for AGI-level reasoning capabilities,
including step-by-step inference, hypothesis generation, and verification.
"""

import json
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Callable
from enum import Enum
from collections import defaultdict
import hashlib


class ReasoningMode(Enum):
    """Different reasoning strategies"""
    CHAIN_OF_THOUGHT = "chain_of_thought"
    STEP_BY_STEP = "step_by_step"
    TREE_OF_THOUGHTS = "tree_of_thoughts"
    HYPOTHESIS_DRIVEN = "hypothesis_driven"
    ABDUCTIVE = "abductive"
    DEDUCTIVE = "deductive"
    INDUCTIVE = "inductive"
    ANALOGICAL = "analogical"


class ReasoningState(Enum):
    """Current state of reasoning process"""
    INITIALIZING = "initializing"
    GENERATING_HYPOTHESES = "generating_hypotheses"
    EVALUATING = "evaluating"
    VERIFYING = "verifying"
    REFLECTING = "reflecting"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    FAILED = "failed"


@dataclass
class InferenceStep:
    """A single step in a reasoning chain"""
    step_id: str
    content: str
    reasoning_mode: ReasoningMode
    confidence: float = 0.0
    evidence: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    sources: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    dependencies: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'step_id': self.step_id,
            'content': self.content,
            'reasoning_mode': self.reasoning_mode.value,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'evidence': self.evidence,
            'sources': self.sources,
            'timestamp': self.timestamp,
            'dependencies': self.dependencies
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'InferenceStep':
        return cls(
            step_id=data.get('step_id', ''),
            content=data.get('content', ''),
            reasoning_mode=ReasoningMode(data.get('reasoning_mode', 'chain_of_thought')),
            confidence=data.get('confidence', 0.0),
            evidence=data.get('evidence', []),
            sources=data.get('sources', []),
            timestamp=data.get('timestamp', time.time()),
            dependencies=data.get('dependencies', [])
        )


@dataclass
class ThoughtChain:
    """A chain of connected reasoning steps"""
    chain_id: str
    goal: str
    steps: List[InferenceStep] = field(default_factory=list)
    current_step_index: int = 0
    state: ReasoningState = ReasoningState.INITIALIZING
    confidence: float = 0.0
    is_valid: bool = True
    contradictions: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    def add_step(self, content: str, reasoning_mode: ReasoningMode, 
                 confidence: float = 0.8, evidence: List[str] = None,
                 sources: List[str] = None) -> InferenceStep:
        """Add a new step to the chain"""
        step_id = f"{self.chain_id}_step_{len(self.steps)}"
        step = InferenceStep(
            step_id=step_id,
            content=content,
            reasoning_mode=reasoning_mode,
            confidence=confidence,
            evidence=evidence or [],
            sources=sources or []
        )
        self.steps.append(step)
        self.current_step_index = len(self.steps) - 1
        return step
    
    def get_current_step(self) -> Optional[InferenceStep]:
        """Get the current step in the chain"""
        if self.current_step_index < len(self.steps):
            return self.steps[self.current_step_index]
        return None
    
    def get_conclusion(self) -> Optional[str]:
        """Get the final conclusion from the chain"""
        if self.steps:
            return self.steps[-1].content
        return None
    
    def validate_chain(self) -> bool:
        """Validate the logical consistency of the chain"""
        # Check for contradictions
        self.contradictions = []
        self.is_valid = True
        
        # Simple validation: check confidence thresholds
        for step in self.steps:
            if step.confidence < 0.3:
                self.contradictions.append(f"Low confidence step: {step.step_id}")
                self.is_valid = False
        
        # Check for circular dependencies
        for step in self.steps:
            if step.step_id in step.dependencies:
                self.contradictions.append(f"Circular dependency in: {step.step_id}")
                self.is_valid = False
        
        return self.is_valid
    
    def calculate_chain_confidence(self) -> float:
        """Calculate overall confidence of the chain"""
        if not self.steps:
            return 0.0
        
        # Weighted average with recency bias
        total_confidence = 0.0
        total_weight = 0.0
        
        for i, step in enumerate(self.steps):
            weight = (i + 1) / len(self.steps)  # Later steps have more weight
            total_confidence += step.confidence * weight
            total_weight += weight
        
        self.confidence = total_confidence / total_weight if total_weight > 0 else 0.0
        return self.confidence
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'chain_id': self.chain_id,
            'goal': self.goal,
            'steps': [s.to_dict() for s in self.steps],
            'current_step_index': self.current_step_index,
            'state': self.state.value,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'is_valid': self.is_valid,
            'contradictions': self.contradictions
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'ThoughtChain':
        chain = cls(
            chain_id=data.get('chain_id', ''),
            goal=data.get('goal', ''),
            steps=[InferenceStep.from_dict(s) for s in data.get('steps', [])],
            current_step_index=data.get('current_step_index', 0),
            state=ReasoningState(data.get('state', 'initializing')),
            confidence=data.get('confidence', 0.0),
            is_valid=data.get('is_valid', True),
            contradictions=data.get('contradictions', [])
        )
        return chain


class ReasoningEngine:
    """
    Core reasoning engine supporting multiple reasoning modes.
    
    This engine can:
    - Generate chain-of-thought reasoning
    - Evaluate hypotheses
    - Perform step-by-step inference
    - Validate reasoning chains
    - Integrate with external knowledge sources
    """
    
    def __init__(self, max_chain_length: int = 20, 
                 default_reasoning_mode: ReasoningMode = ReasoningMode.CHAIN_OF_THOUGHT,
                 knowledge_sources: Optional[List[Callable]] = None):
        self.max_chain_length = max_chain_length
        self.default_reasoning_mode = default_reasoning_mode
        self.knowledge_sources = knowledge_sources or []
        self.chains: Dict[str, ThoughtChain] = {}
        self.current_chain: Optional[ThoughtChain] = None
        
    def create_chain(self, goal: str, chain_id: Optional[str] = None) -> ThoughtChain:
        """Create a new reasoning chain"""
        chain_id = chain_id or f"chain_{int(time.time())}_{hashlib.md5(goal.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]}"
        chain = ThoughtChain(chain_id=chain_id, goal=goal)
        self.chains[chain_id] = chain
        self.current_chain = chain
        return chain
    
    def get_chain(self, chain_id: str) -> Optional[ThoughtChain]:
        """Get a reasoning chain by ID"""
        return self.chains.get(chain_id)
    
    def set_current_chain(self, chain_id: str) -> bool:
        """Set the current active chain"""
        if chain_id in self.chains:
            self.current_chain = self.chains[chain_id]
            return True
        return False
    
    def reason(self, goal: str, reasoning_mode: Optional[ReasoningMode] = None,
               max_steps: int = 10) -> ThoughtChain:
        """
        Perform reasoning on a goal using the specified mode.
        
        This is a simplified version that demonstrates the structure.
        In a full implementation, this would integrate with the LLM.
        """
        mode = reasoning_mode or self.default_reasoning_mode
        chain = self.create_chain(goal)
        chain.state = ReasoningState.GENERATING_HYPOTHESES
        
        # Generate initial hypotheses
        hypotheses = self._generate_hypotheses(goal)
        
        for i, hypothesis in enumerate(hypotheses[:max_steps]):
            step = chain.add_step(
                content=hypothesis,
                reasoning_mode=mode,
                confidence=0.7,
                evidence=[f"Hypothesis {i+1} for {goal}"]
            )
        
        chain.state = ReasoningState.EVALUATING
        
        # Evaluate and refine
        for i in range(min(max_steps, len(hypotheses))):
            refined = self._refine_hypothesis(hypotheses[i], i, chain)
            chain.add_step(
                content=refined,
                reasoning_mode=mode,
                confidence=0.85,
                evidence=[f"Refined from hypothesis {i+1}"]
            )
        
        chain.state = ReasoningState.VERIFYING
        chain.validate_chain()
        chain.calculate_chain_confidence()
        chain.state = ReasoningState.COMPLETED
        
        return chain
    
    def _generate_hypotheses(self, goal: str) -> List[str]:
        """Generate initial hypotheses for a goal"""
        # This would integrate with the LLM in a full implementation
        # For now, return structured hypotheses
        hypotheses = []
        
        # Basic hypothesis generation
        if "?" in goal:
            # It's a question - generate possible answers
            hypotheses.append(f"Possible answer 1: Direct response to {goal}")
            hypotheses.append(f"Possible answer 2: Alternative interpretation of {goal}")
            hypotheses.append(f"Possible answer 3: Context-dependent response to {goal}")
        else:
            # It's a statement - generate implications
            hypotheses.append(f"Implication 1: If {goal}, then consequence A")
            hypotheses.append(f"Implication 2: {goal} suggests underlying cause B")
            hypotheses.append(f"Implication 3: {goal} relates to concept C")
        
        return hypotheses
    
    def _refine_hypothesis(self, hypothesis: str, index: int, chain: ThoughtChain) -> str:
        """Refine a hypothesis based on the current chain state"""
        # In a full implementation, this would use the LLM to refine
        return f"Refined: {hypothesis} [Step {index + 1}]"
    
    def chain_of_thought(self, prompt: str, max_steps: int = 8) -> ThoughtChain:
        """Perform chain-of-thought reasoning"""
        return self.reason(prompt, ReasoningMode.CHAIN_OF_THOUGHT, max_steps)
    
    def tree_of_thoughts(self, prompt: str, branches: int = 3, depth: int = 3) -> ThoughtChain:
        """Perform tree-of-thoughts reasoning"""
        chain = self.create_chain(prompt)
        chain.state = ReasoningState.GENERATING_HYPOTHESES
        
        # Generate multiple branches
        for branch_idx in range(branches):
            branch_goal = f"{prompt} [Branch {branch_idx + 1}]"
            branch_chain = self.reason(branch_goal, ReasoningMode.CHAIN_OF_THOUGHT, depth)
            
            # Merge branch into main chain
            for step in branch_chain.steps:
                chain.add_step(
                    content=f"[Branch {branch_idx + 1}] {step.content}",
                    reasoning_mode=ReasoningMode.TREE_OF_THOUGHTS,
                    confidence=step.confidence * 0.9  # Slightly lower confidence for branches
                )
        
        chain.state = ReasoningState.COMPLETED
        chain.validate_chain()
        return chain
    
    def verify_reasoning(self, chain: ThoughtChain) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Verify the validity of a reasoning chain"""
        result = {
            'is_valid': chain.validate_chain(),
            'confidence': chain.calculate_chain_confidence(),
            'contradictions': chain.contradictions,
            'step_count': len(chain.steps),
            'suggestions': []
        }
        
        # Add suggestions for improvement
        if len(chain.steps) < 3:
            result['suggestions'].append("Consider adding more reasoning steps")
        
        if result['confidence'] < 0.7:
            result['suggestions'].append("Some steps have low confidence - consider gathering more evidence")
        
        return result
    
    def get_best_chain(self, goal_pattern: str = None) -> Optional[ThoughtChain]:
        """Get the best reasoning chain matching a pattern"""
        best_chain = None
        best_confidence = 0.0
        
        for chain in self.chains.values():
            if goal_pattern and goal_pattern not in chain.goal:
                continue
            if chain.confidence > best_confidence:
                best_chain = chain
                best_confidence = chain.confidence
        
        return best_chain
    
    def clear_chains(self) -> None:
        """Clear all reasoning chains"""
        self.chains.clear()
        self.current_chain = None


# FILE: agi_core/reasoning/reflection_engine.py
"""
Reflection Engine - Meta-Cognition and Self-Improvement

This module provides capabilities for the AGI to reflect on its own
thinking processes, identify errors, and improve future reasoning.
"""

import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Callable
from enum import Enum
import json


class ReflectionType(Enum):
    """Types of reflection the system can perform"""
    SELF_CRITIQUE = "self_critique"
    META_COGNITION = "meta_cognition"
    PERFORMANCE_ANALYSIS = "performance_analysis"
    ERROR_ANALYSIS = "error_analysis"
    LEARNING_REFLECTION = "learning_reflection"
    STRATEGY_EVALUATION = "strategy_evaluation"


class ReflectionPhase(Enum):
    """Phases of the reflection process"""
    IDENTIFICATION = "identification"
    ANALYSIS = "analysis"
    EVALUATION = "evaluation"
    IMPROVEMENT = "improvement"
    IMPLEMENTATION = "implementation"


@dataclass
class ReflectionPoint:
    """A specific point of reflection on past reasoning"""
    reflection_id: str
    reflection_type: ReflectionType
    target: str  # What we're reflecting on (chain_id, decision_id, etc.)
    observation: str
    analysis: str
    evaluation: str
    improvement: str
    confidence: float = 0.0
    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    related_reflections: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'reflection_id': self.reflection_id,
            'reflection_type': self.reflection_type.value,
            'target': self.target,
            'observation': self.observation,
            'analysis': self.analysis,
            'evaluation': self.evaluation,
            'improvement': self.improvement,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'timestamp': self.timestamp,
            'related_reflections': self.related_reflections
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'ReflectionPoint':
        return cls(
            reflection_id=data.get('reflection_id', ''),
            reflection_type=ReflectionType(data.get('reflection_type', 'self_critique')),
            target=data.get('target', ''),
            observation=data.get('observation', ''),
            analysis=data.get('analysis', ''),
            evaluation=data.get('evaluation', ''),
            improvement=data.get('improvement', ''),
            confidence=data.get('confidence', 0.0),
            timestamp=data.get('timestamp', time.time()),
            related_reflections=data.get('related_reflections', [])
        )


@dataclass
class SelfCritique:
    """Structured self-critique of reasoning or action"""
    critique_id: str
    subject: str  # What is being critiqued
    strengths: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    weaknesses: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    errors: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    suggestions: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    overall_score: float = 0.0  # 0-100
    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    
    def add_strength(self, strength: str) -> None:
        self.strengths.append(strength)
    
    def add_weakness(self, weakness: str) -> None:
        self.weaknesses.append(weakness)
    
    def add_error(self, error: str) -> None:
        self.errors.append(error)
    
    def add_suggestion(self, suggestion: str) -> None:
        self.suggestions.append(suggestion)
    
    def calculate_score(self) -> float:
        """Calculate overall score based on strengths, weaknesses, and errors"""
        score = 50.0  # Start at middle
        
        # Strengths add to score
        score += len(self.strengths) * 5.0
        
        # Weaknesses subtract from score
        score -= len(self.weaknesses) * 3.0
        
        # Errors significantly subtract from score
        score -= len(self.errors) * 10.0
        
        # Clamp to 0-100
        self.overall_score = max(0.0, min(100.0, score))
        return self.overall_score
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'critique_id': self.critique_id,
            'subject': self.subject,
            'strengths': self.strengths,
            'weaknesses': self.weaknesses,
            'errors': self.errors,
            'suggestions': self.suggestions,
            'overall_score': self.overall_score,
            'timestamp': self.timestamp
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'SelfCritique':
        critique = cls(
            critique_id=data.get('critique_id', ''),
            subject=data.get('subject', ''),
            strengths=data.get('strengths', []),
            weaknesses=data.get('weaknesses', []),
            errors=data.get('errors', []),
            suggestions=data.get('suggestions', []),
            overall_score=data.get('overall_score', 0.0),
            timestamp=data.get('timestamp', time.time())
        )
        return critique


@dataclass
class MetaCognition:
    """Meta-cognitive state and capabilities"""
    thinking_about_thinking: bool = False
    current_focus: str = ""
    cognitive_load: float = 0.0  # 0-1
    uncertainty: float = 0.0  # 0-1
    confidence: float = 1.0  # 0-1
    
    def update_state(self, focus: str, load: float, uncertainty: float) -> None:
        """Update the meta-cognitive state"""
        self.current_focus = focus
        self.cognitive_load = max(0.0, min(1.0, load))
        self.uncertainty = max(0.0, min(1.0, uncertainty))
        self.confidence = max(0.0, min(1.0, 1.0 - uncertainty * 0.5))
    
    def should_reflect(self, threshold: float = 0.7) -> bool:
        """Determine if reflection is needed based on uncertainty"""
        return self.uncertainty > threshold or self.cognitive_load > threshold
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'thinking_about_thinking': self.thinking_about_thinking,
            'current_focus': self.current_focus,
            'cognitive_load': self.cognitive_load,
            'uncertainty': self.uncertainty,
            'confidence': self.confidence
        }


class ReflectionEngine:
    """
    Engine for performing meta-cognitive reflection on reasoning processes.
    
    Capabilities:
    - Self-critique of reasoning chains
    - Meta-cognitive monitoring
    - Performance analysis
    - Error pattern detection
    - Learning from mistakes
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.reflections: Dict[str, ReflectionPoint] = {}
        self.critiques: Dict[str, SelfCritique] = {}
        self.meta_cognition = MetaCognition()
        self.reflection_history: List[str] = []
        
    def create_reflection(self, reflection_type: ReflectionType,
                          target: str, observation: str) -> ReflectionPoint:
        """Create a new reflection point"""
        reflection_id = f"reflect_{int(time.time())}_{len(self.reflections)}"
        reflection = ReflectionPoint(
            reflection_id=reflection_id,
            reflection_type=reflection_type,
            target=target,
            observation=observation
        )
        self.reflections[reflection_id] = reflection
        self.reflection_history.append(reflection_id)
        return reflection
    
    def perform_self_critique(self, subject: str, 
                             strengths: List[str] = None,
                             weaknesses: List[str] = None,
                             errors: List[str] = None) -> SelfCritique:
        """Perform a structured self-critique"""
        critique_id = f"critique_{int(time.time())}_{len(self.critiques)}"
        critique = SelfCritique(
            critique_id=critique_id,
            subject=subject,
            strengths=strengths or [],
            weaknesses=weaknesses or [],
            errors=errors or []
        )
        critique.calculate_score()
        self.critiques[critique_id] = critique
        return critique
    
    def critique_reasoning_chain(self, chain, 
                                  expected_answer: Optional[str] = None) -> SelfCritique:
        """Critique a reasoning chain"""
        subject = f"Chain: {chain.goal}"
        critique = self.perform_self_critique(subject)
        
        # Analyze the chain
        if len(chain.steps) < 3:
            critique.add_weakness("Chain is too short - may lack depth")
        else:
            critique.add_strength(f"Chain has {len(chain.steps)} steps - good depth")
        
        if chain.confidence < 0.7:
            critique.add_weakness(f"Low overall confidence: {chain.confidence:.2f}")
        else:
            critique.add_strength(f"High confidence: {chain.confidence:.2f}")
        
        if chain.contradictions:
            for contradiction in chain.contradictions:
                critique.add_error(f"Contradiction detected: {contradiction}")
        
        # Check if conclusion matches expected
        if expected_answer and chain.get_conclusion():
            if expected_answer.lower() in chain.get_conclusion().lower():
                critique.add_strength("Conclusion matches expected answer")
            else:
                critique.add_weakness("Conclusion doesn't match expected answer")
        
        # Add suggestions
        if chain.confidence < 0.8:
            critique.add_suggestion("Consider gathering more evidence or information")
        
        if len(chain.steps) > 10:
            critique.add_suggestion("Consider breaking into sub-chains for clarity")
        
        critique.calculate_score()
        return critique
    
    def reflect_on_decision(self, decision, outcome: str, 
                           expected_outcome: Optional[str] = None) -> ReflectionPoint:
        """Reflect on a decision and its outcome"""
        reflection = self.create_reflection(
            reflection_type=ReflectionType.PERFORMANCE_ANALYSIS,
            target=decision,
            observation=f"Decision: {decision}, Outcome: {outcome}"
        )
        
        # Analyze the decision
        if expected_outcome:
            if outcome == expected_outcome:
                reflection.analysis = "Decision achieved expected outcome"
                reflection.evaluation = "Good decision"
            else:
                reflection.analysis = f"Decision did not achieve expected outcome. Expected: {expected_outcome}, Got: {outcome}"
                reflection.evaluation = "Needs improvement"
                reflection.improvement = "Consider alternative approaches or gather more information before deciding"
        else:
            reflection.analysis = f"Decision made without clear expectations. Outcome: {outcome}"
            reflection.evaluation = "Neutral - no baseline for comparison"
            reflection.improvement = "Establish expected outcomes before making decisions"
        
        reflection.confidence = 0.8
        return reflection
    
    def detect_error_patterns(self, recent_errors: List[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Detect patterns in recent errors"""
        error_counts = defaultdict(int)
        for error in recent_errors:
            # Simple pattern detection
            error_lower = error.lower()
            if "typeerror" in error_lower:
                error_counts["type_errors"] += 1
            elif "valueerror" in error_lower:
                error_counts["value_errors"] += 1
            elif "index" in error_lower:
                error_counts["index_errors"] += 1
            elif "keyerror" in error_lower:
                error_counts["key_errors"] += 1
            elif "import" in error_lower:
                error_counts["import_errors"] += 1
            else:
                error_counts["other_errors"] += 1
        
        return {
            'patterns': dict(error_counts),
            'total_errors': len(recent_errors),
            'most_common': max(error_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])[0] if error_counts else None
        }
    
    def get_reflection_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get statistics about reflections performed"""
        return {
            'total_reflections': len(self.reflections),
            'total_critiques': len(self.critiques),
            'recent_reflections': self.reflection_history[-10:] if self.reflection_history else [],
            'meta_cognition_state': self.meta_cognition.to_dict()
        }
    
    def update_meta_cognition(self, focus: str, load: float, uncertainty: float) -> None:
        """Update the meta-cognitive state"""
        self.meta_cognition.update_state(focus, load, uncertainty)
    
    def should_trigger_reflection(self, uncertainty: float = 0.0, 
                                  cognitive_load: float = 0.0) -> bool:
        """Determine if reflection should be triggered"""
        self.update_meta_cognition("", cognitive_load, uncertainty)
        return self.meta_cognition.should_reflect()
    
    def save_state(self, path: str = "agi_reflections.json") -> None:
        """Save reflection state to file"""
        state = {
            'reflections': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.reflections.items()},
            'critiques': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.critiques.items()},
            'meta_cognition': self.meta_cognition.to_dict(),
            'reflection_history': self.reflection_history
        }
        with open(path, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(state, f, indent=2)
    
    def load_state(self, path: str = "agi_reflections.json") -> bool:
        """Load reflection state from file"""
        try:
            with open(path, 'r') as f:
                state = json.load(f)
            
            self.reflections = {k: ReflectionPoint.from_dict(v) for k, v in state.get('reflections', {}).items()}
            self.critiques = {k: SelfCritique.from_dict(v) for k, v in state.get('critiques', {}).items()}
            self.reflection_history = state.get('reflection_history', [])
            
            meta_data = state.get('meta_cognition', {})
            self.meta_cognition = MetaCognition(
                thinking_about_thinking=meta_data.get('thinking_about_thinking', False),
                current_focus=meta_data.get('current_focus', ""),
                cognitive_load=meta_data.get('cognitive_load', 0.0),
                uncertainty=meta_data.get('uncertainty', 0.0),
                confidence=meta_data.get('confidence', 1.0)
            )
            return True
        except FileNotFoundError:
            return False


# FILE: agi_core/reasoning/logical_reasoning.py
"""
Logical Reasoning - Deductive and Inductive Logic
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Set
from enum import Enum


class LogicalOperator(Enum):
    AND = "AND"
    OR = "OR"
    NOT = "NOT"
    IMPLIES = "IMPLIES"
    IFF = "IFF"


@dataclass
class Proposition:
    symbol: str
    value: Optional[bool] = None
    description: str = ""


class PropositionalLogic:
    def __init__(self):
        self.propositions: Dict[str, Proposition] = {}
    
    def add_proposition(self, symbol: str, description: str = "", value: Optional[bool] = None):
        prop = Proposition(symbol=symbol, description=description, value=value)
        self.propositions[symbol] = prop
        return prop
    
    def evaluate(self, expr: Any, context: Optional[Dict[str, bool]] = None) -> bool:
        context = context or {}
        context.update({p: v.value for p, v in self.propositions.items() if v.value is not None})
        return self._eval(expr, context)
    
    def _eval(self, expr: Any, context: Dict[str, bool]) -> bool:
        if isinstance(expr, str):
            return context.get(expr, False)
        elif isinstance(expr, list) and len(expr) > 0:
            op = expr[0]
            if op == LogicalOperator.NOT:
                return not self._eval(expr[1], context)
            elif op == LogicalOperator.AND:
                return all(self._eval(a, context) for a in expr[1:])
            elif op == LogicalOperator.OR:
                return any(self._eval(a, context) for a in expr[1:])
            elif op == LogicalOperator.IMPLIES:
                return (not self._eval(expr[1], context)) or self._eval(expr[2], context)
            elif op == LogicalOperator.IFF:
                return self._eval(expr[1], context) == self._eval(expr[2], context)
        return False


class FirstOrderLogic:
    pass


class LogicalDeduction:
    def __init__(self):
        self.prop_logic = PropositionalLogic()
        self.first_order = FirstOrderLogic()

# FILE: agi_core/world_model/world_model.py
"""
World Model - Integrated Model of the World

This module provides a comprehensive world model that integrates:
- Knowledge graph (semantic relationships)
- Spatial model (locations and spatial relationships)
- Temporal model (events and timelines)
- Causal model (cause and effect)
"""

import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Set
import json
import os

from .knowledge_graph import KnowledgeGraph, Entity, Relationship


@dataclass
class WorldState:
    """The current state of the world as understood by the AGI"""
    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    known_entities: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    known_relationships: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    current_location: Optional[str] = None
    current_time: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    active_entities: Set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    recent_changes: List[Dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'timestamp': self.timestamp,
            'known_entities': list(self.known_entities),
            'known_relationships': list(self.known_relationships),
            'current_location': self.current_location,
            'current_time': self.current_time,
            'active_entities': list(self.active_entities),
            'recent_changes': self.recent_changes
        }


class WorldModel:
    """
    Comprehensive world model for the AGI.
    
    This model integrates multiple aspects of world knowledge:
    - Semantic knowledge (entities, types, relationships)
    - Spatial knowledge (locations, distances, paths)
    - Temporal knowledge (events, sequences, timelines)
    - Causal knowledge (cause-effect relationships)
    
    The world model provides a unified interface for:
    - Querying world state
    - Updating world knowledge
    - Making inferences about the world
    - Simulating world changes
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        # Initialize sub-models
        self.knowledge_graph = KnowledgeGraph()
        
        # Additional models would be initialized here:
        # self.spatial_model = SpatialModel()
        # self.temporal_model = TemporalModel()
        # self.causal_model = CausalModel()
        
        # World state
        self.world_state = WorldState()
        self.change_history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        
    def add_entity(self, name: str, entity_type: str = "thing",
                   description: str = "", **properties) -> Entity:
        """Add an entity to the world model"""
        entity = self.knowledge_graph.add_entity(name, entity_type, description)
        
        # Add properties
        for prop_name, prop_value in properties.items():
            entity.add_property(prop_name, prop_value)
        
        # Update world state
        self.world_state.known_entities.add(entity.entity_id)
        self._record_change("add_entity", {"entity_id": entity.entity_id, "name": name})
        
        return entity
    
    def add_relationship(self, relationship_type: str, source: str, target: str,
                        **properties) -> Relationship:
        """Add a relationship between entities"""
        rel = self.knowledge_graph.add_relationship(
            relationship_type, source, target
        )
        
        # Add properties to relationship
        for prop_name, prop_value in properties.items():
            rel.add_property(prop_name, prop_value)
        
        # Update world state
        self.world_state.known_relationships.add(rel.relationship_id)
        self._record_change("add_relationship", {
            "relationship_id": rel.relationship_id,
            "type": relationship_type,
            "source": source,
            "target": target
        })
        
        return rel
    
    def get_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> Optional[Entity]:
        """Get an entity from the world model"""
        return self.knowledge_graph.get_entity(entity_id)
    
    def get_entities_by_type(self, entity_type: str) -> List[Entity]:
        """Get all entities of a specific type"""
        return self.knowledge_graph.get_entities_by_type(entity_type)
    
    def query(self, query: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Entity]:
        """Query the world model"""
        return self.knowledge_graph.query(query)
    
    def infer(self, entity_id: str, inference_type: str = "type_hierarchy") -> Any:
        """Make inferences about entities in the world"""
        if inference_type == "type_hierarchy":
            return self.knowledge_graph.infer_type_hierarchy(entity_id)
        elif inference_type == "related_entities":
            return self.knowledge_graph.get_related_entities(entity_id)
        else:
            return None
    
    def update_entity(self, entity_id: str, **updates) -> bool:
        """Update an entity in the world model"""
        entity = self.get_entity(entity_id)
        if not entity:
            return False
        
        old_values = {}
        for key, value in updates.items():
            if hasattr(entity, key):
                old_values[key] = getattr(entity, key)
                setattr(entity, key, value)
            elif key in entity.properties:
                old_values[key] = entity.properties[key].value
                entity.properties[key].value = value
        
        # Record the change
        self._record_change("update_entity", {
            "entity_id": entity_id,
            "updates": updates,
            "old_values": old_values
        })
        
        return True
    
    def remove_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> bool:
        """Remove an entity from the world model"""
        entity = self.get_entity(entity_id)
        if not entity:
            return False
        
        # Record the removal
        self._record_change("remove_entity", {
            "entity_id": entity_id,
            "name": entity.name
        })
        
        # Remove from knowledge graph
        del self.knowledge_graph.entities[entity_id]
        
        # Update world state
        self.world_state.known_entities.discard(entity_id)
        
        return True
    
    def _record_change(self, change_type: str, details: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Record a change to the world model"""
        change = {
            "timestamp": time.time(),
            "type": change_type,
            "details": details
        }
        self.change_history.append(change)
        self.world_state.recent_changes.append(change)
        
        # Keep only recent changes
        if len(self.world_state.recent_changes) > 100:
            self.world_state.recent_changes = self.world_state.recent_changes[-100:]
        
        if len(self.change_history) > 1000:
            self.change_history = self.change_history[-1000:]
    
    def get_world_state(self) -> WorldState:
        """Get the current world state"""
        return self.world_state
    
    def update_world_state(self, **updates) -> None:
        """Update the world state"""
        for key, value in updates.items():
            if hasattr(self.world_state, key):
                setattr(self.world_state, key, value)
    
    def simulate_change(self, change_description: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Simulate a change to the world and predict its effects.
        
        This would use the causal model to predict what would happen
        if the described change occurred.
        """
        # In a full implementation, this would:
        # 1. Parse the change description
        # 2. Identify affected entities and relationships
        # 3. Use causal model to predict effects
        # 4. Return predicted new world state
        
        return {
            'change': change_description,
            'predicted_effects': [],
            'confidence': 0.5,
            'message': 'Simulation not yet implemented'
        }
    
    def check_consistency(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Check the consistency of the world model"""
        issues = []
        
        # Check for orphaned relationships
        for rel_id, rel in self.knowledge_graph.relationships.items():
            if rel.source_entity_id not in self.knowledge_graph.entities:
                issues.append(f"Orphaned relationship {rel_id}: source entity missing")
            if rel.target_entity_id not in self.knowledge_graph.entities:
                issues.append(f"Orphaned relationship {rel_id}: target entity missing")
        
        # Check for duplicate entity names
        name_count = defaultdict(int)
        for entity in self.knowledge_graph.entities.values():
            name_count[entity.name.lower()] += 1
        
        for name, count in name_count.items():
            if count > 1:
                issues.append(f"Duplicate entity name: {name} ({count} entities)")
        
        return {
            'is_consistent': len(issues) == 0,
            'issues': issues,
            'entity_count': len(self.knowledge_graph.entities),
            'relationship_count': len(self.knowledge_graph.relationships)
        }
    
    def save(self, path: str = "world_model.json") -> None:
        """Save the world model to file"""
        state = {
            'world_state': self.world_state.to_dict(),
            'knowledge_graph': {
                'entities': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.knowledge_graph.entities.items()},
                'relationships': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.knowledge_graph.relationships.items()}
            },
            'change_history': self.change_history
        }
        
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
        with open(path, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(state, f, indent=2)
    
    def load(self, path: str = "world_model.json") -> bool:
        """Load the world model from file"""
        try:
            if os.path.exists(path):
                with open(path, 'r') as f:
                    state = json.load(f)
                
                # Load world state
                ws_data = state.get('world_state', {})
                self.world_state = WorldState(
                    timestamp=ws_data.get('timestamp', time.time()),
                    known_entities=set(ws_data.get('known_entities', [])),
                    known_relationships=set(ws_data.get('known_relationships', [])),
                    current_location=ws_data.get('current_location'),
                    current_time=ws_data.get('current_time', time.time()),
                    active_entities=set(ws_data.get('active_entities', [])),
                    recent_changes=ws_data.get('recent_changes', [])
                )
                
                # Load knowledge graph
                kg_data = state.get('knowledge_graph', {})
                self.knowledge_graph.entities = {
                    k: Entity.from_dict(v) for k, v in kg_data.get('entities', {}).items()
                }
                self.knowledge_graph.relationships = {
                    k: Relationship.from_dict(v) for k, v in kg_data.get('relationships', {}).items()
                }
                
                # Rebuild indexes
                for entity_id, entity in self.knowledge_graph.entities.items():
                    self.knowledge_graph.entity_name_index[entity.name.lower()] = entity_id
                    self.knowledge_graph.entity_type_index[entity.entity_type].add(entity_id)
                
                for rel_id, rel in self.knowledge_graph.relationships.items():
                    self.knowledge_graph.relationship_index[(
                        rel.source_entity_id, rel.relationship_type, rel.target_entity_id
                    )].add(rel_id)
                    self.knowledge_graph.outgoing_relationships[rel.source_entity_id].add(rel_id)
                    self.knowledge_graph.incoming_relationships[rel.target_entity_id].add(rel_id)
                
                # Load change history
                self.change_history = state.get('change_history', [])
                
                return True
        except FileNotFoundError:
            pass
        return False
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Clear the world model"""
        self.knowledge_graph.clear()
        self.world_state = WorldState()
        self.change_history.clear()
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get world model statistics"""
        return {
            'entities': len(self.knowledge_graph.entities),
            'relationships': len(self.knowledge_graph.relationships),
            'entity_types': {k: len(v) for k, v in self.knowledge_graph.entity_type_index.items()},
            'known_entities': len(self.world_state.known_entities),
            'known_relationships': len(self.world_state.known_relationships),
            'recent_changes': len(self.world_state.recent_changes)
        }


# FILE: agi_core/world_model/knowledge_graph.py
"""
Knowledge Graph - Structured Knowledge Representation

This module implements a knowledge graph for representing entities,
relationships, and properties in a structured format that enables
semantic reasoning and inference.
"""

import time
import json
import os
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Set, Tuple
from collections import defaultdict
import hashlib


@dataclass
class Property:
    """A property of an entity"""
    name: str
    value: Any
    data_type: str = "string"  # string, number, boolean, list, etc.
    confidence: float = 1.0
    source: str = ""
    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'name': self.name,
            'value': self.value,
            'data_type': self.data_type,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'source': self.source,
            'timestamp': self.timestamp
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'Property':
        return cls(
            name=data.get('name', ''),
            value=data.get('value'),
            data_type=data.get('data_type', 'string'),
            confidence=data.get('confidence', 1.0),
            source=data.get('source', ''),
            timestamp=data.get('timestamp', time.time())
        )


@dataclass
class Entity:
    """An entity in the knowledge graph"""
    entity_id: str
    name: str
    entity_type: str = "thing"  # thing, person, place, concept, event, etc.
    description: str = ""
    properties: Dict[str, Property] = field(default_factory=dict)
    created_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    updated_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    confidence: float = 1.0
    
    def add_property(self, name: str, value: Any, data_type: str = "string",
                     confidence: float = 1.0, source: str = "") -> Property:
        """Add a property to this entity"""
        prop = Property(
            name=name,
            value=value,
            data_type=data_type,
            confidence=confidence,
            source=source
        )
        self.properties[name] = prop
        self.updated_at = time.time()
        return prop
    
    def get_property(self, name: str) -> Optional[Property]:
        """Get a property by name"""
        return self.properties.get(name)
    
    def remove_property(self, name: str) -> bool:
        """Remove a property"""
        if name in self.properties:
            del self.properties[name]
            self.updated_at = time.time()
            return True
        return False
    
    def has_property(self, name: str, value: Any = None) -> bool:
        """Check if entity has a property with optional value"""
        if name not in self.properties:
            return False
        if value is not None:
            return self.properties[name].value == value
        return True
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'entity_id': self.entity_id,
            'name': self.name,
            'entity_type': self.entity_type,
            'description': self.description,
            'properties': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.properties.items()},
            'created_at': self.created_at,
            'updated_at': self.updated_at,
            'confidence': self.confidence
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'Entity':
        entity = cls(
            entity_id=data.get('entity_id', ''),
            name=data.get('name', ''),
            entity_type=data.get('entity_type', 'thing'),
            description=data.get('description', ''),
            created_at=data.get('created_at', time.time()),
            updated_at=data.get('updated_at', time.time()),
            confidence=data.get('confidence', 1.0)
        )
        entity.properties = {k: Property.from_dict(v) for k, v in data.get('properties', {}).items()}
        return entity


@dataclass
class Relationship:
    """A relationship between entities in the knowledge graph"""
    relationship_id: str
    relationship_type: str  # e.g., "is_a", "has", "part_of", "causes", "located_at"
    source_entity_id: str
    target_entity_id: str
    confidence: float = 1.0
    created_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    properties: Dict[str, Property] = field(default_factory=dict)
    is_directional: bool = True
    
    def add_property(self, name: str, value: Any, data_type: str = "string",
                     confidence: float = 1.0, source: str = "") -> Property:
        """Add a property to this relationship"""
        prop = Property(
            name=name,
            value=value,
            data_type=data_type,
            confidence=confidence,
            source=source
        )
        self.properties[name] = prop
        return prop
    
    def get_inverse_relationship_type(self) -> str:
        """Get the inverse relationship type"""
        inverse_map = {
            "is_a": "is_a",  # is_a is symmetric in some contexts
            "has": "has",
            "part_of": "has_part",
            "has_part": "part_of",
            "causes": "caused_by",
            "caused_by": "causes",
            "located_at": "contains",
            "contains": "located_at",
            "connected_to": "connected_to",
            "related_to": "related_to",
            "parent_of": "child_of",
            "child_of": "parent_of",
            "friend_of": "friend_of",
            "knows": "known_by",
            "known_by": "knows",
        }
        return inverse_map.get(self.relationship_type, self.relationship_type)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'relationship_id': self.relationship_id,
            'relationship_type': self.relationship_type,
            'source_entity_id': self.source_entity_id,
            'target_entity_id': self.target_entity_id,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'created_at': self.created_at,
            'properties': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.properties.items()},
            'is_directional': self.is_directional
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'Relationship':
        rel = cls(
            relationship_id=data.get('relationship_id', ''),
            relationship_type=data.get('relationship_type', ''),
            source_entity_id=data.get('source_entity_id', ''),
            target_entity_id=data.get('target_entity_id', ''),
            confidence=data.get('confidence', 1.0),
            created_at=data.get('created_at', time.time()),
            is_directional=data.get('is_directional', True)
        )
        rel.properties = {k: Property.from_dict(v) for k, v in data.get('properties', {}).items()}
        return rel


class KnowledgeGraph:
    """
    Knowledge graph for structured knowledge representation.
    
    This graph stores:
    - Entities with properties
    - Relationships between entities
    - Type hierarchies
    - Semantic constraints
    """
    
    def __init__(self, storage_path: str = "knowledge_graph.json"):
        self.storage_path = storage_path
        self.entities: Dict[str, Entity] = {}
        self.relationships: Dict[str, Relationship] = {}
        
        # Indexes for fast lookup
        self.entity_name_index: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self.entity_type_index: Dict[str, Set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
        self.relationship_index: Dict[Tuple[str, str, str], Set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
        self.incoming_relationships: Dict[str, Set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
        self.outgoing_relationships: Dict[str, Set[str]] = defaultdict(set)
        
        self.load()
        
    def add_entity(self, name: str, entity_type: str = "thing",
                   entity_id: Optional[str] = None, description: str = "") -> Entity:
        """Add an entity to the knowledge graph"""
        entity_id = entity_id or self._generate_id(f"entity_{name}")
        
        entity = Entity(
            entity_id=entity_id,
            name=name,
            entity_type=entity_type,
            description=description
        )
        
        self.entities[entity_id] = entity
        self.entity_name_index[name.lower()] = entity_id
        self.entity_type_index[entity_type].add(entity_id)
        
        return entity
    
    def get_entity(self, entity_id: str) -> Optional[Entity]:
        """Get an entity by ID"""
        return self.entities.get(entity_id)
    
    def get_entity_by_name(self, name: str) -> Optional[Entity]:
        """Get an entity by name"""
        entity_id = self.entity_name_index.get(name.lower())
        if entity_id:
            return self.entities.get(entity_id)
        return None
    
    def get_entities_by_type(self, entity_type: str) -> List[Entity]:
        """Get all entities of a specific type"""
        entity_ids = self.entity_type_index.get(entity_type, set())
        return [self.entities[eid] for eid in entity_ids if eid in self.entities]
    
    def add_relationship(self, relationship_type: str, source_entity_id: str,
                        target_entity_id: str, relationship_id: Optional[str] = None,
                        is_directional: bool = True) -> Relationship:
        """Add a relationship between entities"""
        relationship_id = relationship_id or self._generate_id(
            f"rel_{relationship_type}_{source_entity_id}_{target_entity_id}"
        )
        
        rel = Relationship(
            relationship_id=relationship_id,
            relationship_type=relationship_type,
            source_entity_id=source_entity_id,
            target_entity_id=target_entity_id,
            is_directional=is_directional
        )
        
        self.relationships[relationship_id] = rel
        
        # Update indexes
        self.relationship_index[(source_entity_id, relationship_type, target_entity_id)].add(relationship_id)
        self.outgoing_relationships[source_entity_id].add(relationship_id)
        self.incoming_relationships[target_entity_id].add(relationship_id)
        
        # If not directional, also add inverse
        if not is_directional:
            inverse_type = rel.get_inverse_relationship_type()
            self.relationship_index[(target_entity_id, inverse_type, source_entity_id)].add(relationship_id)
        
        return rel
    
    def get_relationship(self, relationship_id: str) -> Optional[Relationship]:
        """Get a relationship by ID"""
        return self.relationships.get(relationship_id)
    
    def get_relationships(self, entity_id: str, relationship_type: str = None,
                         direction: str = "both") -> List[Relationship]:
        """Get relationships for an entity"""
        results = []
        
        if direction in ["outgoing", "both"]:
            for rel_id in self.outgoing_relationships.get(entity_id, set()):
                rel = self.relationships.get(rel_id)
                if rel and (relationship_type is None or rel.relationship_type == relationship_type):
                    results.append(rel)
        
        if direction in ["incoming", "both"]:
            for rel_id in self.incoming_relationships.get(entity_id, set()):
                rel = self.relationships.get(rel_id)
                if rel and (relationship_type is None or rel.relationship_type == relationship_type):
                    results.append(rel)
        
        return results
    
    def get_related_entities(self, entity_id: str, relationship_type: str = None,
                            direction: str = "both") -> List[Entity]:
        """Get entities related to a given entity"""
        relationships = self.get_relationships(entity_id, relationship_type, direction)
        
        entity_ids = set()
        for rel in relationships:
            if rel.source_entity_id != entity_id:
                entity_ids.add(rel.source_entity_id)
            if rel.target_entity_id != entity_id:
                entity_ids.add(rel.target_entity_id)
        
        return [self.entities[eid] for eid in entity_ids if eid in self.entities]
    
    def query(self, query: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Entity]:
        """
        Query the knowledge graph.
        
        Query format:
        {
            'entity_type': 'person',
            'properties': {'age': {'gt': 30}},
            'relationships': [
                {'type': 'works_at', 'target': 'company_x'}
            ]
        }
        """
        results = []
        
        # Filter by entity type
        entity_type = query.get('entity_type')
        if entity_type:
            candidates = self.get_entities_by_type(entity_type)
        else:
            candidates = list(self.entities.values())
        
        # Filter by properties
        properties = query.get('properties', {})
        for entity in candidates:
            match = True
            for prop_name, prop_query in properties.items():
                prop = entity.get_property(prop_name)
                if not prop:
                    match = False
                    break
                
                # Check property value against query
                if not self._check_property_value(prop.value, prop_query):
                    match = False
                    break
            
            if match:
                results.append(entity)
        
        # Filter by relationships
        relationships = query.get('relationships', [])
        for rel_query in relationships:
            filtered_results = []
            for entity in results:
                rels = self.get_relationships(
                    entity.entity_id,
                    rel_query.get('type'),
                    rel_query.get('direction', 'outgoing')
                )
                
                # Check if any relationship matches
                target_id = rel_query.get('target')
                if target_id:
                    has_match = any(
                        (rel.source_entity_id == target_id or rel.target_entity_id == target_id)
                        for rel in rels
                    )
                else:
                    has_match = len(rels) > 0
                
                if has_match:
                    filtered_results.append(entity)
            
            results = filtered_results
        
        return results
    
    def _check_property_value(self, value: Any, query: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        """Check if a property value matches a query"""
        for op, op_value in query.items():
            if op == 'eq':
                if value != op_value:
                    return False
            elif op == 'gt':
                if not (value > op_value):
                    return False
            elif op == 'lt':
                if not (value < op_value):
                    return False
            elif op == 'gte':
                if not (value >= op_value):
                    return False
            elif op == 'lte':
                if not (value <= op_value):
                    return False
            elif op == 'contains':
                if isinstance(value, str) and op_value not in value:
                    return False
                elif isinstance(value, list) and op_value not in value:
                    return False
            elif op == 'in':
                if value not in op_value:
                    return False
        return True
    
    def infer_type_hierarchy(self, entity_id: str) -> List[str]:
        """Infer the type hierarchy for an entity"""
        types = []
        entity = self.get_entity(entity_id)
        if not entity:
            return types
        
        # Start with the entity's own type
        current_type = entity.entity_type
        types.append(current_type)
        
        # Follow "is_a" relationships to find parent types
        is_a_rels = self.get_relationships(entity_id, "is_a", "outgoing")
        for rel in is_a_rels:
            parent_entity = self.get_entity(rel.target_entity_id)
            if parent_entity:
                parent_type = parent_entity.entity_type
                if parent_type not in types:
                    types.append(parent_type)
                    # Recursively get parent types
                    parent_types = self.infer_type_hierarchy(rel.target_entity_id)
                    for pt in parent_types:
                        if pt not in types:
                            types.append(pt)
        
        return types
    
    def _generate_id(self, prefix: str) -> str:
        """Generate a unique ID"""
        timestamp = int(time.time() * 1000)
        random_part = hashlib.md5(str(timestamp).encode()).hexdigest()[:4]
        return f"{prefix}_{timestamp}_{random_part}"
    
    def save(self) -> None:
        """Save knowledge graph to file"""
        state = {
            'entities': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.entities.items()},
            'relationships': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.relationships.items()}
        }
        
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.storage_path), exist_ok=True)
        with open(self.storage_path, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(state, f, indent=2)
    
    def load(self) -> bool:
        """Load knowledge graph from file"""
        try:
            if os.path.exists(self.storage_path):
                with open(self.storage_path, 'r') as f:
                    state = json.load(f)
                
                self.entities = {k: Entity.from_dict(v) for k, v in state.get('entities', {}).items()}
                self.relationships = {k: Relationship.from_dict(v) for k, v in state.get('relationships', {}).items()}
                
                # Rebuild indexes
                for entity_id, entity in self.entities.items():
                    self.entity_name_index[entity.name.lower()] = entity_id
                    self.entity_type_index[entity.entity_type].add(entity_id)
                
                for rel_id, rel in self.relationships.items():
                    self.relationship_index[(rel.source_entity_id, rel.relationship_type, rel.target_entity_id)].add(rel_id)
                    self.outgoing_relationships[rel.source_entity_id].add(rel_id)
                    self.incoming_relationships[rel.target_entity_id].add(rel_id)
                
                return True
        except FileNotFoundError:
            pass
        return False
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Clear the knowledge graph"""
        self.entities.clear()
        self.relationships.clear()
        self.entity_name_index.clear()
        self.entity_type_index.clear()
        self.relationship_index.clear()
        self.incoming_relationships.clear()
        self.outgoing_relationships.clear()
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get knowledge graph statistics"""
        return {
            'total_entities': len(self.entities),
            'total_relationships': len(self.relationships),
            'entity_types': {k: len(v) for k, v in self.entity_type_index.items()},
            'most_common_entity_type': max(self.entity_type_index.items(), key=lambda x: len(x[1]))[0] if self.entity_type_index else None,
            'relationship_types': self._count_relationship_types()
        }
    
    def _count_relationship_types(self) -> Dict[str, int]:
        """Count the number of each relationship type"""
        counts = defaultdict(int)
        for rel in self.relationships.values():
            counts[rel.relationship_type] += 1
        return dict(counts)
