# FILE: agi_core/planning/planner.py
"""
Planner - Core Planning and Goal Achievement System

This module provides the AGI's planning capabilities, including:
- Goal definition and management
- Action planning and sequencing
- Plan execution and monitoring
- Plan adaptation and repair
"""

import time
import json
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Callable, Set, Tuple
from enum import Enum
import hashlib


class GoalStatus(Enum):
    """Status of a goal"""
    PENDING = "pending"
    ACTIVE = "active"
    ACHIEVED = "achieved"
    FAILED = "failed"
    ABANDONED = "abandoned"


class ActionStatus(Enum):
    """Status of an action"""
    PENDING = "pending"
    EXECUTING = "executing"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    FAILED = "failed"
    SKIPPED = "skipped"


class PlanStatus(Enum):
    """Status of a plan"""
    DRAFT = "draft"
    ACTIVE = "active"
    PAUSED = "paused"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    FAILED = "failed"
    CANCELLED = "cancelled"


@dataclass
class Goal:
    """A goal that the AGI is trying to achieve"""
    goal_id: str
    description: str
    priority: float = 0.5  # 0-1, higher is more important
    deadline: Optional[float] = None  # Unix timestamp
    status: GoalStatus = GoalStatus.PENDING
    created_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    dependencies: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # Other goal IDs
    subgoals: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    constraints: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    success_criteria: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    failure_criteria: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    progress: float = 0.0  # 0-1
    
    def is_achieved(self) -> bool:
        """Check if goal is achieved"""
        return self.status == GoalStatus.ACHIEVED
    
    def is_active(self) -> bool:
        """Check if goal is currently active"""
        return self.status == GoalStatus.ACTIVE
    
    def is_pending(self) -> bool:
        """Check if goal is pending"""
        return self.status == GoalStatus.PENDING
    
    def is_failed(self) -> bool:
        """Check if goal has failed"""
        return self.status == GoalStatus.FAILED
    
    def update_progress(self, new_progress: float) -> None:
        """Update the progress of this goal"""
        self.progress = max(0.0, min(1.0, new_progress))
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'goal_id': self.goal_id,
            'description': self.description,
            'priority': self.priority,
            'deadline': self.deadline,
            'status': self.status.value,
            'created_at': self.created_at,
            'dependencies': self.dependencies,
            'subgoals': self.subgoals,
            'constraints': self.constraints,
            'success_criteria': self.success_criteria,
            'failure_criteria': self.failure_criteria,
            'progress': self.progress
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'Goal':
        return cls(
            goal_id=data.get('goal_id', ''),
            description=data.get('description', ''),
            priority=data.get('priority', 0.5),
            deadline=data.get('deadline'),
            status=GoalStatus(data.get('status', 'pending')),
            created_at=data.get('created_at', time.time()),
            dependencies=data.get('dependencies', []),
            subgoals=data.get('subgoals', []),
            constraints=data.get('constraints', []),
            success_criteria=data.get('success_criteria', []),
            failure_criteria=data.get('failure_criteria', []),
            progress=data.get('progress', 0.0)
        )


@dataclass
class Action:
    """An action that can be executed to achieve a goal"""
    action_id: str
    name: str
    description: str
    goal_id: str  # The goal this action contributes to
    preconditions: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    postconditions: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    parameters: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    estimated_duration: float = 0.0  # Seconds
    estimated_cost: float = 0.0
    priority: float = 0.5
    status: ActionStatus = ActionStatus.PENDING
    start_time: Optional[float] = None
    end_time: Optional[float] = None
    result: Optional[str] = None
    error: Optional[str] = None
    
    def is_completed(self) -> bool:
        """Check if action is completed"""
        return self.status == ActionStatus.COMPLETED
    
    def is_failed(self) -> bool:
        """Check if action has failed"""
        return self.status == ActionStatus.FAILED
    
    def is_executing(self) -> bool:
        """Check if action is currently executing"""
        return self.status == ActionStatus.EXECUTING
    
    def execute(self, executor: Callable) -> bool:
        """Execute this action using the provided executor"""
        self.status = ActionStatus.EXECUTING
        self.start_time = time.time()
        
        try:
            result = executor(self)
            self.status = ActionStatus.COMPLETED
            self.end_time = time.time()
            self.result = str(result)
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            self.status = ActionStatus.FAILED
            self.end_time = time.time()
            self.error = str(e)
            return False
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'action_id': self.action_id,
            'name': self.name,
            'description': self.description,
            'goal_id': self.goal_id,
            'preconditions': self.preconditions,
            'postconditions': self.postconditions,
            'parameters': self.parameters,
            'estimated_duration': self.estimated_duration,
            'estimated_cost': self.estimated_cost,
            'priority': self.priority,
            'status': self.status.value,
            'start_time': self.start_time,
            'end_time': self.end_time,
            'result': self.result,
            'error': self.error
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'Action':
        return cls(
            action_id=data.get('action_id', ''),
            name=data.get('name', ''),
            description=data.get('description', ''),
            goal_id=data.get('goal_id', ''),
            preconditions=data.get('preconditions', []),
            postconditions=data.get('postconditions', []),
            parameters=data.get('parameters', {}),
            estimated_duration=data.get('estimated_duration', 0.0),
            estimated_cost=data.get('estimated_cost', 0.0),
            priority=data.get('priority', 0.5),
            status=ActionStatus(data.get('status', 'pending')),
            start_time=data.get('start_time'),
            end_time=data.get('end_time'),
            result=data.get('result'),
            error=data.get('error')
        )


@dataclass
class Plan:
    """A plan consisting of ordered actions to achieve a goal"""
    plan_id: str
    goal_id: str
    name: str
    description: str
    actions: List[Action] = field(default_factory=list)
    status: PlanStatus = PlanStatus.DRAFT
    created_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    started_at: Optional[float] = None
    completed_at: Optional[float] = None
    current_action_index: int = 0
    progress: float = 0.0
    
    def add_action(self, action: Action) -> None:
        """Add an action to the plan"""
        self.actions.append(action)
    
    def insert_action(self, index: int, action: Action) -> None:
        """Insert an action at a specific position"""
        self.actions.insert(index, action)
    
    def remove_action(self, action_id: str) -> bool:
        """Remove an action from the plan"""
        for i, action in enumerate(self.actions):
            if action.action_id == action_id:
                self.actions.pop(i)
                return True
        return False
    
    def get_current_action(self) -> Optional[Action]:
        """Get the current action being executed"""
        if 0 <= self.current_action_index < len(self.actions):
            return self.actions[self.current_action_index]
        return None
    
    def get_next_action(self) -> Optional[Action]:
        """Get the next action to execute"""
        if self.current_action_index < len(self.actions):
            return self.actions[self.current_action_index]
        return None
    
    def advance_to_next_action(self) -> bool:
        """Advance to the next action in the plan"""
        if self.current_action_index < len(self.actions):
            self.current_action_index += 1
            return True
        return False
    
    def is_complete(self) -> bool:
        """Check if the plan is complete"""
        return self.status == PlanStatus.COMPLETED
    
    def is_active(self) -> bool:
        """Check if the plan is currently active"""
        return self.status == PlanStatus.ACTIVE
    
    def calculate_progress(self) -> float:
        """Calculate the progress of the plan"""
        if not self.actions:
            return 0.0
        
        completed = sum(1 for a in self.actions if a.is_completed())
        self.progress = completed / len(self.actions)
        return self.progress
    
    def get_remaining_actions(self) -> List[Action]:
        """Get actions that haven't been completed yet"""
        return [a for a in self.actions if not a.is_completed()]
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'plan_id': self.plan_id,
            'goal_id': self.goal_id,
            'name': self.name,
            'description': self.description,
            'actions': [a.to_dict() for a in self.actions],
            'status': self.status.value,
            'created_at': self.created_at,
            'started_at': self.started_at,
            'completed_at': self.completed_at,
            'current_action_index': self.current_action_index,
            'progress': self.progress
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'Plan':
        plan = cls(
            plan_id=data.get('plan_id', ''),
            goal_id=data.get('goal_id', ''),
            name=data.get('name', ''),
            description=data.get('description', ''),
            actions=[Action.from_dict(a) for a in data.get('actions', [])],
            status=PlanStatus(data.get('status', 'draft')),
            created_at=data.get('created_at', time.time()),
            started_at=data.get('started_at'),
            completed_at=data.get('completed_at'),
            current_action_index=data.get('current_action_index', 0),
            progress=data.get('progress', 0.0)
        )
        return plan


class Planner:
    """
    Core planning engine for the AGI.
    
    This engine can:
    - Create and manage goals
    - Generate plans to achieve goals
    - Execute and monitor plans
    - Adapt plans based on changing conditions
    - Repair failed plans
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.goals: Dict[str, Goal] = {}
        self.plans: Dict[str, Plan] = {}
        self.actions: Dict[str, Action] = {}
        self.current_plan: Optional[Plan] = None
        self.current_goal: Optional[Goal] = None
        self.execution_history: List[str] = []
        
    def create_goal(self, description: str, goal_id: Optional[str] = None,
                    priority: float = 0.5, deadline: Optional[float] = None,
                    **kwargs) -> Goal:
        """Create a new goal"""
        goal_id = goal_id or self._generate_id(f"goal_{description}")
        
        goal = Goal(
            goal_id=goal_id,
            description=description,
            priority=priority,
            deadline=deadline,
            **kwargs
        )
        
        self.goals[goal_id] = goal
        return goal
    
    def get_goal(self, goal_id: str) -> Optional[Goal]:
        """Get a goal by ID"""
        return self.goals.get(goal_id)
    
    def update_goal(self, goal_id: str, **kwargs) -> bool:
        """Update a goal"""
        if goal_id in self.goals:
            goal = self.goals[goal_id]
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(goal, key):
                    setattr(goal, key, value)
            return True
        return False
    
    def delete_goal(self, goal_id: str) -> bool:
        """Delete a goal"""
        if goal_id in self.goals:
            del self.goals[goal_id]
            return True
        return False
    
    def create_plan(self, goal_id: str, name: str = None,
                    description: str = "") -> Optional[Plan]:
        """Create a plan for a goal"""
        if goal_id not in self.goals:
            return None
        
        goal = self.goals[goal_id]
        plan_id = self._generate_id(f"plan_{goal_id}")
        
        plan = Plan(
            plan_id=plan_id,
            goal_id=goal_id,
            name=name or f"Plan for {goal.description[:30]}...",
            description=description
        )
        
        self.plans[plan_id] = plan
        goal.status = GoalStatus.ACTIVE
        
        return plan
    
    def get_plan(self, plan_id: str) -> Optional[Plan]:
        """Get a plan by ID"""
        return self.plans.get(plan_id)
    
    def set_current_plan(self, plan_id: str) -> bool:
        """Set the current active plan"""
        if plan_id in self.plans:
            self.current_plan = self.plans[plan_id]
            goal = self.goals.get(self.current_plan.goal_id)
            if goal:
                self.current_goal = goal
            return True
        return False
    
    def plan_for_goal(self, goal_id: str, max_actions: int = 10) -> Optional[Plan]:
        """
        Generate a plan to achieve a goal.
        
        This is a simplified version. In a full implementation, this would
        use the LLM to generate creative plans based on the goal.
        """
        if goal_id not in self.goals:
            return None
        
        goal = self.goals[goal_id]
        plan = self.create_plan(goal_id)
        
        if not plan:
            return None
        
        # Generate actions based on goal description
        actions = self._generate_actions_for_goal(goal, max_actions)
        
        for action in actions:
            plan.add_action(action)
        
        plan.status = PlanStatus.DRAFT
        return plan
    
    def _generate_actions_for_goal(self, goal: Goal, max_actions: int) -> List[Action]:
        """Generate actions to achieve a goal"""
        actions = []
        
        # Simple action generation based on goal description
        description = goal.description.lower()
        
        # Extract potential actions from the goal
        action_verbs = ['create', 'build', 'find', 'get', 'make', 'write', 
                       'read', 'analyze', 'process', 'send', 'receive', 
                       'move', 'go', 'take', 'give', 'use', 'learn']
        
        for i, verb in enumerate(action_verbs[:max_actions]):
            if verb in description:
                action = Action(
                    action_id=self._generate_id(f"action_{goal.goal_id}_{i}"),
                    name=f"{verb.capitalize()} action",
                    description=f"Perform {verb} to achieve: {goal.description[:50]}",
                    goal_id=goal.goal_id,
                    preconditions=[f"Goal {goal.goal_id} is active"],
                    postconditions=[f"Progress toward {goal.description}"],
                    estimated_duration=5.0,
                    priority=goal.priority * 0.8
                )
                actions.append(action)
        
        # If no specific actions found, create generic ones
        if not actions:
            for i in range(min(3, max_actions)):
                action = Action(
                    action_id=self._generate_id(f"action_{goal.goal_id}_{i}"),
                    name=f"Step {i+1}",
                    description=f"Step {i+1} toward: {goal.description[:50]}",
                    goal_id=goal.goal_id,
                    estimated_duration=5.0,
                    priority=goal.priority
                )
                actions.append(action)
        
        return actions
    
    def execute_plan(self, plan_id: str, action_executor: Callable) -> bool:
        """Execute a plan using the provided action executor"""
        plan = self.get_plan(plan_id)
        if not plan:
            return False
        
        if plan.status != PlanStatus.DRAFT and plan.status != PlanStatus.PAUSED:
            return False
        
        plan.status = PlanStatus.ACTIVE
        plan.started_at = time.time()
        
        for action in plan.actions:
            if not action.is_completed():
                plan.current_action_index = plan.actions.index(action)
                
                # Execute the action
                success = action.execute(action_executor)
                
                if not success:
                    # Plan failed
                    plan.status = PlanStatus.FAILED
                    return False
                
                # Record execution
                self.execution_history.append(action.action_id)
        
        # All actions completed
        plan.status = PlanStatus.COMPLETED
        plan.completed_at = time.time()
        plan.progress = 1.0
        
        # Mark goal as achieved
        goal = self.goals.get(plan.goal_id)
        if goal:
            goal.status = GoalStatus.ACHIEVED
            goal.progress = 1.0
        
        return True
    
    def execute_next_action(self, plan_id: str, action_executor: Callable) -> bool:
        """Execute just the next action in a plan"""
        plan = self.get_plan(plan_id)
        if not plan or plan.status != PlanStatus.ACTIVE:
            return False
        
        next_action = plan.get_next_action()
        if not next_action:
            # Plan is complete
            plan.status = PlanStatus.COMPLETED
            plan.completed_at = time.time()
            return True
        
        # Execute the action
        success = next_action.execute(action_executor)
        
        if success:
            plan.advance_to_next_action()
            plan.calculate_progress()
            self.execution_history.append(next_action.action_id)
            
            # If this was the last action, mark plan as complete
            if not plan.get_next_action():
                plan.status = PlanStatus.COMPLETED
                plan.completed_at = time.time()
        else:
            plan.status = PlanStatus.FAILED
        
        return success
    
    def adapt_plan(self, plan_id: str, new_information: str) -> bool:
        """
        Adapt a plan based on new information.
        
        This would use the LLM to modify the plan intelligently.
        For now, this is a placeholder.
        """
        plan = self.get_plan(plan_id)
        if not plan:
            return False
        
        # In a full implementation, we would:
        # 1. Analyze new information
        # 2. Identify which parts of the plan are affected
        # 3. Modify or replace affected actions
        # 4. Re-validate the plan
        
        # For now, just mark that adaptation occurred
        return True
    
    def repair_plan(self, plan_id: str) -> bool:
        """
        Repair a failed plan.
        
        This would identify why the plan failed and create a new plan
        or modify the existing one to avoid the failure.
        """
        plan = self.get_plan(plan_id)
        if not plan or plan.status != PlanStatus.FAILED:
            return False
        
        # In a full implementation, we would:
        # 1. Analyze the failure
        # 2. Identify the failed action
        # 3. Create alternative actions
        # 4. Generate a new plan or modify the existing one
        
        # For now, reset the plan to draft status
        plan.status = PlanStatus.DRAFT
        plan.current_action_index = 0
        
        # Reset all actions to pending
        for action in plan.actions:
            if action.is_failed():
                action.status = ActionStatus.PENDING
                action.start_time = None
                action.end_time = None
                action.error = None
        
        return True
    
    def _generate_id(self, prefix: str) -> str:
        """Generate a unique ID"""
        timestamp = int(time.time() * 1000)
        random_part = hashlib.md5(str(timestamp).encode()).hexdigest()[:4]
        return f"{prefix}_{timestamp}_{random_part}"
    
    def get_active_goals(self) -> List[Goal]:
        """Get all active goals"""
        return [g for g in self.goals.values() if g.is_active()]
    
    def get_pending_goals(self) -> List[Goal]:
        """Get all pending goals"""
        return [g for g in self.goals.values() if g.is_pending()]
    
    def get_achieved_goals(self) -> List[Goal]:
        """Get all achieved goals"""
        return [g for g in self.goals.values() if g.is_achieved()]
    
    def get_failed_goals(self) -> List[Goal]:
        """Get all failed goals"""
        return [g for g in self.goals.values() if g.is_failed()]
    
    def get_active_plans(self) -> List[Plan]:
        """Get all active plans"""
        return [p for p in self.plans.values() if p.is_active()]
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Clear all goals, plans, and actions"""
        self.goals.clear()
        self.plans.clear()
        self.actions.clear()
        self.current_plan = None
        self.current_goal = None
        self.execution_history.clear()
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get planning statistics"""
        return {
            'total_goals': len(self.goals),
            'active_goals': len(self.get_active_goals()),
            'pending_goals': len(self.get_pending_goals()),
            'achieved_goals': len(self.get_achieved_goals()),
            'failed_goals': len(self.get_failed_goals()),
            'total_plans': len(self.plans),
            'active_plans': len(self.get_active_plans()),
            'completed_plans': len([p for p in self.plans.values() if p.is_complete()]),
            'total_actions_executed': len(self.execution_history)
        }
    
    def save_state(self, path: str = "planner_state.json") -> None:
        """Save planner state to file"""
        state = {
            'goals': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.goals.items()},
            'plans': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.plans.items()},
            'actions': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.actions.items()},
            'execution_history': self.execution_history
        }
        
        import os
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
        with open(path, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(state, f, indent=2)
    
    def load_state(self, path: str = "planner_state.json") -> bool:
        """Load planner state from file"""
        try:
            import os
            if os.path.exists(path):
                with open(path, 'r') as f:
                    state = json.load(f)
                
                self.goals = {k: Goal.from_dict(v) for k, v in state.get('goals', {}).items()}
                self.plans = {k: Plan.from_dict(v) for k, v in state.get('plans', {}).items()}
                self.actions = {k: Action.from_dict(v) for k, v in state.get('actions', {}).items()}
                self.execution_history = state.get('execution_history', [])
                
                return True
        except FileNotFoundError:
            pass
        return False


# FILE: agi_core/planning/decision_engine.py
"""
Decision Engine - Multi-Criteria Decision Making

This module provides the AGI's decision-making capabilities, including:
- Option evaluation
- Multi-criteria decision analysis
- Cost-benefit analysis
- Risk assessment
- Decision validation
"""

import time
import json
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Callable, Tuple
from enum import Enum
import math


class DecisionStrategy(Enum):
    """Different decision-making strategies"""
    MAXIMIZE_UTILITY = "maximize_utility"
    MINIMIZE_REGRET = "minimize_regret"
    MINIMIZE_RISK = "minimize_risk"
    MAXIMIZE_CONFIDENCE = "maximize_confidence"
    BALANCED = "balanced"
    CONSENSUS = "consensus"


class DecisionStatus(Enum):
    """Status of a decision"""
    PENDING = "pending"
    EVALUATING = "evaluating"
    DECIDED = "decided"
    EXECUTED = "executed"
    FAILED = "failed"


@dataclass
class Criterion:
    """A criterion for evaluating options"""
    name: str
    description: str
    weight: float = 1.0  # 0-1, importance of this criterion
    is_maximizing: bool = True  # True if higher values are better
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'name': self.name,
            'description': self.description,
            'weight': self.weight,
            'is_maximizing': self.is_maximizing
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'Criterion':
        return cls(
            name=data.get('name', ''),
            description=data.get('description', ''),
            weight=data.get('weight', 1.0),
            is_maximizing=data.get('is_maximizing', True)
        )


@dataclass
class Option:
    """An option to be evaluated in a decision"""
    option_id: str
    description: str
    scores: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)  # criterion_name -> score
    confidence: float = 1.0
    probability: float = 1.0  # Probability of success
    cost: float = 0.0
    benefits: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    risks: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    dependencies: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    def get_score(self, criterion: Criterion) -> float:
        """Get the score for a specific criterion"""
        return self.scores.get(criterion.name, 0.0)
    
    def set_score(self, criterion_name: str, score: float) -> None:
        """Set the score for a criterion"""
        self.scores[criterion_name] = score
    
    def get_weighted_score(self, criteria: List[Criterion]) -> float:
        """Calculate weighted score across all criteria"""
        total_score = 0.0
        total_weight = 0.0
        
        for criterion in criteria:
            score = self.get_score(criterion)
            if not criterion.is_maximizing:
                # For minimizing criteria, invert the score
                score = 1.0 - score
            
            total_score += score * criterion.weight
            total_weight += criterion.weight
        
        return total_score / total_weight if total_weight > 0 else 0.0
    
    def get_utility(self, criteria: List[Criterion], 
                    risk_weight: float = 0.3) -> float:
        """Calculate overall utility considering benefits, costs, and risks"""
        # Weighted score from criteria
        weighted_score = self.get_weighted_score(criteria)
        
        # Adjust for probability of success
        adjusted_score = weighted_score * self.probability
        
        # Adjust for cost (higher cost reduces utility)
        cost_factor = 1.0 / (1.0 + self.cost * 0.1)  # Normalize cost impact
        
        # Adjust for risks
        risk_count = len(self.risks)
        risk_factor = 1.0 - (risk_count * 0.1 * risk_weight)
        
        # Combine factors
        utility = adjusted_score * cost_factor * risk_factor
        
        return max(0.0, min(1.0, utility))
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'option_id': self.option_id,
            'description': self.description,
            'scores': self.scores,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'probability': self.probability,
            'cost': self.cost,
            'benefits': self.benefits,
            'risks': self.risks,
            'dependencies': self.dependencies
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'Option':
        return cls(
            option_id=data.get('option_id', ''),
            description=data.get('description', ''),
            scores=data.get('scores', {}),
            confidence=data.get('confidence', 1.0),
            probability=data.get('probability', 1.0),
            cost=data.get('cost', 0.0),
            benefits=data.get('benefits', []),
            risks=data.get('risks', []),
            dependencies=data.get('dependencies', [])
        )


@dataclass
class Decision:
    """A decision to be made"""
    decision_id: str
    description: str
    context: str = ""
    options: List[Option] = field(default_factory=list)
    criteria: List[Criterion] = field(default_factory=list)
    strategy: DecisionStrategy = DecisionStrategy.BALANCED
    status: DecisionStatus = DecisionStatus.PENDING
    created_at: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    decided_at: Optional[float] = None
    executed_at: Optional[float] = None
    chosen_option: Optional[Option] = None
    reasoning: str = ""
    
    def add_option(self, option: Option) -> None:
        """Add an option to the decision"""
        self.options.append(option)
    
    def add_criterion(self, criterion: Criterion) -> None:
        """Add a criterion for evaluation"""
        self.criteria.append(criterion)
    
    def evaluate_options(self) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, float]]:
        """Evaluate all options against all criteria"""
        results = {}
        
        for option in self.options:
            scores = {}
            for criterion in self.criteria:
                score = option.get_score(criterion)
                if not criterion.is_maximizing:
                    score = 1.0 - score
                scores[criterion.name] = score * criterion.weight
            results[option.option_id] = scores
        
        return results
    
    def make_decision(self) -> Optional[Option]:
        """Make the optimal decision based on the current strategy"""
        if not self.options or not self.criteria:
            return None
        
        self.status = DecisionStatus.EVALUATING
        
        # Evaluate all options
        evaluations = self.evaluate_options()
        
        # Apply the decision strategy
        if self.strategy == DecisionStrategy.MAXIMIZE_UTILITY:
            best_option = self._maximize_utility()
        elif self.strategy == DecisionStrategy.MINIMIZE_REGRET:
            best_option = self._minimize_regret()
        elif self.strategy == DecisionStrategy.MINIMIZE_RISK:
            best_option = self._minimize_risk()
        elif self.strategy == DecisionStrategy.MAXIMIZE_CONFIDENCE:
            best_option = self._maximize_confidence()
        elif self.strategy == DecisionStrategy.CONSENSUS:
            best_option = self._consensus()
        else:  # BALANCED
            best_option = self._balanced_decision()
        
        if best_option:
            self.chosen_option = best_option
            self.status = DecisionStatus.DECIDED
            self.decided_at = time.time()
            self.reasoning = f"Chose {best_option.description} using {self.strategy.value} strategy"
        
        return best_option
    
    def _maximize_utility(self) -> Optional[Option]:
        """Choose the option with maximum utility"""
        best_option = None
        best_utility = -1.0
        
        for option in self.options:
            utility = option.get_utility(self.criteria)
            if utility > best_utility:
                best_utility = utility
                best_option = option
        
        return best_option
    
    def _minimize_regret(self) -> Optional[Option]:
        """Choose the option that minimizes potential regret"""
        # Calculate regret as the difference between the best possible outcome
        # and the chosen option's outcome
        best_option = None
        min_regret = float('inf')
        
        # Find the best possible score for each criterion
        best_scores = {}
        for criterion in self.criteria:
            best_score = max([o.get_score(criterion) for o in self.options])
            best_scores[criterion.name] = best_score
        
        for option in self.options:
            # Calculate regret for this option
            regret = 0.0
            for criterion in self.criteria:
                option_score = option.get_score(criterion)
                best_score = best_scores[criterion.name]
                
                if criterion.is_maximizing:
                    regret += (best_score - option_score) * criterion.weight
                else:
                    # For minimizing criteria, regret is higher when score is lower
                    regret += (option_score - best_score) * criterion.weight
            
            if regret < min_regret:
                min_regret = regret
                best_option = option
        
        return best_option
    
    def _minimize_risk(self) -> Optional[Option]:
        """Choose the option with the least risk"""
        best_option = None
        min_risk = float('inf')
        
        for option in self.options:
            # Calculate risk score (lower is better)
            risk_score = len(option.risks) * (1.0 - option.probability)
            
            if risk_score < min_risk:
                min_risk = risk_score
                best_option = option
        
        return best_option
    
    def _maximize_confidence(self) -> Optional[Option]:
        """Choose the option with the highest confidence"""
        best_option = None
        best_confidence = -1.0
        
        for option in self.options:
            if option.confidence > best_confidence:
                best_confidence = option.confidence
                best_option = option
        
        return best_option
    
    def _consensus(self) -> Optional[Option]:
        """Choose the option that performs well across all criteria"""
        # Calculate consensus score based on variance across criteria
        best_option = None
        best_consensus = -1.0
        
        for option in self.options:
            # Calculate scores for all criteria
            scores = [option.get_score(c) for c in self.criteria]
            
            if not scores:
                continue
            
            # Calculate mean and variance
            mean_score = sum(scores) / len(scores)
            variance = sum((s - mean_score) ** 2 for s in scores) / len(scores)
            
            # Consensus score: high mean, low variance
            consensus_score = mean_score - variance * 0.5
            
            if consensus_score > best_consensus:
                best_consensus = consensus_score
                best_option = option
        
        return best_option
    
    def _balanced_decision(self) -> Optional[Option]:
        """Make a balanced decision considering multiple factors"""
        best_option = None
        best_score = -1.0
        
        for option in self.options:
            # Calculate utility
            utility = option.get_utility(self.criteria)
            
            # Adjust for confidence
            confidence_factor = option.confidence
            
            # Adjust for cost
            cost_factor = 1.0 / (1.0 + option.cost * 0.05)
            
            # Adjust for probability
            probability_factor = option.probability
            
            # Combined score
            combined_score = utility * confidence_factor * cost_factor * probability_factor
            
            if combined_score > best_score:
                best_score = combined_score
                best_option = option
        
        return best_option
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'decision_id': self.decision_id,
            'description': self.description,
            'context': self.context,
            'options': [o.to_dict() for o in self.options],
            'criteria': [c.to_dict() for c in self.criteria],
            'strategy': self.strategy.value,
            'status': self.status.value,
            'created_at': self.created_at,
            'decided_at': self.decided_at,
            'executed_at': self.executed_at,
            'chosen_option_id': self.chosen_option.option_id if self.chosen_option else None,
            'reasoning': self.reasoning
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'Decision':
        decision = cls(
            decision_id=data.get('decision_id', ''),
            description=data.get('description', ''),
            context=data.get('context', ''),
            options=[Option.from_dict(o) for o in data.get('options', [])],
            criteria=[Criterion.from_dict(c) for c in data.get('criteria', [])],
            strategy=DecisionStrategy(data.get('strategy', 'balanced')),
            status=DecisionStatus(data.get('status', 'pending')),
            created_at=data.get('created_at', time.time()),
            decided_at=data.get('decided_at'),
            executed_at=data.get('executed_at'),
            reasoning=data.get('reasoning', '')
        )
        
        # Set chosen option
        chosen_option_id = data.get('chosen_option_id')
        if chosen_option_id:
            for option in decision.options:
                if option.option_id == chosen_option_id:
                    decision.chosen_option = option
                    break
        
        return decision


class DecisionEngine:
    """
    Engine for making decisions using multiple strategies and criteria.
    
    This engine can:
    - Define and evaluate decision criteria
    - Generate and evaluate options
    - Make optimal decisions based on selected strategies
    - Track decision outcomes and learn from them
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.decisions: Dict[str, Decision] = {}
        self.criteria_library: Dict[str, Criterion] = {}
        self.decision_history: List[str] = []
        self.outcome_history: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
        
        # Initialize with common criteria
        self._initialize_criteria_library()
        
    def _initialize_criteria_library(self) -> None:
        """Initialize the criteria library with common criteria"""
        # Effectiveness criteria
        self.add_criterion(Criterion(
            name="effectiveness",
            description="How well the option achieves the desired outcome",
            weight=1.0,
            is_maximizing=True
        ))
        
        # Efficiency criteria
        self.add_criterion(Criterion(
            name="efficiency",
            description="How efficiently the option achieves the outcome",
            weight=0.8,
            is_maximizing=True
        ))
        
        # Cost criteria (minimizing)
        self.add_criterion(Criterion(
            name="cost",
            description="The cost of executing the option",
            weight=0.7,
            is_maximizing=False
        ))
        
        # Risk criteria (minimizing)
        self.add_criterion(Criterion(
            name="risk",
            description="The risk associated with the option",
            weight=0.8,
            is_maximizing=False
        ))
        
        # Confidence criteria
        self.add_criterion(Criterion(
            name="confidence",
            description="Confidence in the success of the option",
            weight=0.6,
            is_maximizing=True
        ))
        
        # Time criteria (minimizing)
        self.add_criterion(Criterion(
            name="time",
            description="Time required to execute the option",
            weight=0.5,
            is_maximizing=False
        ))
    
    def add_criterion(self, criterion: Criterion) -> None:
        """Add a criterion to the library"""
        self.criteria_library[criterion.name] = criterion
    
    def get_criterion(self, name: str) -> Optional[Criterion]:
        """Get a criterion by name"""
        return self.criteria_library.get(name)
    
    def create_decision(self, description: str, context: str = "",
                        decision_id: Optional[str] = None) -> Decision:
        """Create a new decision"""
        decision_id = decision_id or f"dec_{int(time.time() * 1000)}"
        
        decision = Decision(
            decision_id=decision_id,
            description=description,
            context=context
        )
        
        self.decisions[decision_id] = decision
        return decision
    
    def get_decision(self, decision_id: str) -> Optional[Decision]:
        """Get a decision by ID"""
        return self.decisions.get(decision_id)
    
    def make_decision(self, description: str, options: List[str],
                      context: str = "", strategy: DecisionStrategy = None,
                      criteria_names: List[str] = None) -> Decision:
        """
        Make a decision given a description, options, and context.
        
        This is a high-level interface for decision making.
        """
        # Create decision
        decision = self.create_decision(description, context)
        
        # Add criteria
        if criteria_names:
            for name in criteria_names:
                criterion = self.get_criterion(name)
                if criterion:
                    decision.add_criterion(criterion)
        else:
            # Use default criteria
            for criterion in self.criteria_library.values():
                decision.add_criterion(criterion)
        
        # Add options
        for i, option_desc in enumerate(options):
            option = Option(
                option_id=f"opt_{decision.decision_id}_{i}",
                description=option_desc
            )
            decision.add_option(option)
        
        # Set strategy
        if strategy:
            decision.strategy = strategy
        
        # Make the decision
        decision.make_decision()
        
        return decision
    
    def record_outcome(self, decision_id: str, outcome: str,
                       success: bool = True, metrics: Dict[str, float] = None) -> None:
        """Record the outcome of a decision"""
        if decision_id in self.outcome_history:
            # Update existing
            self.outcome_history[decision_id].update({
                'outcome': outcome,
                'success': success,
                'metrics': metrics or {},
                'timestamp': time.time()
            })
        else:
            # Create new
            self.outcome_history[decision_id] = {
                'outcome': outcome,
                'success': success,
                'metrics': metrics or {},
                'timestamp': time.time()
            }
        
        self.decision_history.append(decision_id)
    
    def learn_from_outcomes(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Learn from recorded decision outcomes to improve future decisions.
        
        This would adjust criteria weights and decision strategies based on
        which decisions led to successful outcomes.
        """
        # Count successful vs failed decisions by strategy
        strategy_success = defaultdict(lambda: {'success': 0, 'total': 0})
        
        for decision_id, outcome in self.outcome_history.items():
            if decision_id in self.decisions:
                decision = self.decisions[decision_id]
                strategy_success[decision.strategy.value]['total'] += 1
                if outcome.get('success', False):
                    strategy_success[decision.strategy.value]['success'] += 1
        
        # Calculate success rates
        strategy_stats = {}
        for strategy, counts in strategy_success.items():
            success_rate = counts['success'] / counts['total'] if counts['total'] > 0 else 0.0
            strategy_stats[strategy] = {
                'success_rate': success_rate,
                'total': counts['total']
            }
        
        return {
            'strategy_stats': strategy_stats,
            'total_decisions': len(self.decision_history),
            'successful_decisions': sum(1 for o in self.outcome_history.values() if o.get('success', False))
        }
    
    def get_best_strategy(self) -> DecisionStrategy:
        """Get the strategy with the highest success rate"""
        stats = self.learn_from_outcomes()
        best_strategy = None
        best_rate = -1.0
        
        for strategy, data in stats['strategy_stats'].items():
            if data['success_rate'] > best_rate:
                best_rate = data['success_rate']
                best_strategy = DecisionStrategy(strategy)
        
        return best_strategy or DecisionStrategy.BALANCED
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Clear all decisions and history"""
        self.decisions.clear()
        self.decision_history.clear()
        self.outcome_history.clear()
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get decision engine statistics"""
        return {
            'total_decisions': len(self.decisions),
            'total_criteria': len(self.criteria_library),
            'decision_history_length': len(self.decision_history),
            'outcome_history_length': len(self.outcome_history),
            'best_strategy': self.get_best_strategy().value
        }

"""
Goal Manager - Manage multiple goals and priorities
"""

from enum import Enum

    PENDING = "pending"
    ACTIVE = "active"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    FAILED = "failed"


@dataclass
class GoalManager:
    pass


# FILE: agi_core/meta_learning/meta_learner.py
"""
Meta-Learner - Learning to Learn

This module provides meta-learning capabilities that allow the AGI to:
- Learn from its own learning process
- Adapt its learning strategies
- Improve its knowledge acquisition efficiency
- Develop better problem-solving approaches
"""

import time
import json
import os
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Callable, Tuple
from collections import defaultdict
import hashlib


@dataclass
class LearningExperience:
    """A record of a learning experience"""
    experience_id: str
    task_description: str
    learning_method: str
    duration: float  # Seconds
    success: bool
    accuracy: float = 0.0  # 0-1
    confidence: float = 0.0  # 0-1
    lessons_learned: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    mistakes_made: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'experience_id': self.experience_id,
            'task_description': self.task_description,
            'learning_method': self.learning_method,
            'duration': self.duration,
            'success': self.success,
            'accuracy': self.accuracy,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'lessons_learned': self.lessons_learned,
            'mistakes_made': self.mistakes_made,
            'timestamp': self.timestamp
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'LearningExperience':
        return cls(
            experience_id=data.get('experience_id', ''),
            task_description=data.get('task_description', ''),
            learning_method=data.get('learning_method', ''),
            duration=data.get('duration', 0.0),
            success=data.get('success', False),
            accuracy=data.get('accuracy', 0.0),
            confidence=data.get('confidence', 0.0),
            lessons_learned=data.get('lessons_learned', []),
            mistakes_made=data.get('mistakes_made', []),
            timestamp=data.get('timestamp', time.time())
        )


@dataclass
class LearningStrategy:
    """A learning strategy with performance metrics"""
    strategy_id: str
    name: str
    description: str
    success_rate: float = 0.0
    avg_accuracy: float = 0.0
    avg_duration: float = 0.0
    use_count: int = 0
    best_for: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    parameters: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    
    def update_from_experience(self, experience: LearningExperience) -> None:
        """Update strategy metrics from a learning experience"""
        self.use_count += 1
        
        # Update success rate
        total_success = self.success_rate * (self.use_count - 1)
        if experience.success:
            total_success += 1
        self.success_rate = total_success / self.use_count
        
        # Update average accuracy
        total_accuracy = self.avg_accuracy * (self.use_count - 1)
        total_accuracy += experience.accuracy
        self.avg_accuracy = total_accuracy / self.use_count
        
        # Update average duration
        total_duration = self.avg_duration * (self.use_count - 1)
        total_duration += experience.duration
        self.avg_duration = total_duration / self.use_count
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'strategy_id': self.strategy_id,
            'name': self.name,
            'description': self.description,
            'success_rate': self.success_rate,
            'avg_accuracy': self.avg_accuracy,
            'avg_duration': self.avg_duration,
            'use_count': self.use_count,
            'best_for': self.best_for,
            'parameters': self.parameters
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'LearningStrategy':
        return cls(
            strategy_id=data.get('strategy_id', ''),
            name=data.get('name', ''),
            description=data.get('description', ''),
            success_rate=data.get('success_rate', 0.0),
            avg_accuracy=data.get('avg_accuracy', 0.0),
            avg_duration=data.get('avg_duration', 0.0),
            use_count=data.get('use_count', 0),
            best_for=data.get('best_for', []),
            parameters=data.get('parameters', {})
        )


@dataclass
class MetaKnowledge:
    """Knowledge about knowledge and learning"""
    known_domains: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    learned_concepts: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    effective_strategies: Dict[str, List[str]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    common_mistakes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    learning_preferences: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'known_domains': self.known_domains,
            'learned_concepts': self.learned_concepts,
            'effective_strategies': self.effective_strategies,
            'common_mistakes': self.common_mistakes,
            'learning_preferences': self.learning_preferences
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'MetaKnowledge':
        return cls(
            known_domains=data.get('known_domains', []),
            learned_concepts=data.get('learned_concepts', []),
            effective_strategies=data.get('effective_strategies', {}),
            common_mistakes=data.get('common_mistakes', []),
            learning_preferences=data.get('learning_preferences', {})
        )


class MetaLearner:
    """
    Meta-learner that learns from the AGI's learning experiences.
    
    This module enables the AGI to:
    - Track learning experiences
    - Evaluate learning strategies
    - Adapt learning approaches based on past performance
    - Identify effective strategies for different task types
    - Avoid repeating mistakes
    """
    
    def __init__(self, storage_path: str = "meta_learner.json"):
        self.storage_path = storage_path
        self.learning_experiences: Dict[str, LearningExperience] = {}
        self.learning_strategies: Dict[str, LearningStrategy] = {}
        self.meta_knowledge = MetaKnowledge()
        self.experience_history: List[str] = []
        
        # Initialize with common learning strategies
        self._initialize_strategies()
        self.load()
        
    def _initialize_strategies(self) -> None:
        """Initialize common learning strategies"""
        strategies = [
            {
                'name': 'memorization',
                'description': 'Memorize facts and information directly',
                'best_for': ['facts', 'definitions', 'simple information']
            },
            {
                'name': 'pattern_recognition',
                'description': 'Identify and learn from patterns in data',
                'best_for': ['sequences', 'repetitive tasks', 'data analysis']
            },
            {
                'name': 'analogical_reasoning',
                'description': 'Learn by drawing analogies to known concepts',
                'best_for': ['new concepts', 'problem solving', 'creative tasks']
            },
            {
                'name': 'deductive_reasoning',
                'description': 'Learn through logical deduction from first principles',
                'best_for': ['mathematics', 'logic', 'structured problems']
            },
            {
                'name': 'inductive_reasoning',
                'description': 'Learn by generalizing from specific examples',
                'best_for': ['hypothesis formation', 'prediction', 'generalization']
            },
            {
                'name': 'experimentation',
                'description': 'Learn through trial and error and experimentation',
                'best_for': ['exploration', 'discovery', 'optimization']
            },
            {
                'name': 'instruction_following',
                'description': 'Learn by following step-by-step instructions',
                'best_for': ['procedures', 'skills', 'step-by-step tasks']
            },
            {
                'name': 'collaborative_learning',
                'description': 'Learn through interaction and collaboration',
                'best_for': ['social learning', 'discussion', 'teamwork']
            }
        ]
        
        for i, strategy_data in enumerate(strategies):
            strategy = LearningStrategy(
                strategy_id=f"strategy_{i}",
                name=strategy_data['name'],
                description=strategy_data['description'],
                best_for=strategy_data['best_for']
            )
            self.learning_strategies[strategy.strategy_id] = strategy
        
    def record_experience(self, task_description: str, learning_method: str,
                          duration: float, success: bool, accuracy: float = 1.0,
                          confidence: float = 1.0, lessons: List[str] = None,
                          mistakes: List[str] = None) -> LearningExperience:
        """Record a learning experience"""
        experience_id = self._generate_id(f"exp_{learning_method}")
        
        experience = LearningExperience(
            experience_id=experience_id,
            task_description=task_description,
            learning_method=learning_method,
            duration=duration,
            success=success,
            accuracy=accuracy,
            confidence=confidence,
            lessons_learned=lessons or [],
            mistakes_made=mistakes or []
        )
        
        self.learning_experiences[experience_id] = experience
        self.experience_history.append(experience_id)
        
        # Update the corresponding strategy
        if learning_method in self.learning_strategies:
            self.learning_strategies[learning_method].update_from_experience(experience)
        
        # Update meta-knowledge
        self._update_meta_knowledge(experience)
        
        return experience
    
    def _update_meta_knowledge(self, experience: LearningExperience) -> None:
        """Update meta-knowledge based on a learning experience"""
        # Track learned concepts from task descriptions
        words = experience.task_description.lower().split()
        for word in words:
            if len(word) > 4 and word not in self.meta_knowledge.learned_concepts:
                self.meta_knowledge.learned_concepts.append(word)
        
        # Track mistakes
        for mistake in experience.mistakes_made:
            if mistake not in self.meta_knowledge.common_mistakes:
                self.meta_knowledge.common_mistakes.append(mistake)
        
        # Update effective strategies
        if experience.success:
            task_type = self._infer_task_type(experience.task_description)
            if task_type not in self.meta_knowledge.effective_strategies:
                self.meta_knowledge.effective_strategies[task_type] = []
            if experience.learning_method not in self.meta_knowledge.effective_strategies[task_type]:
                self.meta_knowledge.effective_strategies[task_type].append(experience.learning_method)
    
    def _infer_task_type(self, task_description: str) -> str:
        """Infer the type of task from its description"""
        task_lower = task_description.lower()
        
        if any(word in task_lower for word in ['fact', 'definition', 'remember', 'memorize']):
            return 'memorization'
        elif any(word in task_lower for word in ['pattern', 'sequence', 'repeat']):
            return 'pattern_recognition'
        elif any(word in task_lower for word in ['analogy', 'similar', 'like']):
            return 'analogical_reasoning'
        elif any(word in task_lower for word in ['logic', 'deduce', 'prove', 'math']):
            return 'deductive_reasoning'
        elif any(word in task_lower for word in ['generalize', 'predict', 'hypothesis']):
            return 'inductive_reasoning'
        elif any(word in task_lower for word in ['try', 'experiment', 'test']):
            return 'experimentation'
        elif any(word in task_lower for word in ['instruct', 'follow', 'step']):
            return 'instruction_following'
        else:
            return 'general'
    
    def get_best_strategy(self, task_description: str = None) -> Optional[LearningStrategy]:
        """Get the best learning strategy for a given task"""
        if task_description:
            task_type = self._infer_task_type(task_description)
            
            # Check if we have effective strategies for this task type
            if task_type in self.meta_knowledge.effective_strategies:
                strategy_names = self.meta_knowledge.effective_strategies[task_type]
                best_strategy = None
                best_score = -1.0
                
                for strategy_name in strategy_names:
                    for strategy in self.learning_strategies.values():
                        if strategy.name == strategy_name:
                            # Score based on success rate and relevance
                            score = strategy.success_rate * 0.7 + strategy.avg_accuracy * 0.3
                            if score > best_score:
                                best_score = score
                                best_strategy = strategy
                
                if best_strategy:
                    return best_strategy
        
        # Fall back to the overall best strategy
        return self.get_overall_best_strategy()
    
    def get_overall_best_strategy(self) -> Optional[LearningStrategy]:
        """Get the overall best learning strategy"""
        best_strategy = None
        best_score = -1.0
        
        for strategy in self.learning_strategies.values():
            if strategy.use_count == 0:
                continue
            score = strategy.success_rate * 0.7 + strategy.avg_accuracy * 0.3
            if score > best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_strategy = strategy
        
        return best_strategy
    
    def get_strategy_recommendations(self, task_description: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Get recommendations for learning strategies for a task"""
        recommendations = []
        
        # Get the best strategy
        best = self.get_best_strategy(task_description)
        if best:
            recommendations.append({
                'strategy': best.name,
                'description': best.description,
                'success_rate': best.success_rate,
                'avg_accuracy': best.avg_accuracy,
                'reason': 'Best match for task type'
            })
        
        # Add alternative strategies
        task_type = self._infer_task_type(task_description) if task_description else 'general'
        for strategy in self.learning_strategies.values():
            if strategy.name != best.name and strategy.use_count > 0:
                if task_type in strategy.best_for or 'general' in strategy.best_for:
                    recommendations.append({
                        'strategy': strategy.name,
                        'description': strategy.description,
                        'success_rate': strategy.success_rate,
                        'avg_accuracy': strategy.avg_accuracy,
                        'reason': 'Alternative approach'
                    })
        
        # Sort by success rate
        recommendations.sort(key=lambda x: x['success_rate'], reverse=True)
        
        return recommendations[:3]  # Return top 3
    
    def learn_from_mistakes(self) -> List[str]:
        """Analyze common mistakes and suggest improvements"""
        suggestions = []
        
        # Count mistake frequency
        mistake_counts = defaultdict(int)
        for experience in self.learning_experiences.values():
            for mistake in experience.mistakes_made:
                mistake_counts[mistake] += 1
        
        # Generate suggestions for frequent mistakes
        for mistake, count in mistake_counts.items():
            if count > 1:  # Only suggest for repeated mistakes
                suggestions.append(f"Avoid: {mistake} (occurred {count} times)")
        
        return suggestions
    
    def get_learning_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get statistics about learning experiences"""
        total_experiences = len(self.learning_experiences)
        successful = sum(1 for e in self.learning_experiences.values() if e.success)
        
        return {
            'total_experiences': total_experiences,
            'success_rate': successful / total_experiences if total_experiences > 0 else 0.0,
            'total_strategies': len(self.learning_strategies),
            'learned_concepts': len(self.meta_knowledge.learned_concepts),
            'common_mistakes': len(self.meta_knowledge.common_mistakes),
            'best_strategy': self.get_overall_best_strategy().name if self.get_overall_best_strategy() else None
        }
    
    def _generate_id(self, prefix: str) -> str:
        """Generate a unique ID"""
        timestamp = int(time.time() * 1000)
        random_part = hashlib.md5(str(timestamp).encode()).hexdigest()[:4]
        return f"{prefix}_{timestamp}_{random_part}"
    
    def save(self) -> None:
        """Save meta-learner state to file"""
        state = {
            'learning_experiences': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.learning_experiences.items()},
            'learning_strategies': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.learning_strategies.items()},
            'meta_knowledge': self.meta_knowledge.to_dict(),
            'experience_history': self.experience_history
        }
        
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.storage_path), exist_ok=True)
        with open(self.storage_path, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(state, f, indent=2)
    
    def load(self) -> bool:
        """Load meta-learner state from file"""
        try:
            if os.path.exists(self.storage_path):
                with open(self.storage_path, 'r') as f:
                    state = json.load(f)
                
                self.learning_experiences = {
                    k: LearningExperience.from_dict(v) for k, v in state.get('learning_experiences', {}).items()
                }
                self.learning_strategies = {
                    k: LearningStrategy.from_dict(v) for k, v in state.get('learning_strategies', {}).items()
                }
                self.meta_knowledge = MetaKnowledge.from_dict(state.get('meta_knowledge', {}))
                self.experience_history = state.get('experience_history', [])
                
                return True
        except FileNotFoundError:
            pass
        return False
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Clear all learning data"""
        self.learning_experiences.clear()
        self.experience_history.clear()
        self.meta_knowledge = MetaKnowledge()
