# FILE: agi_core/memory/episodic_memory.py
"""
Episodic Memory - Experience and Event Storage

This module implements the AGI's episodic memory system, which stores
personal experiences, events, and temporal sequences.
"""

import time
import json
import os
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Tuple
from collections import defaultdict
import hashlib


@dataclass
class Experience:
    """A single experience or event in episodic memory"""
    experience_id: str
    title: str
    description: str
    timestamp: float
    duration: float = 0.0  # Duration in seconds
    location: str = ""
    participants: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    emotions: Dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)  # emotion -> intensity (0-1)
    tags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    related_experiences: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    importance: float = 0.5  # 0-1
    confidence: float = 1.0
    access_count: int = 0
    last_accessed: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    
    def access(self) -> None:
        """Record an access to this experience"""
        self.access_count += 1
        self.last_accessed = time.time()
    
    def add_participant(self, participant: str) -> None:
        """Add a participant to this experience"""
        if participant not in self.participants:
            self.participants.append(participant)
    
    def add_emotion(self, emotion: str, intensity: float) -> None:
        """Add or update an emotion for this experience"""
        self.emotions[emotion] = max(0.0, min(1.0, intensity))
    
    def add_tag(self, tag: str) -> None:
        """Add a tag to this experience"""
        if tag not in self.tags:
            self.tags.append(tag)
    
    def add_related_experience(self, experience_id: str) -> None:
        """Add a related experience"""
        if experience_id not in self.related_experiences:
            self.related_experiences.append(experience_id)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'experience_id': self.experience_id,
            'title': self.title,
            'description': self.description,
            'timestamp': self.timestamp,
            'duration': self.duration,
            'location': self.location,
            'participants': self.participants,
            'emotions': self.emotions,
            'tags': self.tags,
            'related_experiences': self.related_experiences,
            'importance': self.importance,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'access_count': self.access_count,
            'last_accessed': self.last_accessed
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'Experience':
        return cls(
            experience_id=data.get('experience_id', ''),
            title=data.get('title', ''),
            description=data.get('description', ''),
            timestamp=data.get('timestamp', time.time()),
            duration=data.get('duration', 0.0),
            location=data.get('location', ''),
            participants=data.get('participants', []),
            emotions=data.get('emotions', {}),
            tags=data.get('tags', []),
            related_experiences=data.get('related_experiences', []),
            importance=data.get('importance', 0.5),
            confidence=data.get('confidence', 1.0),
            access_count=data.get('access_count', 0),
            last_accessed=data.get('last_accessed', time.time())
        )


@dataclass
class EventSequence:
    """A sequence of related events"""
    sequence_id: str
    name: str
    events: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # List of experience_ids
    start_time: float = 0.0
    end_time: float = 0.0
    
    def add_event(self, experience_id: str) -> None:
        """Add an experience to the sequence"""
        if experience_id not in self.events:
            self.events.append(experience_id)
    
    def get_duration(self) -> float:
        """Get the duration of the sequence"""
        return self.end_time - self.start_time
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'sequence_id': self.sequence_id,
            'name': self.name,
            'events': self.events,
            'start_time': self.start_time,
            'end_time': self.end_time
        }


class EpisodicMemory:
    """
    Episodic memory - stores personal experiences and events.
    
    Characteristics:
    - Temporal organization
    - Personal experiences
    - Emotional context
    - Spatial context
    - Event sequences
    """
    
    def __init__(self, storage_path: str = "episodic_memory.json"):
        self.storage_path = storage_path
        self.experiences: Dict[str, Experience] = {}
        self.sequences: Dict[str, EventSequence] = {}
        
        # Indexes for fast lookup
        self.tag_index: Dict[str, List[str]] = defaultdict(list)
        self.time_index: Dict[float, str] = {}
        self.participant_index: Dict[str, List[str]] = defaultdict(list)
        self.location_index: Dict[str, List[str]] = defaultdict(list)
        
        self.load()
        
    def add_experience(self, title: str, description: str,
                       experience_id: Optional[str] = None,
                       timestamp: Optional[float] = None) -> Experience:
        """Add an experience to episodic memory"""
        timestamp = timestamp or time.time()
        experience_id = experience_id or self._generate_id(f"exp_{title}")
        
        experience = Experience(
            experience_id=experience_id,
            title=title,
            description=description,
            timestamp=timestamp
        )
        
        self.experiences[experience_id] = experience
        self.time_index[timestamp] = experience_id
        
        return experience
    
    def get_experience(self, experience_id: str) -> Optional[Experience]:
        """Get an experience by ID"""
        return self.experiences.get(experience_id)
    
    def update_experience(self, experience_id: str, **kwargs) -> bool:
        """Update an experience"""
        if experience_id in self.experiences:
            experience = self.experiences[experience_id]
            for key, value in kwargs.items():
                if hasattr(experience, key):
                    setattr(experience, key, value)
            return True
        return False
    
    def add_experience_to_sequence(self, sequence_name: str, 
                                   experience_id: str) -> EventSequence:
        """Add an experience to a sequence"""
        # Find or create sequence
        sequence_id = self._generate_id(f"seq_{sequence_name}")
        
        if sequence_id not in self.sequences:
            self.sequences[sequence_id] = EventSequence(
                sequence_id=sequence_id,
                name=sequence_name
            )
        
        sequence = self.sequences[sequence_id]
        sequence.add_event(experience_id)
        
        # Update sequence timestamps
        experience = self.experiences.get(experience_id)
        if experience:
            if sequence.start_time == 0 or experience.timestamp < sequence.start_time:
                sequence.start_time = experience.timestamp
            if experience.timestamp > sequence.end_time:
                sequence.end_time = experience.timestamp
        
        return sequence
    
    def get_experiences_by_tag(self, tag: str) -> List[Experience]:
        """Get experiences with a specific tag"""
        experience_ids = self.tag_index.get(tag, [])
        return [self.experiences.get(eid) for eid in experience_ids if eid in self.experiences]
    
    def get_experiences_by_participant(self, participant: str) -> List[Experience]:
        """Get experiences with a specific participant"""
        experience_ids = self.participant_index.get(participant.lower(), [])
        return [self.experiences.get(eid) for eid in experience_ids if eid in self.experiences]
    
    def get_experiences_by_location(self, location: str) -> List[Experience]:
        """Get experiences at a specific location"""
        experience_ids = self.location_index.get(location.lower(), [])
        return [self.experiences.get(eid) for eid in experience_ids if eid in self.experiences]
    
    def get_experiences_in_time_range(self, start: float, end: float) -> List[Experience]:
        """Get experiences within a time range"""
        results = []
        for experience in self.experiences.values():
            if start <= experience.timestamp <= end:
                results.append(experience)
        results.sort(key=lambda x: x.timestamp, reverse=True)
        return results
    
    def get_recent_experiences(self, n: int = 10) -> List[Experience]:
        """Get most recent experiences"""
        sorted_experiences = sorted(self.experiences.values(), 
                                     key=lambda x: x.timestamp, 
                                     reverse=True)
        return sorted_experiences[:n]
    
    def get_important_experiences(self, n: int = 10) -> List[Experience]:
        """Get most important experiences"""
        sorted_experiences = sorted(self.experiences.values(),
                                     key=lambda x: (x.importance, x.access_count),
                                     reverse=True)
        return sorted_experiences[:n]
    
    def search_experiences(self, query: str, limit: int = 10) -> List[Experience]:
        """Search experiences by content"""
        query_lower = query.lower()
        results = []
        
        for experience in self.experiences.values():
            if (query_lower in experience.title.lower() or
                query_lower in experience.description.lower()):
                results.append(experience)
        
        results.sort(key=lambda x: (x.importance, x.access_count), reverse=True)
        return results[:limit]
    
    def _generate_id(self, prefix: str) -> str:
        """Generate a unique ID"""
        timestamp = int(time.time() * 1000)
        random_part = hashlib.md5(str(timestamp).encode()).hexdigest()[:4]
        return f"{prefix}_{timestamp}_{random_part}"
    
    def _update_indexes(self, experience: Experience) -> None:
        """Update indexes for an experience"""
        for tag in experience.tags:
            if experience.experience_id not in self.tag_index[tag]:
                self.tag_index[tag].append(experience.experience_id)
        
        for participant in experience.participants:
            if experience.experience_id not in self.participant_index[participant.lower()]:
                self.participant_index[participant.lower()].append(experience.experience_id)
        
        if experience.location:
            if experience.experience_id not in self.location_index[experience.location.lower()]:
                self.location_index[experience.location.lower()].append(experience.experience_id)
    
    def save(self) -> None:
        """Save episodic memory to file"""
        state = {
            'experiences': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.experiences.items()},
            'sequences': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.sequences.items()}
        }
        
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.storage_path), exist_ok=True)
        with open(self.storage_path, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(state, f, indent=2)
    
    def load(self) -> bool:
        """Load episodic memory from file"""
        try:
            if os.path.exists(self.storage_path):
                with open(self.storage_path, 'r') as f:
                    state = json.load(f)
                
                self.experiences = {k: Experience.from_dict(v) for k, v in state.get('experiences', {}).items()}
                self.sequences = {k: EventSequence.from_dict(v) for k, v in state.get('sequences', {}).items()}
                
                # Rebuild indexes
                for experience in self.experiences.values():
                    self.time_index[experience.timestamp] = experience.experience_id
                    self._update_indexes(experience)
                
                return True
        except FileNotFoundError:
            pass
        return False
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Clear all episodic memory"""
        self.experiences.clear()
        self.sequences.clear()
        self.tag_index.clear()
        self.time_index.clear()
        self.participant_index.clear()
        self.location_index.clear()
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get memory statistics"""
        return {
            'total_experiences': len(self.experiences),
            'total_sequences': len(self.sequences),
            'total_tags': len(self.tag_index),
            'total_participants': len(self.participant_index),
            'total_locations': len(self.location_index),
            'most_accessed': max(self.experiences.values(), key=lambda x: x.access_count).title if self.experiences else None
        }


# FILE: agi_core/memory/short_term_memory.py
"""
Short-Term Memory - Working Memory and Immediate Context

This module implements the AGI's short-term memory system, which holds
information currently being processed and provides fast access to recent context.
"""

import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Set
from collections import OrderedDict
import json


@dataclass
class MemoryItem:
    """A single item in short-term memory"""
    content: Any
    memory_type: str = "fact"  # fact, observation, hypothesis, question, intention
    confidence: float = 1.0
    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    access_count: int = 0
    last_accessed: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    tags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    
    def access(self) -> None:
        """Record an access to this memory item"""
        self.access_count += 1
        self.last_accessed = time.time()
    
    def is_recent(self, threshold_seconds: float = 300) -> bool:
        """Check if this item was accessed recently"""
        return time.time() - self.last_accessed < threshold_seconds
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'content': self.content,
            'memory_type': self.memory_type,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'timestamp': self.timestamp,
            'access_count': self.access_count,
            'last_accessed': self.last_accessed,
            'tags': self.tags
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'MemoryItem':
        return cls(
            content=data.get('content'),
            memory_type=data.get('memory_type', 'fact'),
            confidence=data.get('confidence', 1.0),
            timestamp=data.get('timestamp', time.time()),
            access_count=data.get('access_count', 0),
            last_accessed=data.get('last_accessed', time.time()),
            tags=data.get('tags', [])
        )


@dataclass
class ContextWindow:
    """A contextual window of related memory items"""
    window_id: str
    items: List[MemoryItem] = field(default_factory=list)
    focus: str = ""  # Current focus of attention
    recency_score: float = 1.0
    relevance_score: float = 1.0
    
    def add_item(self, item: MemoryItem) -> None:
        """Add an item to the context window"""
        self.items.append(item)
    
    def get_recent_items(self, n: int = 5) -> List[MemoryItem]:
        """Get the most recently accessed items"""
        return sorted(self.items, key=lambda x: x.last_accessed, reverse=True)[:n]
    
    def get_by_tag(self, tag: str) -> List[MemoryItem]:
        """Get items with a specific tag"""
        return [item for item in self.items if tag in item.tags]
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'window_id': self.window_id,
            'items': [item.to_dict() for item in self.items],
            'focus': self.focus,
            'recency_score': self.recency_score,
            'relevance_score': self.relevance_score
        }


class ShortTermMemory:
    """
    Short-term memory system with limited capacity.
    
    Characteristics:
    - Limited capacity (typically 7 +/- 2 items for humans, more for AGI)
    - Fast access
    - Decay over time
    - Context-based organization
    """
    
    def __init__(self, max_items: int = 50, decay_rate: float = 0.01,
                 max_context_windows: int = 5):
        self.max_items = max_items
        self.decay_rate = decay_rate
        self.max_context_windows = max_context_windows
        
        self.items: OrderedDict[str, MemoryItem] = OrderedDict()
        self.context_windows: Dict[str, ContextWindow] = {}
        self.current_window: Optional[ContextWindow] = None
        self.access_history: List[str] = []
        
    def add(self, content: Any, memory_type: str = "fact",
            confidence: float = 1.0, tags: List[str] = None) -> str:
        """Add an item to short-term memory"""
        # Generate a unique ID
        item_id = f"stm_{int(time.time() * 1000)}_{len(self.items)}"
        
        item = MemoryItem(
            content=content,
            memory_type=memory_type,
            confidence=confidence,
            tags=tags or []
        )
        
        # If at capacity, remove oldest
        while len(self.items) >= self.max_items:
            self.items.popitem(last=False)
        
        self.items[item_id] = item
        
        # Add to current context window if exists
        if self.current_window:
            self.current_window.add_item(item)
        
        return item_id
    
    def get(self, item_id: str) -> Optional[MemoryItem]:
        """Get an item from memory"""
        if item_id in self.items:
            item = self.items[item_id]
            item.access()
            self.access_history.append(item_id)
            return item
        return None
    
    def remove(self, item_id: str) -> bool:
        """Remove an item from memory"""
        if item_id in self.items:
            del self.items[item_id]
            return True
        return False
    
    def search(self, query: str, limit: int = 10) -> List[MemoryItem]:
        """Search memory for items matching query"""
        results = []
        query_lower = query.lower()
        
        for item_id, item in self.items.items():
            # Simple string matching
            content_str = str(item.content).lower()
            if query_lower in content_str:
                results.append(item)
            
            # Check tags
            for tag in item.tags:
                if query_lower in tag.lower():
                    results.append(item)
                    break
        
        # Sort by recency and relevance
        results.sort(key=lambda x: (x.last_accessed, x.access_count), reverse=True)
        return results[:limit]
    
    def create_context_window(self, window_id: str = None, 
                              focus: str = "") -> ContextWindow:
        """Create a new context window"""
        window_id = window_id or f"ctx_{int(time.time() * 1000)}"
        
        # Clean up old windows if at capacity
        if len(self.context_windows) >= self.max_context_windows:
            # Remove oldest window
            oldest_id = min(self.context_windows.keys(), 
                          key=lambda x: min([item.timestamp for item in self.context_windows[x].items]) if self.context_windows[x].items else float('inf'))
            del self.context_windows[oldest_id]
        
        window = ContextWindow(window_id=window_id, focus=focus)
        self.context_windows[window_id] = window
        self.current_window = window
        return window
    
    def set_current_window(self, window_id: str) -> bool:
        """Set the current active context window"""
        if window_id in self.context_windows:
            self.current_window = self.context_windows[window_id]
            return True
        return False
    
    def decay_memory(self) -> None:
        """Apply decay to memory items"""
        current_time = time.time()
        items_to_remove = []
        
        for item_id, item in self.items.items():
            # Decay confidence over time
            age = current_time - item.timestamp
            decay_factor = 1.0 - (self.decay_rate * age)
            item.confidence = max(0.1, item.confidence * decay_factor)
            
            # Remove if confidence is too low or too old
            if item.confidence < 0.2:
                items_to_remove.append(item_id)
            elif age > 3600:  # 1 hour
                items_to_remove.append(item_id)
        
        for item_id in items_to_remove:
            self.remove(item_id)
    
    def get_recent(self, n: int = 10) -> List[MemoryItem]:
        """Get most recently accessed items"""
        return sorted(self.items.values(), 
                     key=lambda x: x.last_accessed, 
                     reverse=True)[:n]
    
    def get_by_type(self, memory_type: str) -> List[MemoryItem]:
        """Get items of a specific type"""
        return [item for item in self.items.values() 
                if item.memory_type == memory_type]
    
    def get_by_tag(self, tag: str) -> List[MemoryItem]:
        """Get items with a specific tag"""
        return [item for item in self.items.values() if tag in item.tags]
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Clear all short-term memory"""
        self.items.clear()
        self.context_windows.clear()
        self.current_window = None
        self.access_history.clear()
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get memory statistics"""
        return {
            'total_items': len(self.items),
            'max_capacity': self.max_items,
            'context_windows': len(self.context_windows),
            'access_history_length': len(self.access_history),
            'memory_types': {item.memory_type: len([i for i in self.items.values() if i.memory_type == item.memory_type]) 
                           for item in self.items.values()}
        }


class WorkingMemory:
    """
    Working memory - a specialized form of short-term memory
    that holds information currently being manipulated.
    
    This is the "workspace" where active thinking happens.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, capacity: int = 7):
        self.capacity = capacity
        self.items: List[MemoryItem] = []
        self.focus_stack: List[str] = []  # Stack of current foci
        
    def add(self, content: Any, memory_type: str = "working",
            confidence: float = 1.0) -> bool:
        """Add an item to working memory"""
        if len(self.items) >= self.capacity:
            # Remove least recently used
            self.items.pop(0)
        
        item = MemoryItem(
            content=content,
            memory_type=memory_type,
            confidence=confidence
        )
        self.items.append(item)
        return True
    
    def push_focus(self, focus: str) -> None:
        """Push a new focus onto the stack"""
        self.focus_stack.append(focus)
    
    def pop_focus(self) -> Optional[str]:
        """Pop the current focus from the stack"""
        if self.focus_stack:
            return self.focus_stack.pop()
        return None
    
    def get_current_focus(self) -> Optional[str]:
        """Get the current focus"""
        if self.focus_stack:
            return self.focus_stack[-1]
        return None
    
    def get_item(self, index: int) -> Optional[MemoryItem]:
        """Get item by index"""
        if 0 <= index < len(self.items):
            return self.items[index]
        return None
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Clear working memory"""
        self.items.clear()
        self.focus_stack.clear()
    
    def get_contents(self) -> List[Any]:
        """Get all contents in working memory"""
        return [item.content for item in self.items]


# FILE: agi_core/memory/long_term_memory.py
"""
Long-Term Memory - Semantic and Structural Knowledge Storage

This module implements the AGI's long-term memory system, which stores
knowledge, concepts, and relationships in a structured format.
"""

import time
import json
import os
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Set, Tuple
from collections import defaultdict
import hashlib


@dataclass
class Concept:
    """A concept stored in semantic memory"""
    concept_id: str
    name: str
    definition: str
    examples: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    properties: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    related_concepts: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    confidence: float = 1.0
    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    last_accessed: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    access_count: int = 0
    
    def add_example(self, example: str) -> None:
        """Add an example of this concept"""
        if example not in self.examples:
            self.examples.append(example)
    
    def add_property(self, name: str, value: Any) -> None:
        """Add a property to this concept"""
        self.properties[name] = value
    
    def add_related_concept(self, concept_id: str) -> None:
        """Add a related concept"""
        if concept_id not in self.related_concepts:
            self.related_concepts.append(concept_id)
    
    def access(self) -> None:
        """Record an access to this concept"""
        self.access_count += 1
        self.last_accessed = time.time()
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'concept_id': self.concept_id,
            'name': self.name,
            'definition': self.definition,
            'examples': self.examples,
            'properties': self.properties,
            'related_concepts': self.related_concepts,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'timestamp': self.timestamp,
            'last_accessed': self.last_accessed,
            'access_count': self.access_count
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'Concept':
        return cls(
            concept_id=data.get('concept_id', ''),
            name=data.get('name', ''),
            definition=data.get('definition', ''),
            examples=data.get('examples', []),
            properties=data.get('properties', {}),
            related_concepts=data.get('related_concepts', []),
            confidence=data.get('confidence', 1.0),
            timestamp=data.get('timestamp', time.time()),
            last_accessed=data.get('last_accessed', time.time()),
            access_count=data.get('access_count', 0)
        )


@dataclass
class Fact:
    """A factual statement stored in memory"""
    fact_id: str
    statement: str
    subject: str
    predicate: str
    object: str
    confidence: float = 1.0
    source: str = ""
    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    last_accessed: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    access_count: int = 0
    
    def access(self) -> None:
        """Record an access to this fact"""
        self.access_count += 1
        self.last_accessed = time.time()
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'fact_id': self.fact_id,
            'statement': self.statement,
            'subject': self.subject,
            'predicate': self.predicate,
            'object': self.object,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'source': self.source,
            'timestamp': self.timestamp,
            'last_accessed': self.last_accessed,
            'access_count': self.access_count
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'Fact':
        return cls(
            fact_id=data.get('fact_id', ''),
            statement=data.get('statement', ''),
            subject=data.get('subject', ''),
            predicate=data.get('predicate', ''),
            object=data.get('object', ''),
            confidence=data.get('confidence', 1.0),
            source=data.get('source', ''),
            timestamp=data.get('timestamp', time.time()),
            last_accessed=data.get('last_accessed', time.time()),
            access_count=data.get('access_count', 0)
        )


@dataclass
class Procedure:
    """A procedure or skill stored in memory"""
    procedure_id: str
    name: str
    description: str
    steps: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    parameters: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    preconditions: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    postconditions: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    confidence: float = 1.0
    success_count: int = 0
    failure_count: int = 0
    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)
    
    def add_step(self, step: str) -> None:
        """Add a step to the procedure"""
        self.steps.append(step)
    
    def record_success(self) -> None:
        """Record a successful execution"""
        self.success_count += 1
    
    def record_failure(self) -> None:
        """Record a failed execution"""
        self.failure_count += 1
    
    def get_success_rate(self) -> float:
        """Get the success rate of this procedure"""
        total = self.success_count + self.failure_count
        if total == 0:
            return 0.0
        return self.success_count / total
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'procedure_id': self.procedure_id,
            'name': self.name,
            'description': self.description,
            'steps': self.steps,
            'parameters': self.parameters,
            'preconditions': self.preconditions,
            'postconditions': self.postconditions,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'success_count': self.success_count,
            'failure_count': self.failure_count,
            'timestamp': self.timestamp
        }
    
    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> 'Procedure':
        return cls(
            procedure_id=data.get('procedure_id', ''),
            name=data.get('name', ''),
            description=data.get('description', ''),
            steps=data.get('steps', []),
            parameters=data.get('parameters', {}),
            preconditions=data.get('preconditions', []),
            postconditions=data.get('postconditions', []),
            confidence=data.get('confidence', 1.0),
            success_count=data.get('success_count', 0),
            failure_count=data.get('failure_count', 0),
            timestamp=data.get('timestamp', time.time())
        )


class SemanticMemory:
    """
    Semantic memory - stores concepts, facts, and relationships.
    
    Characteristics:
    - Structured knowledge storage
    - Concepts with definitions and examples
    - Facts as subject-predicate-object triples
    - Procedures and skills
    - Persistent storage
    """
    
    def __init__(self, storage_path: str = "semantic_memory.json"):
        self.storage_path = storage_path
        self.concepts: Dict[str, Concept] = {}
        self.facts: Dict[str, Fact] = {}
        self.procedures: Dict[str, Procedure] = {}
        
        # Indexes for fast lookup
        self.concept_name_index: Dict[str, str] = {}
        self.fact_index: Dict[Tuple[str, str, str], str] = {}
        self.procedure_name_index: Dict[str, str] = {}
        
        self.load()
        
    def add_concept(self, name: str, definition: str, 
                    concept_id: Optional[str] = None) -> Concept:
        """Add a concept to semantic memory"""
        concept_id = concept_id or self._generate_id(f"concept_{name}")
        
        concept = Concept(
            concept_id=concept_id,
            name=name,
            definition=definition
        )
        
        self.concepts[concept_id] = concept
        self.concept_name_index[name.lower()] = concept_id
        
        return concept
    
    def get_concept(self, concept_id: str) -> Optional[Concept]:
        """Get a concept by ID"""
        return self.concepts.get(concept_id)
    
    def get_concept_by_name(self, name: str) -> Optional[Concept]:
        """Get a concept by name"""
        concept_id = self.concept_name_index.get(name.lower())
        if concept_id:
            return self.concepts.get(concept_id)
        return None
    
    def add_fact(self, statement: str, subject: str, predicate: str, 
                 obj: str, source: str = "", fact_id: Optional[str] = None) -> Fact:
        """Add a fact to semantic memory"""
        fact_id = fact_id or self._generate_id(f"fact_{subject}_{predicate}_{obj}")
        
        fact = Fact(
            fact_id=fact_id,
            statement=statement,
            subject=subject,
            predicate=predicate,
            object=obj,
            source=source
        )
        
        self.facts[fact_id] = fact
        self.fact_index[(subject, predicate, obj)] = fact_id
        
        return fact
    
    def get_fact(self, fact_id: str) -> Optional[Fact]:
        """Get a fact by ID"""
        return self.facts.get(fact_id)
    
    def get_facts_about(self, subject: str) -> List[Fact]:
        """Get all facts about a subject"""
        results = []
        for fact in self.facts.values():
            if fact.subject == subject:
                results.append(fact)
        return results
    
    def add_procedure(self, name: str, description: str,
                      procedure_id: Optional[str] = None) -> Procedure:
        """Add a procedure to semantic memory"""
        procedure_id = procedure_id or self._generate_id(f"proc_{name}")
        
        procedure = Procedure(
            procedure_id=procedure_id,
            name=name,
            description=description
        )
        
        self.procedures[procedure_id] = procedure
        self.procedure_name_index[name.lower()] = procedure_id
        
        return procedure
    
    def get_procedure(self, procedure_id: str) -> Optional[Procedure]:
        """Get a procedure by ID"""
        return self.procedures.get(procedure_id)
    
    def get_procedure_by_name(self, name: str) -> Optional[Procedure]:
        """Get a procedure by name"""
        procedure_id = self.procedure_name_index.get(name.lower())
        if procedure_id:
            return self.procedures.get(procedure_id)
        return None
    
    def search_concepts(self, query: str, limit: int = 10) -> List[Concept]:
        """Search concepts by name or definition"""
        query_lower = query.lower()
        results = []
        
        for concept in self.concepts.values():
            if (query_lower in concept.name.lower() or 
                query_lower in concept.definition.lower()):
                results.append(concept)
        
        results.sort(key=lambda x: x.access_count, reverse=True)
        return results[:limit]
    
    def search_facts(self, query: str, limit: int = 10) -> List[Fact]:
        """Search facts by content"""
        query_lower = query.lower()
        results = []
        
        for fact in self.facts.values():
            if (query_lower in fact.statement.lower() or
                query_lower in fact.subject.lower() or
                query_lower in fact.object.lower()):
                results.append(fact)
        
        results.sort(key=lambda x: x.access_count, reverse=True)
        return results[:limit]
    
    def _generate_id(self, prefix: str) -> str:
        """Generate a unique ID"""
        timestamp = int(time.time() * 1000)
        random_part = hashlib.md5(str(timestamp).encode()).hexdigest()[:4]
        return f"{prefix}_{timestamp}_{random_part}"
    
    def save(self) -> None:
        """Save semantic memory to file"""
        state = {
            'concepts': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.concepts.items()},
            'facts': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.facts.items()},
            'procedures': {k: v.to_dict() for k, v in self.procedures.items()}
        }
        
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.storage_path), exist_ok=True)
        with open(self.storage_path, 'w') as f:
            json.dump(state, f, indent=2)
    
    def load(self) -> bool:
        """Load semantic memory from file"""
        try:
            if os.path.exists(self.storage_path):
                with open(self.storage_path, 'r') as f:
                    state = json.load(f)
                
                self.concepts = {k: Concept.from_dict(v) for k, v in state.get('concepts', {}).items()}
                self.facts = {k: Fact.from_dict(v) for k, v in state.get('facts', {}).items()}
                self.procedures = {k: Procedure.from_dict(v) for k, v in state.get('procedures', {}).items()}
                
                # Rebuild indexes
                for concept_id, concept in self.concepts.items():
                    self.concept_name_index[concept.name.lower()] = concept_id
                
                for fact_id, fact in self.facts.items():
                    self.fact_index[(fact.subject, fact.predicate, fact.object)] = fact_id
                
                for proc_id, proc in self.procedures.items():
                    self.procedure_name_index[proc.name.lower()] = proc_id
                
                return True
        except FileNotFoundError:
            pass
        return False
    
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Clear all semantic memory"""
        self.concepts.clear()
        self.facts.clear()
        self.procedures.clear()
        self.concept_name_index.clear()
        self.fact_index.clear()
        self.procedure_name_index.clear()
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get memory statistics"""
        return {
            'total_concepts': len(self.concepts),
            'total_facts': len(self.facts),
            'total_procedures': len(self.procedures),
            'most_accessed_concept': max(self.concepts.values(), key=lambda x: x.access_count).name if self.concepts else None,
            'most_accessed_fact': max(self.facts.values(), key=lambda x: x.access_count).statement if self.facts else None
        }


class LongTermMemory:
    """
    Long-term memory system combining semantic and episodic memory.
    
    This is the main interface for long-term memory operations.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, storage_path: str = "semantic_memory.json"):
        self.semantic_memory = SemanticMemory(storage_path=storage_path)
        # Episodic memory is handled separately
        
    def store_concept(self, name: str, definition: str) -> Concept:
        """Store a concept in long-term memory"""
        return self.semantic_memory.add_concept(name, definition)
    
    def retrieve_concept(self, name: str) -> Optional[Concept]:
        """Retrieve a concept from long-term memory"""
        return self.semantic_memory.get_concept_by_name(name)
    
    def store_fact(self, statement: str, subject: str, predicate: str, 
                   obj: str, source: str = "") -> Fact:
        """Store a fact in long-term memory"""
        return self.semantic_memory.add_fact(statement, subject, predicate, obj, source)
    
    def store_procedure(self, name: str, description: str) -> Procedure:
        """Store a procedure in long-term memory"""
        return self.semantic_memory.add_procedure(name, description)
    
    def search(self, query: str, limit: int = 10) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Search long-term memory"""
        return {
            'concepts': [c.to_dict() for c in self.semantic_memory.search_concepts(query, limit)],
            'facts': [f.to_dict() for f in self.semantic_memory.search_facts(query, limit)]
        }
    
    def save(self) -> None:
        """Save long-term memory"""
        self.semantic_memory.save()
    
    def load(self) -> None:
        """Load long-term memory"""
        self.semantic_memory.load()
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get memory statistics"""
        return self.semantic_memory.get_stats()


# FILE: agi_core/memory/memory_system.py
"""
Memory System - Unified Interface for All Memory Types

This module provides a unified interface for the AGI's complete memory system,
integrating short-term, long-term, and episodic memory.
"""

import time
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Union
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import json
import os

from .short_term_memory import ShortTermMemory, WorkingMemory, MemoryItem
from .long_term_memory import LongTermMemory, SemanticMemory, Concept, Fact, Procedure
from .episodic_memory import EpisodicMemory, Experience, EventSequence


@dataclass
class MemoryQuery:
    """A query to the memory system"""
    query: str
    memory_types: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)  # 'stm', 'ltm', 'episodic'
    limit: int = 10
    confidence_threshold: float = 0.0
    tags: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    time_range: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None  # {'start': ..., 'end': ...}
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        if not self.memory_types:
            self.memory_types = ['stm', 'ltm', 'episodic']


@dataclass
class MemoryResult:
    """A result from a memory query"""
    memory_type: str
    item_type: str  # 'concept', 'fact', 'experience', 'memory_item'
    item: Any
    confidence: float
    relevance: float
    timestamp: float
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'memory_type': self.memory_type,
            'item_type': self.item_type,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'relevance': self.relevance,
            'timestamp': self.timestamp
        }


class MemorySystem:
    """
    Unified memory system for the AGI.
    
    This system integrates:
    - Short-term memory (working memory, immediate context)
    - Long-term memory (semantic knowledge, concepts, facts)
    - Episodic memory (personal experiences, events)
    
    It provides a single interface for storing, retrieving, and
    reasoning about information across all memory types.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, stm_capacity: int = 50, ltm_path: str = "ltm.json",
                 episodic_path: str = "episodic.json"):
        # Initialize memory subsystems
        self.stm = ShortTermMemory(max_items=stm_capacity)
        self.working_memory = WorkingMemory(capacity=7)
        self.ltm = LongTermMemory(storage_path=ltm_path)
        self.episodic = EpisodicMemory(storage_path=episodic_path)
        
        # Memory statistics
        self.access_count = 0
        self.store_count = 0
        self.last_access_time = time.time()
        
    def store(self, content: Any, memory_type: str = "fact",
              destination: str = "auto", **kwargs) -> str:
        """
        Store information in memory.
        
        Args:
            content: The information to store
            memory_type: Type of memory item ('fact', 'concept', 'experience', etc.)
            destination: Where to store ('stm', 'ltm', 'episodic', 'auto')
            **kwargs: Additional parameters for specific memory types
        
        Returns:
            The ID of the stored item
        """
        self.store_count += 1
        
        if destination == "stm" or (destination == "auto" and self._should_use_stm(content)):
            # Store in short-term memory
            item_id = self.stm.add(content, memory_type=memory_type, **kwargs)
            return f"stm:{item_id}"
        
        elif destination == "ltm" or (destination == "auto" and memory_type in ['concept', 'fact']):
            # Store in long-term memory
            if memory_type == 'concept':
                concept = self.ltm.store_concept(
                    kwargs.get('name', str(content)[:50]),
                    kwargs.get('definition', str(content))
                )
                return f"ltm:concept:{concept.concept_id}"
            else:
                # Default to storing as a fact
                fact = self.ltm.store_fact(
                    statement=str(content),
                    subject=kwargs.get('subject', 'unknown'),
                    predicate=kwargs.get('predicate', 'has'),
                    obj=kwargs.get('object', str(content)[:50])
                )
                return f"ltm:fact:{fact.fact_id}"
        
        elif destination == "episodic" or (destination == "auto" and memory_type == 'experience'):
            # Store in episodic memory
            experience = self.episodic.add_experience(
                title=kwargs.get('title', str(content)[:50]),
                description=str(content),
                timestamp=kwargs.get('timestamp')
            )
            return f"episodic:{experience.experience_id}"
        
        else:
            # Default to short-term memory
            item_id = self.stm.add(content, memory_type=memory_type, **kwargs)
            return f"stm:{item_id}"
    
    def _should_use_stm(self, content: Any) -> bool:
        """Determine if content should be stored in short-term memory"""
        # Simple heuristic: short content or recent context goes to STM
        content_str = str(content)
        return len(content_str) < 500
    
    def retrieve(self, item_id: str) -> Optional[Any]:
        """Retrieve an item from memory by ID"""
        self.access_count += 1
        self.last_access_time = time.time()
        
        if item_id.startswith("stm:"):
            return self.stm.get(item_id[4:])
        elif item_id.startswith("ltm:concept:"):
            return self.ltm.retrieve_concept(item_id[12:])
        elif item_id.startswith("ltm:fact:"):
            return self.ltm.semantic_memory.get_fact(item_id[9:])
        elif item_id.startswith("episodic:"):
            return self.episodic.get_experience(item_id[9:])
        else:
            # Try all memory types
            # STM
            result = self.stm.get(item_id)
            if result:
                return result
            
            # LTM
            concept = self.ltm.semantic_memory.get_concept(item_id)
            if concept:
                return concept
            
            fact = self.ltm.semantic_memory.get_fact(item_id)
            if fact:
                return fact
            
            # Episodic
            return self.episodic.get_experience(item_id)
    
    def query(self, query: Union[str, MemoryQuery]) -> List[MemoryResult]:
        """
        Query memory across all types.
        
        Args:
            query: Either a string query or a MemoryQuery object
        
        Returns:
            List of MemoryResult objects
        """
        self.access_count += 1
        self.last_access_time = time.time()
        
        if isinstance(query, str):
            query = MemoryQuery(query=query)
        
        results: List[MemoryResult] = []
        
        # Query short-term memory
        if 'stm' in query.memory_types:
            stm_results = self.stm.search(query.query, query.limit)
            for item in stm_results:
                if item.confidence >= query.confidence_threshold:
                    results.append(MemoryResult(
                        memory_type='stm',
                        item_type='memory_item',
                        item=item,
                        confidence=item.confidence,
                        relevance=1.0,
                        timestamp=item.timestamp
                    ))
        
        # Query long-term memory
        if 'ltm' in query.memory_types:
            ltm_results = self.ltm.search(query.query, query.limit)
            for concept in ltm_results.get('concepts', []):
                results.append(MemoryResult(
                    memory_type='ltm',
                    item_type='concept',
                    item=concept,
                    confidence=concept.get('confidence', 1.0),
                    relevance=0.9,
                    timestamp=concept.get('timestamp', time.time())
                ))
            
            for fact in ltm_results.get('facts', []):
                results.append(MemoryResult(
                    memory_type='ltm',
                    item_type='fact',
                    item=fact,
                    confidence=fact.get('confidence', 1.0),
                    relevance=0.9,
                    timestamp=fact.get('timestamp', time.time())
                ))
        
        # Query episodic memory
        if 'episodic' in query.memory_types:
            episodic_results = self.episodic.search_experiences(query.query, query.limit)
            for experience in episodic_results:
                if query.time_range:
                    start = query.time_range.get('start', 0)
                    end = query.time_range.get('end', float('inf'))
                    if not (start <= experience.timestamp <= end):
                        continue
                
                results.append(MemoryResult(
                    memory_type='episodic',
                    item_type='experience',
                    item=experience,
                    confidence=experience.confidence,
                    relevance=0.8,
                    timestamp=experience.timestamp
                ))
        
        # Sort by relevance and confidence
        results.sort(key=lambda x: (x.relevance, x.confidence), reverse=True)
        
        return results[:query.limit]
    
    def remember_experience(self, title: str, description: str,
                           **kwargs) -> str:
        """
        Store an experience in episodic memory.
        
        This is a convenience method for storing experiences.
        """
        experience = self.episodic.add_experience(title, description, **kwargs)
        
        # Also store key information in STM for immediate access
        self.stm.add(
            content=f"Experience: {title}",
            memory_type="experience",
            tags=['experience', title.lower()]
        )
        
        return f"episodic:{experience.experience_id}"
    
    def learn_concept(self, name: str, definition: str, 
                      examples: List[str] = None) -> str:
        """
        Learn a new concept and store it in long-term memory.
        """
        concept = self.ltm.store_concept(name, definition)
        
        if examples:
            for example in examples:
                concept.add_example(example)
        
        # Store in STM for immediate use
        self.stm.add(
            content=f"Concept: {name} - {definition}",
            memory_type="concept",
            tags=['concept', name.lower()]
        )
        
        return f"ltm:concept:{concept.concept_id}"
    
    def learn_fact(self, statement: str, subject: str, predicate: str, 
                   obj: str, source: str = "") -> str:
        """
        Learn a new fact and store it in long-term memory.
        """
        fact = self.ltm.store_fact(statement, subject, predicate, obj, source)
        
        # Store in STM for immediate use
        self.stm.add(
            content=f"Fact: {statement}",
            memory_type="fact",
            tags=['fact', subject.lower(), predicate.lower()]
        )
        
        return f"ltm:fact:{fact.fact_id}"
    
    def forget(self, item_id: str) -> bool:
        """Remove an item from memory"""
        if item_id.startswith("stm:"):
            return self.stm.remove(item_id[4:])
        elif item_id.startswith("ltm:concept:"):
            concept_id = item_id[12:]
            if concept_id in self.ltm.semantic_memory.concepts:
                del self.ltm.semantic_memory.concepts[concept_id]
                return True
        elif item_id.startswith("ltm:fact:"):
            fact_id = item_id[9:]
            if fact_id in self.ltm.semantic_memory.facts:
                del self.ltm.semantic_memory.facts[fact_id]
                return True
        elif item_id.startswith("episodic:"):
            exp_id = item_id[9:]
            if exp_id in self.episodic.experiences:
                del self.episodic.experiences[exp_id]
                return True
        return False
    
    def consolidate_memory(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Consolidate information from short-term to long-term memory.
        
        This moves important or frequently accessed information from
        STM to LTM for permanent storage.
        """
        results = {
            'consolidated_items': 0,
            'stm_to_ltm': 0,
            'stm_to_episodic': 0
        }
        
        # Get frequently accessed STM items
        stm_items = self.stm.get_recent(20)
        
        for item in stm_items:
            # If accessed many times or has high importance tags
            if item.access_count > 3 or any(tag in ['important', 'remember'] for tag in item.tags):
                # Convert to LTM based on type
                if item.memory_type == 'experience':
                    # Store as experience in episodic memory
                    self.episodic.add_experience(
                        title=str(item.content)[:50],
                        description=str(item.content),
                        timestamp=item.timestamp
                    )
                    results['stm_to_episodic'] += 1
                else:
                    # Store as concept or fact in LTM
                    content_str = str(item.content)
                    if len(content_str) > 100:
                        # Store as concept
                        self.ltm.store_concept(
                            name=content_str[:50],
                            definition=content_str
                        )
                    else:
                        # Store as fact
                        self.ltm.store_fact(
                            statement=content_str,
                            subject="unknown",
                            predicate="has",
                            obj=content_str
                        )
                    results['stm_to_ltm'] += 1
                
                results['consolidated_items'] += 1
        
        return results
    
    def get_context(self, focus: str, n: int = 10) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Get relevant context for a given focus.
        
        This retrieves information from all memory types that is
        relevant to the current focus of attention.
        """
        # Create a context window in STM
        context_window = self.stm.create_context_window(focus=focus)
        
        # Query all memory types
        results = self.query(MemoryQuery(
            query=focus,
            limit=n
        ))
        
        # Add results to context window
        for result in results:
            self.stm.add(
                content=result.item,
                memory_type=result.item_type,
                tags=[focus, 'context']
            )
        
        return {
            'context_window_id': context_window.window_id,
            'results': results,
            'stm_context': self.stm.get_recent(5)
        }
    
    def save_all(self) -> None:
        """Save all memory to persistent storage"""
        self.ltm.save()
        self.episodic.save()
    
    def load_all(self) -> None:
        """Load all memory from persistent storage"""
        self.ltm.load()
        self.episodic.load()
    
    def clear_all(self) -> None:
        """Clear all memory"""
        self.stm.clear()
        self.working_memory.clear()
        self.ltm.semantic_memory.clear()
        self.episodic.clear()
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get comprehensive memory statistics"""
        return {
            'access_count': self.access_count,
            'store_count': self.store_count,
            'stm': self.stm.get_stats(),
            'ltm': self.ltm.get_stats(),
            'episodic': self.episodic.get_stats(),
            'working_memory': {
                'items': len(self.working_memory.items),
                'capacity': self.working_memory.capacity
            }
        }
    
    def set_working_memory_focus(self, focus: str) -> None:
        """Set the current focus of working memory"""
        self.working_memory.push_focus(focus)
    
    def get_working_memory_contents(self) -> List[Any]:
        """Get the current contents of working memory"""
        return self.working_memory.get_contents()
