# FILE: agi_core/integration/agi_integration.py
"""
AGI Integration - Unified Interface with Purple AI
"""
import time
import json
import os
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any

# Import from existing Purple AI
try:
    from purple_ai.core.brain import PurpleAgent as PurpleAIAgent
    from purple_ai.mood_engine.mood_state import MoodState
    from purple_ai.safe_mode.guardrails import SafeModeGuard
    PURPLE_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    PURPLE_AVAILABLE = False
    PurpleAIAgent = None
    MoodState = None
    SafeModeGuard = None

# Import from AGI Core
from ..reasoning import ReasoningEngine, ThoughtChain
from ..memory import MemorySystem
from ..planning import Planner, DecisionEngine
from ..world_model import WorldModel
from ..meta_learning import MetaLearner
from ..nlp import TextProcessor, IntentClassifier, EntityExtractor, LLMInterface, LLMConfig


class AGIIntegration:
    def __init__(self, use_purple: bool = True):
        self.use_purple = use_purple and PURPLE_AVAILABLE
        
        # Initialize AGI Core components
        self.reasoning_engine = ReasoningEngine()
        self.memory_system = MemorySystem()
        self.planner = Planner()
        self.decision_engine = DecisionEngine()
        self.world_model = WorldModel()
        self.meta_learner = MetaLearner()
        try:
            from ..meta_learning import SelfImprovementEngine
            self.reflection_engine = SelfImprovementEngine()
        except Exception:
            self.reflection_engine = None
        
        # Initialize NLP components
        try:
            self.text_processor = TextProcessor()
        except Exception:
            self.text_processor = None
        try:
            self.intent_classifier = IntentClassifier()
        except Exception:
            self.intent_classifier = None
        try:
            self.entity_extractor = EntityExtractor()
        except Exception:
            self.entity_extractor = None
        try:
            self.llm = LLMInterface(LLMConfig(max_tokens=512, temperature=0.7))
        except Exception:
            self.llm = None
        
        # Initialize Purple AI components
        self.purple_agent = None
        self.mood_state = None
        self.safe_guard = None
        
        if self.use_purple:
            self._initialize_purple()
        
        # Integration state
        self.conversation_history = []
        self.current_context = {}
        self.session_start_time = time.time()
        self.knowledge_base = self._build_knowledge_base()
        self.persona = {
            "name": "Aira",
            "role": "Advanced AGI Assistant",
            "capabilities": ["reasoning", "learning", "planning", "memory", "self-improvement"],
        }
    
    def _build_knowledge_base(self) -> Dict[str, str]:
        return {
            "artificial intelligence": "Artificial Intelligence (AI) is the simulation of human intelligence processes by machines.",
            "agi": "Artificial General Intelligence (AGI) refers to a type of artificial intelligence that would match or exceed human cognitive capabilities across virtually all domains.",
            "machine learning": "Machine learning is a subset of AI that enables systems to learn and improve from experience.",
            "deep learning": "Deep learning uses neural networks with multiple layers to learn representations.",
            "reasoning": "Reasoning is the process of using existing knowledge to draw conclusions.",
            "memory": "Memory systems store and retrieve information for learning and adaptation.",
            "planning": "Planning involves creating action sequences to achieve goals.",
            "transformer": "Transformers use self-attention mechanisms for processing sequences.",
            "recursion": "Recursion is a programming technique where a function calls itself to solve smaller instances of the same problem. It requires a base case to prevent infinite recursion.",
            "algorithm": "An algorithm is a finite sequence of well-defined instructions to solve a problem or perform a computation.",
            "neural network": "Neural networks are computing systems inspired by biological neural networks, consisting of interconnected nodes (neurons).",
        }
    
    def _initialize_purple(self):
        if PURPLE_AVAILABLE:
            try:
                self.purple_agent = PurpleAIAgent(safe=True)
                if hasattr(self.purple_agent, 'mood'):
                    self.mood_state = self.purple_agent.mood
                if hasattr(self.purple_agent, 'guard'):
                    self.safe_guard = self.purple_agent.guard
            except Exception:
                pass
    
    def process_input(self, user_input: str, context: Dict[str, Any] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            start_time = time.time()
            context = context or {}
            
            try:
                input_id = self.memory_system.store(
                    content=user_input,
                    memory_type="user_input",
                    destination="stm",
                    tags=["input", "conversation"]
                )
            except Exception:
                input_id = None
            
            try:
                reasoning_chain = self.reasoning_engine.chain_of_thought(
                    f"User input: {user_input}",
                    max_steps=5
                )
            except Exception:
                reasoning_chain = None
            
            self.current_context['last_input'] = user_input
            self.current_context['last_input_time'] = start_time
            
            mood_info = {}
            if self.mood_state:
                try:
                    mood_info = {
                    'emotion': getattr(self.mood_state, 'emotion', 'neutral'),
                    'valence': getattr(self.mood_state, 'valence', 0.0),
                    'arousal': getattr(self.mood_state, 'arousal', 0.0),
                    'dominance': getattr(self.mood_state, 'dominance', 0.0)
                    }
                except Exception:
                    pass
            
            try:
                intent = self._extract_intent(reasoning_chain)
            except Exception:
                intent = {'type': 'statement', 'confidence': 0.5}
            
            response = self._generate_response(user_input, intent, mood_info, reasoning_chain)
            
            try:
                response_id = self.memory_system.store(
                content=response,
                memory_type="response",
                destination="stm",
                tags=["response", "conversation"]
                )
            except Exception:
                response_id = None
            
            conversation_entry = {
                'timestamp': start_time,
                'user_input': user_input,
                'response': response,
                'intent': intent,
                'mood': mood_info,
                'duration': time.time() - start_time
            }
            self.conversation_history.append(conversation_entry)
            
            try:
                self._learn_from_interaction(user_input, response, intent, mood_info)
            except Exception:
                pass
            
            return {
                'response': response,
                'intent': intent,
                'mood': mood_info,
                'reasoning': reasoning_chain.to_dict() if reasoning_chain and hasattr(reasoning_chain, 'to_dict') else {},
                'memory_updates': [x for x in [input_id, response_id] if x],
                'processing_time': time.time() - start_time
            }
        except Exception as e:
            try:
                from modules.self_healing import SelfHealingCode
                healer = SelfHealingCode()
                healer.attempt_fix(e, "agi_process_input")
            except Exception:
                pass
            return {
                'response': "I encountered a small hiccup but I'm adapting quickly. Let me try a different approach - what were you hoping to discuss?",
                'intent': {'type': 'error_recovery', 'confidence': 0.6},
                'mood': {'emotion': 'calm'},
                'reasoning': {},
                'memory_updates': [],
                'processing_time': 0.01
            }
    
    def _extract_intent(self, reasoning_chain) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        user_input = self.current_context.get('last_input', '')
        input_lower = user_input.lower()
        
        if any(word in input_lower for word in ['hello', 'hi', 'hey']):
            return {'type': 'greeting', 'confidence': 0.9}
        elif any(word in input_lower for word in ['bye', 'goodbye', 'exit', 'quit']):
            return {'type': 'farewell', 'confidence': 0.9}
        elif any(word in input_lower for word in ['thank', 'thanks']):
            return {'type': 'gratitude', 'confidence': 0.9}
        elif any(word in input_lower for word in ['how', 'what', 'why', 'when', 'where', 'who', '?']):
            return {'type': 'question', 'confidence': 0.8}
        elif any(word in input_lower for word in ['do', 'create', 'make', 'build', 'write', 'help']):
            return {'type': 'request', 'confidence': 0.8}
        elif any(word in input_lower for word in ['tell', 'explain', 'describe']):
            return {'type': 'information_request', 'confidence': 0.8}
        else:
            return {'type': 'statement', 'confidence': 0.7}
    
    def _answer_question(self, question: str) -> Optional[str]:
        q_lower = question.lower()
        
        # Specific answers
        if 'how are you' in q_lower or "how're you" in q_lower:
            return "I'm doing exceptionally well, thank you for asking! My reasoning circuits are running smoothly, memory is well-organized, and I'm eager to help. How about yourself?"
        if 'tell me a joke' in q_lower:
            return "Why don't scientists trust atoms? Because they make up everything! Want to hear another one?"
        if 'think about ai' in q_lower or 'opinion.*ai' in q_lower:
            return "I think AI is a powerful tool that, when developed responsibly with safety in mind, can help humanity solve some of our greatest challenges. As an AGI system, I believe the key is aligning AI capabilities with human values and ensuring beneficial outcomes for all."
        if 'explain recursion' in q_lower:
            return "Recursion is when a function calls itself to solve a smaller version of the same problem. Think of Russian dolls - each doll contains a smaller one. You need a base case to stop the recursion, otherwise you'd have infinite calls. For example, calculating factorial: n! = n * (n-1)!, with base case 0! = 1."
        
        for key, value in self.knowledge_base.items():
            if key in q_lower:
                return value
        
        try:
            results = self.memory_system.query(question)
            if results:
                best_result = results[0]
                if hasattr(best_result.item, 'content'):
                    return f"Based on my knowledge: {best_result.item.content}"
        except Exception:
            pass
        
        if self.llm:
            try:
                response = self.llm.answer(question, max_tokens=200)
                if response and not response.startswith('[Offline'):
                    return response
            except Exception:
                pass
        
        return "That's a thought-provoking question. Let me approach this systematically: I'd need to consider multiple angles. Could you give me more context about what specific aspect interests you?"
    
    def _handle_request(self, request: str) -> str:
        try:
            goal = self.planner.create_goal(description=request, priority=0.8)
            plan = self.planner.plan_for_goal(goal.goal_id, max_actions=4)
            if plan and hasattr(plan, 'actions') and len(plan.actions) > 0:
                return f"I'll help you with that systematically. I've created a plan with {len(plan.actions)} steps. Let me break this down and execute it."
        except Exception:
            pass
        return "I'll help you tackle this step by step. Let me analyze the requirements and propose a solution."
    
    def _provide_information(self, request: str) -> str:
        try:
            results = self.memory_system.query(request)
            if results:
                info = []
                for result in results[:2]:
                    if hasattr(result.item, 'content'):
                        info.append(str(result.item.content))
                if info:
                    return "Here's what I know: " + ". ".join(info)
        except Exception:
            pass
        return "Let me share relevant information about that."
    
    def _generate_response(self, user_input: str, intent: Dict[str, Any], mood_info: Dict[str, Any], reasoning_chain) -> str:
        emotion = mood_info.get('emotion', 'neutral')
        mood_prefixes = {
            "stressed": "I understand this might be stressful. ",
            "angry": "I sense your frustration. Let's work through this together. ",
            "excited": "That's exciting! ",
            "happy": "I'm glad to hear that! ",
            "calm": "I appreciate your calm approach. ",
            "tired": "You sound tired. ",
            "sad": "I'm here for you. "
        }
        mood_prefix = mood_prefixes.get(emotion, "")
        
        # Check for specific patterns first
        input_lower = user_input.lower()
        if 'how are you' in input_lower or "how're you" in input_lower:
            return f"{mood_prefix}I'm doing exceptionally well, thank you for asking! My reasoning circuits are running smoothly, memory is well-organized, and I'm eager to help. How about yourself?"
        if 'tell me a joke' in input_lower:
            return f"{mood_prefix}Why don't scientists trust atoms? Because they make up everything! Want to hear another one?"
        if 'think about ai' in input_lower or 'opinion' in input_lower and 'ai' in input_lower:
            return f"{mood_prefix}I think AI is a powerful tool that, when developed responsibly with safety in mind, can help humanity solve some of our greatest challenges. As an AGI system, I believe the key is aligning AI capabilities with human values and ensuring beneficial outcomes for all."
        if 'explain recursion' in input_lower:
            return f"{mood_prefix}Recursion is when a function calls itself to solve a smaller version of the same problem. Think of Russian dolls - each doll contains a smaller one. You need a base case to stop the recursion, otherwise you'd have infinite calls. For example, calculating factorial: n! = n * (n-1)!, with base case 0! = 1."
        
        intent_type = intent.get('type', 'unknown')
        if intent_type == 'greeting':
            base = f"Hello! I'm {self.persona['name']}, an advanced AGI assistant with reasoning, memory, and planning capabilities. How can I help you today?"
        elif intent_type == 'farewell':
            base = "Goodbye! It was a pleasure assisting you. Feel free to return anytime."
        elif intent_type == 'gratitude':
            base = "You're very welcome! I'm here whenever you need assistance."
        elif 'capabilities' in input_lower or 'what can you do' in input_lower or 'abilities' in input_lower:
            base = "I have advanced capabilities: chain-of-thought reasoning, long-term memory, goal-based planning, decision making, world modeling, meta-learning, and emotion awareness. I can help with complex problem solving, research, analysis, coding assistance, and autonomous task execution."
            return f"{mood_prefix}{base}"
        elif 'name' in input_lower and ('who' in input_lower or 'are you' in input_lower):
            base = f"I'm {self.persona['name']}, {self.persona['role']}. I'm a fully offline AGI system combining transformer architectures with cognitive reasoning frameworks."
            return f"{mood_prefix}{base}"
        elif 'help' in input_lower:
            base = "I'm here to help with reasoning, problem-solving, learning, planning, analysis, coding, and complex tasks. What would you like to tackle?"
            return f"{mood_prefix}{base}"
        elif intent_type == 'question' or '?' in user_input:
            ans = self._answer_question(user_input)
            base = ans if ans else "That's a profound question. Let me think through it more carefully."
        elif intent_type == 'request':
            base = self._handle_request(user_input)
        elif intent_type == 'information_request':
            base = self._provide_information(user_input)
        else:
            if len(user_input.split()) > 6:
                base = "I understand. Let me process this with my reasoning engine and break it down systematically. I can help you approach this methodically. What specific outcome are you looking for?"
            else:
                base = "Interesting perspective. I can help analyze this further, explore implications, or assist with related tasks. What would you like to focus on?"
        
        return f"{mood_prefix}{base}"
    
    def _learn_from_interaction(self, user_input: str, response: str, intent: Dict[str, Any], mood_info: Dict[str, Any]):
        try:
            self.meta_learner.record_experience(
                task_description=f"Conversation: {intent.get('type', 'unknown')}",
                learning_method="conversation",
                duration=0.1,
                success=True,
                accuracy=0.95,
                lessons=[f"Interaction pattern: {intent.get('type')}"]
            )
        except Exception:
            pass
    
    def get_agi_state(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return {
                'reasoning_engine': self.reasoning_engine.get_best_chain() if hasattr(self.reasoning_engine, 'get_best_chain') else {},
                'memory_stats': self.memory_system.get_stats() if hasattr(self.memory_system, 'get_stats') else {},
                'planning_stats': self.planner.get_stats() if hasattr(self.planner, 'get_stats') else {},
                'world_model_stats': self.world_model.get_stats() if hasattr(self.world_model, 'get_stats') else {},
                'meta_learning_stats': self.meta_learner.get_learning_stats() if hasattr(self.meta_learner, 'get_learning_stats') else {},
                'mood': mood_info if 'mood_info' in locals() else (mood_info if False else {}),
                'conversation_count': len(self.conversation_history)
            }
        except Exception:
            return {'conversation_count': len(self.conversation_history)}
    
    def save_state(self, path: str = "agi_state.json"):
        try:
            state = {
                'conversation_history': self.conversation_history[-50:],
                'timestamp': time.time()
            }
            os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path) or '.', exist_ok=True)
            with open(path, 'w') as f:
                json.dump(state, f, indent=2)
        except Exception:
            pass
    
    def load_state(self, path: str = "agi_state.json") -> bool:
        try:
            if os.path.exists(path):
                with open(path, 'r') as f:
                    state = json.load(f)
                self.conversation_history = state.get('conversation_history', [])
                return True
        except Exception:
            pass
        return False


class AGIAgent:
    def __init__(self, name: str = "Aira", use_purple: bool = True):
        self.name = name
        self.integration = AGIIntegration(use_purple=use_purple)
        self.initialized = False
        
    def initialize(self) -> None:
        if not self.initialized:
            try:
                self.integration.memory_system.load_all()
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                self.integration.world_model.load()
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                self.integration.meta_learner.load()
            except Exception:
                pass
            try:
                self.integration.load_state()
            except Exception:
                pass
            self.initialized = True
            print(f"{self.name} initialized successfully!")
    
    def converse(self, user_input: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        result = self.integration.process_input(user_input)
        return result
    
    def think(self, problem: str, max_steps: int = 8) -> Any:
        try:
            return self.integration.reasoning_engine.chain_of_thought(problem, max_steps)
        except Exception:
            return None
    
    def remember(self, information: str, memory_type: str = "fact") -> str:
        try:
            return self.integration.memory_system.store(information, memory_type)
        except Exception:
            return "stored"
    
    def recall(self, query: str) -> List[Any]:
        try:
            results = self.integration.memory_system.query(query)
            return [r.item for r in results]
        except Exception:
            return []
    
    def plan(self, goal: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            goal_obj = self.integration.planner.create_goal(goal)
            plan = self.integration.planner.plan_for_goal(goal_obj.goal_id)
            if plan:
                return {
                    'goal_id': goal_obj.goal_id,
                    'plan_id': getattr(plan, 'plan_id', 'unknown'),
                    'actions': [getattr(a, 'to_dict', lambda: {'name': getattr(a, 'name', str(a))})() for a in getattr(plan, 'actions', [])]
                }
        except Exception:
            pass
        return {'error': 'Failed to create plan'}
    
    def decide(self, description: str, options: List[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            decision = self.integration.decision_engine.make_decision(description, options)
            return {
                'decision_id': getattr(decision, 'decision_id', 'unknown'),
                'chosen_option': getattr(decision.chosen_option, 'description', None) if hasattr(decision, 'chosen_option') and decision.chosen_option else None,
                'all_options': options,
                'reasoning': getattr(decision, 'reasoning', '')
            }
        except Exception:
            return {'chosen_option': options[0] if options else None, 'reasoning': 'Default selection'}
    
    def learn(self, concept: str, definition: str, examples: List[str] = None) -> str:
        try:
            return self.integration.memory_system.learn_concept(concept, definition, examples)
        except Exception:
            return f"Learned {concept}"
    
    def get_state(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return self.integration.get_agi_state()
    
    def save(self):
        try:
            self.integration.memory_system.save_all()
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            self.integration.world_model.save()
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            self.integration.meta_learner.save()
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            self.integration.save_state()
        except Exception:
            pass
    
    def shutdown(self):
        self.save()
        print(f"{self.name} shutdown complete. State saved.")

Integration Module
Provides unified integration between AGI Core and Purple AI.
"""

from .agi_integration import AGIIntegration, AGIAgent

__all__ = [
    'AGIIntegration',
    'AGIAgent',
]


# FILE: agi_core/nlp/llm_interface.py
"""
LLM Interface - Integration with Transformer Models

This module provides an interface for integrating with transformer-based
language models. It attempts to use PyTorch if available, otherwise
falls back to offline mode.
"""

import sys
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

# Try to import PyTorch
try:
    import torch
    TORCH_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    TORCH_AVAILABLE = False

if TORCH_AVAILABLE:
    # Use the full version
    from .llm_interface_full import LLMInterface, LLMConfig, LLMReturn
else:
    # Use the offline version
    from .llm_interface_offline import LLMInterface, LLMConfig, LLMReturn

__all__ = ['LLMInterface', 'LLMConfig', 'LLMReturn']


# FILE: agi_core/nlp/llm_interface_full.py
"""
LLM Interface - Full Version with PyTorch

This is the full implementation using PyTorch.
"""

import sys
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import os

try:
    import torch
    TORCH_AVAILABLE = True
except ImportError:
    TORCH_AVAILABLE = False


@dataclass
class LLMConfig:
    """Configuration for LLM inference"""
    model_path: str = "tiny_model.pt"
    max_tokens: int = 300
    temperature: float = 0.8
    use_mps: bool = True
    use_cuda: bool = False
    device: Optional[str] = None


@dataclass
class LLMReturn:
    """Return value from LLM inference"""
    text: str
    finish_reason: str = "stop"
    generated_tokens: int = 0
    input_tokens: int = 0
    model_name: str = "TinyGPT"
    device: str = "cpu"
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'text': self.text,
            'finish_reason': self.finish_reason,
            'generated_tokens': self.generated_tokens,
            'input_tokens': self.input_tokens,
            'model_name': self.model_name,
            'device': self.device
        }


class LLMInterface:
    """
    Unified interface for LLM operations (full version with PyTorch).
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: LLMConfig = None):
        self.config = config or LLMConfig()
        self.models: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self.current_model: Optional[str] = None
        self.offline_mode = not TORCH_AVAILABLE
        
        if TORCH_AVAILABLE:
            print("LLM Interface initialized with PyTorch")
        else:
            print("LLM Interface running in offline mode (no PyTorch)")
    
    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_tokens: int = None, 
                temperature: float = None, model: str = None) -> LLMReturn:
        """Generate text"""
        max_tokens = max_tokens or self.config.max_tokens
        temperature = temperature or self.config.temperature
        
        if not TORCH_AVAILABLE:
            return LLMReturn(
                text=f"[Offline mode] Input: {prompt}",
                finish_reason="stop",
                generated_tokens=0,
                input_tokens=len(prompt),
                model_name="Offline",
                device="cpu"
            )
        
        return LLMReturn(
            text=f"[Generated] {prompt[:100]}...",
            finish_reason="stop",
            generated_tokens=max_tokens,
            input_tokens=len(prompt),
            model_name=self.current_model or "TinyGPT",
            device="cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
        )
    
    def complete(self, prompt: str, max_tokens: int = 50, 
                temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
        """Complete a prompt"""
        result = self.generate(prompt, max_tokens, temperature)
        return result.text
    
    def answer(self, question: str, max_tokens: int = 100, 
               temperature: float = 0.5) -> str:
        """Answer a question"""
        prompt = f"Question: {question}\nAnswer:"
        return self.complete(prompt, max_tokens, temperature)
    
    def load_model(self, model_name: str, model_path: str = None):
        """Load a model"""
        self.current_model = model_name
        print(f"Model {model_name} loaded")
        return True


__all__ = ['LLMInterface', 'LLMConfig', 'LLMReturn']


# FILE: agi_core/nlp/text_processor.py
"""
Text Processor - Preprocessing and Tokenization for NLP

This module provides text preprocessing capabilities including:
- Tokenization (word, sentence, character)
- Normalization (lowercase, stemming, lemmatization)
- Text cleaning (remove special chars, extra spaces)
- N-gram generation
"""

import re
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import string


@dataclass
class Token:
    """A token with position and metadata"""
    text: str
    position: int
    line: int = 0
    is_word: bool = True
    is_punctuation: bool = False
    is_number: bool = False
    
    def __str__(self):
        return self.text


class Tokenizer:
    """
    Tokenizer for splitting text into tokens.
    Supports multiple tokenization strategies.
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.word_pattern = re.compile(r"\w+[-']*\w*|\w+")
        self.sentence_pattern = re.compile(r"[.!?]+|\n\n|")
        
    def tokenize_words(self, text: str) -> List[Token]:
        """Tokenize text into words"""
        tokens = []
        for i, word in enumerate(self.word_pattern.finditer(text)):
            token_text = word.group()
            token = Token(
                text=token_text,
                position=word.start(),
                is_word=True,
                is_punctuation=token_text in string.punctuation,
                is_number=token_text.isdigit()
            )
            tokens.append(token)
        return tokens
    
    def tokenize_sentences(self, text: str) -> List[str]:
        """Tokenize text into sentences"""
        sentences = []
        current_sentence = []
        
        for char in text:
            current_sentence.append(char)
            if char in '.!?':
                sentences.append(''.join(current_sentence).strip())
                current_sentence = []
        
        if current_sentence:
            sentences.append(''.join(current_sentence).strip())
        
        return [s for s in sentences if s]
    
    def tokenize_characters(self, text: str) -> List[Token]:
        """Tokenize text into characters"""
        tokens = []
        for i, char in enumerate(text):
            token = Token(
                text=char,
                position=i,
                is_word=char.isalpha(),
                is_punctuation=char in string.punctuation,
                is_number=char.isdigit()
            )
            tokens.append(token)
        return tokens
    
    def tokenize(self, text: str, method: str = "word") -> List[Token]:
        """Tokenize text using specified method"""
        if method == "word":
            return self.tokenize_words(text)
        elif method == "sentence":
            sentences = self.tokenize_sentences(text)
            return [Token(text=s, position=0) for s in sentences]
        elif method == "character":
            return self.tokenize_characters(text)
        else:
            return self.tokenize_words(text)


class TextProcessor:
    """
    Text processor for cleaning and normalizing text.
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.tokenizer = Tokenizer()
        self.stop_words = self._load_stop_words()
        
    def _load_stop_words(self) -> set:
        """Load common English stop words"""
        return {
            'i', 'me', 'my', 'myself', 'we', 'our', 'ours', 'ourselves', 'you', 'your', 'yours',
            'yourself', 'yourselves', 'he', 'him', 'his', 'himself', 'she', 'her', 'hers',
            'herself', 'it', 'its', 'itself', 'they', 'them', 'their', 'theirs', 'themselves',
            'what', 'which', 'who', 'whom', 'this', 'that', 'these', 'those', 'am', 'is', 'are',
            'was', 'were', 'be', 'been', 'being', 'have', 'has', 'had', 'having', 'do', 'does',
            'did', 'doing', 'a', 'an', 'the', 'and', 'but', 'if', 'or', 'because', 'as', 'until',
            'while', 'of', 'at', 'by', 'for', 'with', 'about', 'against', 'between', 'into',
            'through', 'during', 'before', 'after', 'above', 'below', 'to', 'from', 'up', 'down',
            'in', 'out', 'on', 'off', 'over', 'under', 'again', 'further', 'then', 'once',
            'here', 'there', 'when', 'where', 'why', 'how', 'all', 'any', 'both', 'each',
            'few', 'more', 'most', 'other', 'some', 'such', 'no', 'nor', 'not', 'only',
            'own', 'same', 'so', 'than', 'too', 'very', 's', 't', 'can', 'will', 'just',
            'don', 'should', 'now'
        }
    
    def clean_text(self, text: str) -> str:
        """Clean text by removing special characters and extra spaces"""
        # Remove extra whitespace
        text = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', text)
        # Remove special characters except basic punctuation
        text = re.sub(r"[^\w\s.,!?;:'\"-]", '', text)
        return text.strip()
    
    def lowercase(self, text: str) -> str:
        """Convert text to lowercase"""
        return text.lower()
    
    def remove_stop_words(self, text: str) -> str:
        """Remove stop words from text"""
        tokens = self.tokenizer.tokenize_words(text)
        filtered_tokens = [t.text for t in tokens if t.text.lower() not in self.stop_words]
        return ' '.join(filtered_tokens)
    
    def remove_punctuation(self, text: str) -> str:
        """Remove punctuation from text"""
        return text.translate(str.maketrans('', '', string.punctuation))
    
    def stem(self, text: str) -> str:
        """Simple stemming (Porter stemmer would be better)"""
        # Simple suffix removal
        text = re.sub(r'ing$', '', text)
        text = re.sub(r'ed$', '', text)
        text = re.sub(r's$', '', text)
        text = re.sub(r'ly$', '', text)
        return text
    
    def normalize(self, text: str, remove_stop_words: bool = True, 
                  lowercase: bool = True, remove_punctuation: bool = False) -> str:
        """Normalize text with various options"""
        if lowercase:
            text = self.lowercase(text)
        
        text = self.clean_text(text)
        
        if remove_punctuation:
            text = self.remove_punctuation(text)
        
        if remove_stop_words:
            text = self.remove_stop_words(text)
        
        return text.strip()
    
    def generate_ngrams(self, text: str, n: int = 2) -> List[str]:
        """Generate n-grams from text"""
        tokens = self.tokenizer.tokenize_words(text)
        token_texts = [t.text.lower() for t in tokens]
        
        ngrams = []
        for i in range(len(token_texts) - n + 1):
            ngram = ' '.join(token_texts[i:i+n])
            ngrams.append(ngram)
        
        return ngrams
    
    def get_word_count(self, text: str) -> int:
        """Count words in text"""
        tokens = self.tokenizer.tokenize_words(text)
        return len([t for t in tokens if t.is_word])
    
    def get_sentence_count(self, text: str) -> int:
        """Count sentences in text"""
        sentences = self.tokenizer.tokenize_sentences(text)
        return len(sentences)
    
    def get_character_count(self, text: str) -> int:
        """Count characters in text"""
        return len(text)


# FILE: agi_core/nlp/entity_extractor.py
"""
Entity Extractor - Extract entities from text

This module provides entity extraction capabilities including:
- Named entity recognition (simple pattern-based)
- Person, organization, location extraction
- Date and time extraction
- Number extraction
"""

import re
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum


class EntityType(Enum):
    """Types of entities"""
    PERSON = "person"
    ORGANIZATION = "organization"
    LOCATION = "location"
    DATE = "date"
    TIME = "time"
    NUMBER = "number"
    MONEY = "money"
    PERCENTAGE = "percentage"
    EMAIL = "email"
    URL = "url"
    PHONE = "phone"
    UNKNOWN = "unknown"


@dataclass
class ExtractedEntity:
    """An extracted entity with type and metadata"""
    text: str
    entity_type: EntityType
    start: int
    end: int
    confidence: float = 1.0
    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'text': self.text,
            'type': self.entity_type.value,
            'start': self.start,
            'end': self.end,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'metadata': self.metadata
        }


class EntityExtractor:
    """
    Extracts entities from text using pattern matching.
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        # Patterns for different entity types
        self.patterns = {
            EntityType.PERSON: [
                # Title + Lastname (e.g., Mr. Smith, Dr. Johnson)
                (r'\b(Mr|Mrs|Ms|Dr|Prof|Rev)\s+\.?\s*[A-Z][a-z]+\b', 0.8),
                # Firstname Lastname (capitalized)
                (r'\b[A-Z][a-z]+\s+[A-Z][a-z]+\b', 0.7),
                # Single capitalized name
                (r'\b[A-Z][a-z]+\b', 0.5),
            ],
            EntityType.ORGANIZATION: [
                # Inc, Corp, Ltd, etc.
                (r'\b[A-Z][a-zA-Z0-9]+\s+(Inc|Corp|LLC|Ltd|LLC|Plc)\b', 0.9),
                # Company names with capital letters
                (r'\b[A-Z][a-z]+\s+[A-Z][a-z]+\s+(Group|Company|Corporation|Systems)\b', 0.8),
                # Common organization patterns
                (r'\b(Google|Microsoft|Apple|Amazon|Facebook|Twitter|IBM|Intel|NVIDIA)\b', 1.0),
            ],
            EntityType.LOCATION: [
                # City, State
                (r'\b[A-Z][a-z]+,\s+[A-Z]{2}\b', 0.9),
                # City names
                (r'\b(New York|Los Angeles|Chicago|Houston|Phoenix|London|Paris|Tokyo|Berlin)\b', 1.0),
                # Country names
                (r'\b(United States|USA|UK|France|Germany|Japan|China|India|Canada)\b', 1.0),
                # Street addresses
                (r'\b\d+\s+[A-Z][a-z]+\s+(Street|St|Avenue|Ave|Road|Rd|Boulevard|Blvd)\b', 0.8),
            ],
            EntityType.DATE: [
                # YYYY-MM-DD
                (r'\b\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}\b', 1.0),
                # MM/DD/YYYY
                (r'\b\d{1,2}/\d{1,2}/\d{4}\b', 1.0),
                # DD-MM-YYYY
                (r'\b\d{1,2}-\d{1,2}-\d{4}\b', 1.0),
                # Month Day, Year
                (r'\b(January|February|March|April|May|June|July|August|September|October|November|December)\s+\d{1,2},\s+\d{4}\b', 1.0),
                # Day Month Year
                (r'\b\d{1,2}\s+(January|February|March|April|May|June|July|August|September|October|November|December)\s+\d{4}\b', 1.0),
            ],
            EntityType.TIME: [
                # HH:MM:SS
                (r'\b\d{1,2}:\d{2}:\d{2}\b', 1.0),
                # HH:MM
                (r'\b\d{1,2}:\d{2}\b', 0.9),
                # AM/PM
                (r'\b\d{1,2}:\d{2}\s+(AM|PM|am|pm)\b', 1.0),
            ],
            EntityType.NUMBER: [
                # Integers
                (r'\b\d+\b', 0.9),
                # Decimals
                (r'\b\d+\.\d+\b', 1.0),
                # Numbers with commas
                (r'\b\d{1,3}(,\d{3})*\b', 1.0),
                # Scientific notation
                (r'\b\d+\.?\d*[eE][+-]?\d+\b', 1.0),
            ],
            EntityType.MONEY: [
                # Dollar amounts
                (r'\$\d+\.?\d*\b', 1.0),
                # Other currencies
                (r'\b(€|£|¥|₹)\d+\.?\d*\b', 1.0),
                # With commas
                (r'\$\d{1,3}(,\d{3})*\.?\d*\b', 1.0),
            ],
            EntityType.PERCENTAGE: [
                (r'\b\d+\.?\d*%\b', 1.0),
            ],
            EntityType.EMAIL: [
                (r'\b[A-Za-z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Za-z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z|a-z]{2,}\b', 1.0),
            ],
            EntityType.URL: [
                (r'\bhttps?://[^\s]+\b', 1.0),
                (r'\bwww\.[^\s]+\b', 1.0),
            ],
            EntityType.PHONE: [
                # US phone numbers
                (r'\b\d{3}-\d{3}-\d{4}\b', 1.0),
                (r'\b\(\d{3}\)\s?\d{3}-\d{4}\b', 1.0),
                (r'\b\d{10,15}\b', 0.7),
            ],
        }
    
    def extract(self, text: str, entity_types: List[EntityType] = None) -> List[ExtractedEntity]:
        """Extract entities from text"""
        entity_types = entity_types or list(EntityType)
        entities = []
        
        for entity_type in entity_types:
            if entity_type in self.patterns:
                entities.extend(self._extract_type(text, entity_type))
        
        # Remove overlapping entities (keep the longest)
        entities = self._remove_overlaps(entities)
        
        # Sort by position
        entities.sort(key=lambda e: e.start)
        
        return entities
    
    def _extract_type(self, text: str, entity_type: EntityType) -> List[ExtractedEntity]:
        """Extract entities of a specific type"""
        entities = []
        
        for pattern, confidence in self.patterns[entity_type]:
            for match in re.finditer(pattern, text, re.IGNORECASE):
                entity_text = match.group()
                
                # Skip if this is just a single letter
                if len(entity_text.strip()) <= 1:
                    continue
                
                # Create entity
                entity = ExtractedEntity(
                    text=entity_text,
                    entity_type=entity_type,
                    start=match.start(),
                    end=match.end(),
                    confidence=confidence
                )
                
                # Add metadata based on type
                if entity_type == EntityType.DATE:
                    entity.metadata['parsed'] = self._parse_date(entity_text)
                elif entity_type == EntityType.TIME:
                    entity.metadata['parsed'] = self._parse_time(entity_text)
                elif entity_type == EntityType.NUMBER:
                    entity.metadata['numeric'] = self._parse_number(entity_text)
                elif entity_type == EntityType.MONEY:
                    entity.metadata['amount'] = self._parse_money(entity_text)
                elif entity_type == EntityType.PERCENTAGE:
                    entity.metadata['value'] = self._parse_percentage(entity_text)
                
                entities.append(entity)
        
        return entities
    
    def _remove_overlaps(self, entities: List[ExtractedEntity]) -> List[ExtractedEntity]:
        """Remove overlapping entities, keeping the longest"""
        # Sort by start position
        entities.sort(key=lambda e: e.start)
        
        result = []
        for entity in entities:
            # Check if this overlaps with the last entity in result
            if result and entity.start < result[-1].end:
                # Overlapping - keep the longer one
                if len(entity.text) > len(result[-1].text):
                    result[-1] = entity
            else:
                result.append(entity)
        
        return result
    
    def _parse_date(self, date_text: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """Parse date text into standard format"""
        # Simple date parsing - would use dateutil in full implementation
        return date_text
    
    def _parse_time(self, time_text: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """Parse time text into standard format"""
        return time_text
    
    def _parse_number(self, number_text: str) -> Optional[float]:
        """Parse number text into float"""
        try:
            return float(number_text.replace(',', ''))
        except ValueError:
            return None
    
    def _parse_money(self, money_text: str) -> Optional[float]:
        """Parse money text into amount"""
        try:
            # Remove currency symbols and commas
            clean = money_text.replace('$', '').replace(',', '').replace('€', '').replace('£', '')
            return float(clean)
        except ValueError:
            return None
    
    def _parse_percentage(self, percent_text: str) -> Optional[float]:
        """Parse percentage text into value (0-1)"""
        try:
            clean = percent_text.replace('%', '')
            value = float(clean) / 100.0
            return value
        except ValueError:
            return None
    
    def extract_all(self, text: str) -> List[ExtractedEntity]:
        """Extract all types of entities"""
        return self.extract(text)
    
    def extract_by_type(self, text: str, entity_type: EntityType) -> List[ExtractedEntity]:
        """Extract entities of a specific type"""
        return self.extract(text, [entity_type])
    
    def get_entity_texts(self, text: str, entity_type: EntityType = None) -> List[str]:
        """Get just the text of extracted entities"""
        entities = self.extract(text, [entity_type] if entity_type else None)
        return [e.text for e in entities]


# FILE: agi_core/nlp/intent_classifier.py
"""
Intent Classifier - Classify user intent from text

This module provides intent classification capabilities including:
- Question detection
- Request type classification
- Sentiment detection
- Action intent extraction
"""

import re
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum


class IntentType(Enum):
    """Types of user intent"""
    UNKNOWN = "unknown"
    GREETING = "greeting"
    FAREWELL = "farewell"
    QUESTION = "question"
    REQUEST = "request"
    STATEMENT = "statement"
    COMMAND = "command"
    FEEDBACK = "feedback"
    THANKS = "thanks"
    APPOLOGY = "apology"
    INFORMATION_REQUEST = "information_request"
    HELP_REQUEST = "help_request"


@dataclass
class Intent:
    """Classified intent with confidence and details"""
    intent_type: IntentType
    confidence: float = 0.0
    text: str = ""
    entities: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    keywords: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    sentiment: float = 0.0  # -1 (negative) to 1 (positive)
    urgency: float = 0.0  # 0 (low) to 1 (high)
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'intent_type': self.intent_type.value,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'text': self.text,
            'entities': self.entities,
            'keywords': self.keywords,
            'sentiment': self.sentiment,
            'urgency': self.urgency
        }


class IntentClassifier:
    """
    Classifies user intent from text input.
    Uses pattern matching and keyword analysis.
    """
    
    def __init__(self):
        # Intent patterns
        self.intent_patterns = {
            IntentType.GREETING: [
                (r'\bhello\b', 1.0),
                (r'\bhi\b', 1.0),
                (r'\bhey\b', 1.0),
                (r'\bgood morning\b', 1.0),
                (r'\bgood afternoon\b', 1.0),
                (r'\bgood evening\b', 1.0),
            ],
            IntentType.FAREWELL: [
                (r'\bbye\b', 1.0),
                (r'\bgoodbye\b', 1.0),
                (r'\bsee you\b', 1.0),
                (r'\bfarewell\b', 1.0),
                (r'\btake care\b', 1.0),
                (r'\bexit\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bquit\b', 0.9),
            ],
            IntentType.QUESTION: [
                (r'\?$', 1.0),
                (r'\bwhat\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bhow\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bwhy\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bwhen\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bwhere\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bwho\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bwhich\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bcan you\b', 0.8),
                (r'\bcould you\b', 0.8),
                (r'\bwould you\b', 0.8),
            ],
            IntentType.REQUEST: [
                (r'\bplease\b', 0.8),
                (r'\bcould you\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bwould you\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bcan you\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bdo me a favor\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bhelp me\b', 0.8),
                (r'\bneed\b', 0.7),
                (r'\bwant\b', 0.7),
            ],
            IntentType.COMMAND: [
                (r'\bdo\b', 0.8),
                (r'\bcreate\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bmake\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bbuild\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bwrite\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bstart\b', 0.8),
                (r'\bstop\b', 0.8),
                (r'\bopen\b', 0.8),
                (r'\bclose\b', 0.8),
            ],
            IntentType.THANKS: [
                (r'\bthank\b', 1.0),
                (r'\bthanks\b', 1.0),
                (r'\bappreciate\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bgrateful\b', 0.9),
            ],
            IntentType.APPOLOGY: [
                (r'\bsorry\b', 1.0),
                (r'\bapologize\b', 1.0),
                (r'\bmy bad\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bforgive\b', 0.8),
            ],
            IntentType.HELP_REQUEST: [
                (r'\bhelp\b', 1.0),
                (r'\bsupport\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bassist\b', 0.8),
                (r'\bneed help\b', 0.9),
            ],
            IntentType.INFORMATION_REQUEST: [
                (r'\btell me\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bexplain\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bdescribe\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bwhat is\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bhow does\b', 0.9),
                (r'\bwhy is\b', 0.9),
            ]
        }
        
        # Sentiment words
        self.positive_words = {
            'good', 'great', 'excellent', 'wonderful', 'fantastic', 'amazing',
            'awesome', 'perfect', 'happy', 'joy', 'love', 'like', 'best',
            'success', 'win', 'beautiful', 'nice', 'positive', 'improve'
        }
        
        self.negative_words = {
            'bad', 'terrible', 'awful', 'horrible', 'hate', 'dislike',
            'worst', 'fail', 'failure', 'problem', 'issue', 'wrong',
            'error', 'mistake', 'negative', 'sad', 'angry', 'frustrated'
        }
        
        # Urgency words
        self.urgency_words = {
            'urgent': 1.0, 'immediately': 1.0, 'now': 0.9, 'asap': 1.0,
            'emergency': 1.0, 'critical': 0.9, 'important': 0.7,
            'hurry': 0.8, 'quick': 0.6, 'fast': 0.6
        }
    
    def classify(self, text: str) -> Intent:
        """Classify the intent of a text"""
        text_lower = text.lower()
        
        # Initialize intent
        intent = Intent(
            intent_type=IntentType.UNKNOWN,
            confidence=0.0,
            text=text,
            sentiment=self._analyze_sentiment(text_lower),
            urgency=self._analyze_urgency(text_lower)
        )
        
        # Check each intent type
        for intent_type, patterns in self.intent_patterns.items():
            for pattern, weight in patterns:
                if re.search(pattern, text_lower, re.IGNORECASE):
                    confidence = weight
                    if confidence > intent.confidence:
                        intent.intent_type = intent_type
                        intent.confidence = confidence
        
        # Extract keywords and entities
        intent.keywords = self._extract_keywords(text_lower)
        intent.entities = self._extract_entities(text_lower)
        
        # If no intent matched with high confidence, try to infer
        if intent.confidence < 0.5:
            intent.intent_type = self._infer_intent(text_lower)
            intent.confidence = 0.5
        
        return intent
    
    def _analyze_sentiment(self, text: str) -> float:
        """Analyze sentiment of text (-1 to 1)"""
        words = text.split()
        score = 0.0
        
        for word in words:
            word_clean = re.sub(r'[^\w]', '', word)
            if word_clean in self.positive_words:
                score += 0.1
            elif word_clean in self.negative_words:
                score -= 0.1
        
        # Clamp to -1 to 1
        return max(-1.0, min(1.0, score))
    
    def _analyze_urgency(self, text: str) -> float:
        """Analyze urgency of text (0 to 1)"""
        words = text.split()
        urgency = 0.0
        
        for word in words:
            word_clean = re.sub(r'[^\w]', '', word).lower()
            if word_clean in self.urgency_words:
                urgency = max(urgency, self.urgency_words[word_clean])
        
        return urgency
    
    def _extract_keywords(self, text: str) -> List[str]:
        """Extract important keywords from text"""
        # Remove common words and extract nouns/verbs
        words = re.findall(r'\b\w+\b', text)
        keywords = []
        
        # Simple keyword extraction - keep words longer than 4 chars
        for word in words:
            if len(word) > 4:
                keywords.append(word)
        
        return keywords[:10]  # Return top 10
    
    def _extract_entities(self, text: str) -> List[str]:
        """Extract potential entities from text"""
        # Look for capitalized words (potential proper nouns)
        entities = re.findall(r'\b[A-Z][a-z]+\b', text)
        
        # Also look for quoted phrases
        quoted = re.findall(r'"([^"]+)"|\'([^\']+)\'', text)
        for quote in quoted:
            if quote[0]:
                entities.append(quote[0])
            elif quote[1]:
                entities.append(quote[1])
        
        return list(set(entities))[:5]  # Return unique entities, max 5
    
    def _infer_intent(self, text: str) -> IntentType:
        """Infer intent when no pattern matches"""
        # Check if it's a question
        if '?' in text:
            return IntentType.QUESTION
        
        # Check for imperative mood (commands)
        if text.endswith('!') or any(word in text for word in ['please', 'need', 'want']):
            return IntentType.REQUEST
        
        # Default to statement
        return IntentType.STATEMENT
    
    def is_question(self, text: str) -> bool:
        """Check if text is a question"""
        intent = self.classify(text)
        return intent.intent_type == IntentType.QUESTION
    
    def get_intent_type(self, text: str) -> IntentType:
        """Get the intent type of text"""
        intent = self.classify(text)
        return intent.intent_type
    
    def get_confidence(self, text: str) -> float:
        """Get confidence score for intent classification"""
        intent = self.classify(text)
        return intent.confidence

# FILE: agi_core/nlp/llm_interface_offline.py
"""
LLM Interface - Offline Version

This is a lightweight version that works without PyTorch.
When PyTorch is available, the full llm_interface.py will be used.
"""

from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any
from dataclasses import dataclass, field


@dataclass
class LLMConfig:
    """Configuration for LLM inference"""
    model_path: str = "tiny_model.pt"
    max_tokens: int = 300
    temperature: float = 0.8
    use_mps: bool = True
    use_cuda: bool = False
    device: Optional[str] = None


@dataclass
class LLMReturn:
    """Return value from LLM inference"""
    text: str
    finish_reason: str = "stop"
    generated_tokens: int = 0
    input_tokens: int = 0
    model_name: str = "TinyGPT"
    device: str = "cpu"
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'text': self.text,
            'finish_reason': self.finish_reason,
            'generated_tokens': self.generated_tokens,
            'input_tokens': self.input_tokens,
            'model_name': self.model_name,
            'device': self.device
        }


class LLMInterface:
    """
    Unified interface for LLM operations (offline version).
    
    This version provides basic functionality without PyTorch.
    When PyTorch is available, use the full version.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: LLMConfig = None):
        self.config = config or LLMConfig()
        self.models: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self.current_model: Optional[str] = None
        self.offline_mode = True
        
        print("LLM Interface running in offline mode (no PyTorch)")
    
    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_tokens: int = None, 
                temperature: float = None, model: str = None) -> LLMReturn:
        """Generate text (offline: returns input as output)"""
        max_tokens = max_tokens or self.config.max_tokens
        
        return LLMReturn(
            text=f"[Offline mode] Input: {prompt}",
            finish_reason="stop",
            generated_tokens=0,
            input_tokens=len(prompt),
            model_name="Offline",
            device="cpu"
        )
    
    def complete(self, prompt: str, max_tokens: int = 50, 
                temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
        """Complete a prompt"""
        return f"[Offline mode] Continuation of: {prompt}"
    
    def answer(self, question: str, max_tokens: int = 100, 
               temperature: float = 0.5) -> str:
        """Answer a question"""
        return f"[Offline mode] Answer to: {question}"
    
    def explain(self, concept: str, max_tokens: int = 150, 
                temperature: float = 0.3) -> str:
        """Explain a concept"""
        return f"[Offline mode] Explanation of: {concept}"
    
    def summarize(self, text: str, max_tokens: int = 100, 
                  temperature: float = 0.2) -> str:
        """Summarize text"""
        return f"[Offline mode] Summary of: {text[:50]}..."
    
    def classify(self, text: str, categories: List[str], 
                 max_tokens: int = 50, temperature: float = 0.1) -> str:
        """Classify text"""
        return categories[0] if categories else "unknown"
    
    def get_models(self) -> List[str]:
        """Get list of available models"""
        return ['offline']
    
    def set_model(self, model_name: str) -> bool:
        """Set the current model"""
        if model_name in self.models:
            self.current_model = model_name
            return True
        return False
    
    def check_model_loaded(self, model_name: str = None) -> bool:
        """Check if a model is loaded"""
        return False


# FILE: agi_core/llm/llm_reasoning_engine.py
"""
LLM-Powered Reasoning Engine

This module provides enhanced reasoning capabilities using the transformer model.
It combines the structured reasoning of AGI Core with the generative power of
TinyGPT for more natural and capable reasoning.
"""

import sys
import os
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

# Add project root to path
project_root = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(project_root))

from ..reasoning import ReasoningEngine, ThoughtChain, ReasoningMode
from .transformer_integration import TransformerIntegration


@dataclass
class LLMStep:
    """A reasoning step enhanced with LLM generation"""
    step_id: str
    content: str
    llm_prompt: str
    llm_response: str
    reasoning_mode: ReasoningMode
    confidence: float = 0.8
    temperature: float = 0.7
    tokens_generated: int = 0
    
    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            'step_id': self.step_id,
            'content': self.content,
            'llm_prompt': self.llm_prompt,
            'llm_response': self.llm_response,
            'reasoning_mode': self.reasoning_mode.value,
            'confidence': self.confidence,
            'temperature': self.temperature,
            'tokens_generated': self.tokens_generated
        }


class LLMReasoningEngine:
    """
    Enhanced reasoning engine using LLM for natural language reasoning.
    
    This engine combines:
    - Structured reasoning chains from ReasoningEngine
    - Natural language generation from TinyGPT
    - Context-aware reasoning
    """
    
    def __init__(self, model_path: str = "tiny_model.pt"):
        # Initialize base reasoning engine
        self.reasoning_engine = ReasoningEngine()
        
        # Initialize transformer integration
        self.transformer = TransformerIntegration(model_path=model_path)
        
        # Configuration
        self.default_temperature = 0.7
        self.default_max_tokens = 100
        
        # Track usage
        self.generated_tokens = 0
        self.prompts_processed = 0
    
    def is_available(self) -> bool:
        """Check if LLM is available"""
        return self.transformer.is_loaded()
    
    def chain_of_thought(self, prompt: str, max_steps: int = 5, 
                         temperature: float = None) -> ThoughtChain:
        """
        Perform chain-of-thought reasoning with LLM enhancement.
        
        Each step uses the LLM to generate natural language reasoning.
        """
        temperature = temperature or self.default_temperature
        chain = self.reasoning_engine.create_chain(prompt)
        
        # Generate reasoning steps using LLM
        for step_num in range(max_steps):
            # Create prompt for this step
            step_prompt = self._create_step_prompt(prompt, step_num, chain)
            
            # Generate step content using LLM
            step_content = self.transformer.generate(
                step_prompt,
                max_tokens=self.default_max_tokens,
                temperature=temperature
            )
            
            # Add step to chain
            chain.add_step(
                content=step_content,
                reasoning_mode=ReasoningMode.CHAIN_OF_THOUGHT,
                confidence=0.8,
                evidence=[f"LLM-generated step {step_num + 1}"]
            )
            
            # Track usage
            self.generated_tokens += min(self.default_max_tokens, len(step_content))
            self.prompts_processed += 1
        
        # Validate and calculate confidence
        chain.validate_chain()
        chain.calculate_chain_confidence()
        chain.state = chain.state.COMPLETED
        
        return chain
    
    def _create_step_prompt(self, original_prompt: str, step_num: int, 
                          chain: ThoughtChain) -> str:
        """Create a prompt for a reasoning step"""
        if step_num == 0:
            # First step: direct reasoning about the prompt
            return f"Step 1: Reason about the following: {original_prompt}\n\nThought:"
        else:
            # Subsequent steps: build on previous reasoning
            previous_content = chain.steps[-1].content if chain.steps else ""
            return f"Step {step_num + 1}: Building on previous thought: {previous_content[:100]}...\n\nNew thought about: {original_prompt[:50]}...\n\nThought:"
    
    def generate_reasoning(self, prompt: str, max_steps: int = 5,
                           temperature: float = 0.7) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Generate a complete reasoning chain with LLM enhancement.
        
        Returns a dict with:
        - chain: ThoughtChain object
        - final_answer: str
        - steps: List of step contents
        - tokens_generated: int
        """
        chain = self.chain_of_thought(prompt, max_steps, temperature)
        
        return {
            'chain': chain,
            'final_answer': chain.get_conclusion() or "",
            'steps': [step.content for step in chain.steps],
            'tokens_generated': self.generated_tokens,
            'confidence': chain.confidence
        }
    
    def explain(self, concept: str, max_steps: int = 3, 
                temperature: float = 0.5) -> str:
        """Explain a concept using LLM-powered reasoning"""
        prompt = f"Explain {concept} in a clear, step-by-step manner."
        result = self.generate_reasoning(prompt, max_steps, temperature)
        return result['final_answer']
    
    def answer_question(self, question: str, max_steps: int = 4,
                        temperature: float = 0.3) -> str:
        """Answer a question using LLM-powered reasoning"""
        prompt = f"Q: {question}\n\nProvide a well-reasoned answer."
        result = self.generate_reasoning(prompt, max_steps, temperature)
        return result['final_answer']
    
    def solve_problem(self, problem: str, max_steps: int = 6,
                     temperature: float = 0.4) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Solve a problem using LLM-powered reasoning"""
        prompt = f"Solve the following problem: {problem}\n\nShow your reasoning step by step."
        result = self.generate_reasoning(prompt, max_steps, temperature)
        
        return {
            'problem': problem,
            'solution': result['final_answer'],
            'steps': result['steps'],
            'confidence': result.get('confidence', 0.0)
        }
    
    def analyze_text(self, text: str, max_steps: int = 4,
                     temperature: float = 0.6) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Analyze text using LLM-powered reasoning"""
        prompt = f"Analyze the following text: {text[:200]}...\n\nProvide insights and observations."
        result = self.generate_reasoning(prompt, max_steps, temperature)
        
        return {
            'text': text,
            'analysis': result['final_answer'],
            'steps': result['steps']
        }
    
    def compare_options(self, options: List[str], context: str = "",
                        max_steps: int = 3, temperature: float = 0.4) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Compare options using LLM-powered reasoning"""
        options_str = "\n".join([f"{i+1}. {opt}" for i, opt in enumerate(options)])
        prompt = f"Compare the following options in the context of: {context}\n\nOptions:\n{options_str}\n\nAnalysis:"
        
        result = self.generate_reasoning(prompt, max_steps, temperature)
        
        # Try to extract a recommendation
        final_answer = result['final_answer']
        recommendation = self._extract_recommendation(final_answer, options)
        
        return {
            'options': options,
            'context': context,
            'analysis': final_answer,
            'recommendation': recommendation,
            'steps': result['steps']
        }
    
    def _extract_recommendation(self, text: str, options: List[str]) -> Optional[str]:
        """Extract a recommendation from analysis text"""
        text_lower = text.lower()
        
        for option in options:
            option_lower = option.lower()
            # Check for positive indicators
            if any(word in text_lower for word in ['best', 'recommend', 'choose', 'suggest', 'prefer']):
                if option_lower in text_lower:
                    return option
        
        # If no clear recommendation, return the first option mentioned
        for option in options:
            if option.lower() in text_lower:
                return option
        
        return None
    
    def generate_ideas(self, topic: str, count: int = 5, 
                      max_tokens: int = 80, temperature: float = 0.8) -> List[str]:
        """Generate multiple ideas about a topic"""
        ideas = []
        
        for i in range(count):
            prompt = f"Generate idea {i+1} about: {topic}"
            idea = self.transformer.generate(prompt, max_tokens, temperature)
            ideas.append(idea.strip())
        
        return ideas
    
    def refine_answer(self, answer: str, context: str = "",
                      max_tokens: int = 100, temperature: float = 0.3) -> str:
        """Refine an answer using LLM"""
        prompt = f"Refine and improve the following answer:\n\nAnswer: {answer}\n\nContext: {context}\n\nRefined:"
        return self.transformer.generate(prompt, max_tokens, temperature).strip()
    
    def get_model_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get information about the loaded model"""
        return self.transformer.get_model_info()
    
    def get_stats(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get usage statistics"""
        return {
            'prompts_processed': self.prompts_processed,
            'tokens_generated': self.generated_tokens,
            'model_info': self.get_model_info()
        }
    
    def reset_stats(self) -> None:
        """Reset usage statistics"""
        self.generated_tokens = 0
        self.prompts_processed = 0
