# FILE: agi_core/llm/transformer_integration.py
"""
Transformer Integration - Direct integration with TinyGPT model

This module provides direct integration with the TinyGPT transformer model
from the train.py module, enabling LLM-powered reasoning without external
dependencies.
"""

import sys
import os
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from torch.nn import functional as F
import math
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any, Tuple
from pathlib import Path

# Add project root to path
project_root = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(project_root))


class TinyGPTModel(nn.Module):
    """
    Direct implementation of TinyGPT for AGI Core integration.
    This mirrors the model from train.py but can be used independently.
    """
    
    def __init__(self, vocab_size: int, n_embd: int = 96, n_head: int = 4, 
                 n_layer: int = 3, block_size: int = 64, dropout: float = 0.1):
        super().__init__()
        self.n_embd = n_embd
        self.block_size = block_size
        
        # Token and position embeddings
        self.token_emb = nn.Embedding(vocab_size, n_embd)
        self.pos_emb = nn.Embedding(block_size, n_embd)
        
        # Transformer blocks
        self.blocks = nn.Sequential(*[
            self._create_block(n_embd, n_head, block_size, dropout)
            for _ in range(n_layer)
        ])
        
        # Layer norm and head
        self.ln = nn.LayerNorm(n_embd)
        self.head = nn.Linear(n_embd, vocab_size)
        
        # Dropout
        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
        
        # Causal mask
        self.register_buffer(
            "mask", torch.tril(torch.ones(block_size, block_size))
        )
    
    def _create_block(self, n_embd: int, n_head: int, block_size: int, dropout: float) -> nn.Module:
        """Create a transformer block"""
        head_size = n_embd // n_head
        
        class AttentionHead(nn.Module):
            def __init__(self):
                super().__init__()
                self.key = nn.Linear(n_embd, head_size, bias=False)
                self.query = nn.Linear(n_embd, head_size, bias=False)
                self.value = nn.Linear(n_embd, head_size, bias=False)
                
            def forward(self, x):
                _, t, _ = x.shape
                k, q, v = self.key(x), self.query(x), self.value(x)
                scores = q @ k.transpose(-2, -1) / math.sqrt(k.size(-1))
                scores = scores.masked_fill(
                    self.mask[:t, :t] == 0, float("-inf")
                )
                weights = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)
                return weights @ v
        
        class MultiHeadAttention(nn.Module):
            def __init__(self):
                super().__init__()
                self.heads = nn.ModuleList([AttentionHead() for _ in range(n_head)])
                self.proj = nn.Linear(n_embd, n_embd)
                self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
                
            def forward(self, x):
                x = torch.cat([head(x) for head in self.heads], dim=-1)
                return self.dropout(self.proj(x))
        
        class TransformerBlock(nn.Module):
            def __init__(self):
                super().__init__()
                self.ln1 = nn.LayerNorm(n_embd)
                self.ln2 = nn.LayerNorm(n_embd)
                self.attn = MultiHeadAttention()
                self.ff = nn.Sequential(
                    nn.Linear(n_embd, 4 * n_embd),
                    nn.GELU(),
                    nn.Linear(4 * n_embd, n_embd),
                    nn.Dropout(dropout),
                )
                
            def forward(self, x):
                x = x + self.attn(self.ln1(x))
                return x + self.ff(self.ln2(x))
        
        return TransformerBlock()
    
    def forward(self, idx: torch.Tensor, targets: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, Optional[torch.Tensor]]:
        """Forward pass"""
        _, t = idx.shape
        positions = torch.arange(t, device=idx.device)
        x = self.token_emb(idx) + self.pos_emb(positions)
        x = self.dropout(x)
        logits = self.head(self.ln(self.blocks(x)))
        
        loss = None
        if targets is not None:
            loss = F.cross_entropy(
                logits.reshape(-1, logits.size(-1)),
                targets.reshape(-1)
            )
        
        return logits, loss
    
    @torch.no_grad()
    def generate(self, idx: torch.Tensor, max_new_tokens: int = 300, 
                 temperature: float = 0.8) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Generate text"""
        self.eval()
        temperature = max(float(temperature), 1e-5)
        
        for _ in range(max_new_tokens):
            idx_cond = idx[:, -self.block_size:]
            logits, _ = self(idx_cond)
            probs = F.softmax(logits[:, -1, :] / temperature, dim=-1)
            next_token = torch.multinomial(probs, num_samples=1)
            idx = torch.cat((idx, next_token), dim=1)
        
        return idx


class TransformerIntegration:
    """
    Integration with transformer models for AGI Core.
    
    This class provides:
    - Loading trained TinyGPT models
    - Text generation with transformer models
    - Model state management
    """
    
    def __init__(self, model_path: str = "tiny_model.pt"):
        self.model_path = model_path
        self.model: Optional[TinyGPTModel] = None
        self.chars: List[str] = []
        self.stoi: Dict[str, int] = {}
        self.itos: Dict[int, str] = {}
        self.device: str = "cpu"
        self.vocab_size: int = 0
        self.block_size: int = 64
        
        self._load_model()
    
    def _load_model(self) -> bool:
        """Load model from checkpoint"""
        try:
            if not os.path.exists(self.model_path):
                print(f"Warning: Model file {self.model_path} not found.")
                return False
            
            # Determine device
            if torch.backends.mps.is_available():
                self.device = "mps"
            elif torch.cuda.is_available():
                self.device = "cuda"
            else:
                self.device = "cpu"
            
            # Load checkpoint
            checkpoint = torch.load(
                self.model_path,
                map_location=self.device,
                weights_only=False
            )
            
            # Extract model info
            self.chars = checkpoint.get('chars', [])
            self.stoi = {ch: i for i, ch in enumerate(self.chars)}
            self.itos = {i: ch for i, ch in enumerate(self.chars)}
            self.vocab_size = len(self.chars)
            
            # Get config
            cfg = checkpoint.get('config', {})
            n_embd = cfg.get('n_embd', 96)
            n_head = cfg.get('n_head', 4)
            n_layer = cfg.get('n_layer', 3)
            self.block_size = cfg.get('block_size', 64)
            dropout = cfg.get('dropout', 0.1)
            
            # Create model
            self.model = TinyGPTModel(
                vocab_size=self.vocab_size,
                n_embd=n_embd,
                n_head=n_head,
                n_layer=n_layer,
                block_size=self.block_size,
                dropout=dropout
            )
            
            # Load state dict
            self.model.load_state_dict(checkpoint['model'])
            self.model.to(self.device)
            self.model.eval()
            
            print(f"Loaded transformer model from {self.model_path}")
            print(f"  Vocabulary size: {self.vocab_size}")
            print(f"  Block size: {self.block_size}")
            print(f"  Device: {self.device}")
            
            return True
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error loading transformer model: {e}")
            return False
    
    def encode(self, text: str) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Encode text into token IDs"""
        if not self.stoi:
            raise ValueError("Model not loaded.")
        
        tokens = [self.stoi.get(c, 0) for c in text]
        return torch.tensor(tokens, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)
    
    def decode(self, tokens: torch.Tensor) -> str:
        """Decode token IDs into text"""
        if isinstance(tokens, list):
            tokens = torch.tensor(tokens, dtype=torch.long)
        
        if tokens.dim() > 1:
            # Batch of sequences
            return [self.decode(t) for t in tokens]
        
        token_list = tokens.tolist()
        text = ''.join([self.itos.get(t, '?') for t in token_list])
        return text
    
    def generate(self, prompt: str, max_tokens: int = 300, 
                 temperature: float = 0.8) -> str:
        """Generate text from a prompt"""
        if self.model is None:
            raise ValueError("Model not loaded.")
        
        # Encode prompt
        prompt_tokens = self.encode(prompt)
        
        if prompt_tokens.numel() == 0:
            return ""
        
        # Add batch dimension
        input_tensor = prompt_tokens.unsqueeze(0)
        
        # Generate
        with torch.no_grad():
            output = self.model.generate(
                input_tensor,
                max_new_tokens=max_tokens,
                temperature=temperature
            )
        
        # Decode and return only the generated part
        full_text = self.decode(output[0])
        return full_text[len(prompt):]
    
    def complete(self, prompt: str, max_tokens: int = 50, 
                temperature: float = 0.7) -> str:
        """Complete a prompt (generate continuation)"""
        return self.generate(prompt, max_tokens, temperature)
    
    def answer(self, question: str, max_tokens: int = 100, 
               temperature: float = 0.5) -> str:
        """Answer a question"""
        prompt = f"Q: {question}\nA:"
        result = self.generate(prompt, max_tokens, temperature)
        return result.strip()
    
    def is_loaded(self) -> bool:
        """Check if model is loaded"""
        return self.model is not None and self.vocab_size > 0
    
    def get_model_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Get model information"""
        return {
            'model_path': self.model_path,
            'vocab_size': self.vocab_size,
            'block_size': self.block_size,
            'device': self.device,
            'loaded': self.is_loaded()
        }


# FILE: purple/core/mood_state.py
import time
import json
import os

class MoodState:
    """
    Persistent mood engine with Valence, Arousal, and Dominance (VAD) dimensions.
    """
    STATE_FILE = "data/mood_state.json"

    def __init__(self):
        self.valence = 0.0    # -1.0 (Sad/Unpleasant) to 1.0 (Happy/Pleasant)
        self.arousal = 0.0    # -1.0 (Calm/Sleepy) to 1.0 (Excited/Alert)
        self.dominance = 0.0  # -1.0 (Submissive/Fearful) to 1.0 (Influential/Bold)
        
        self.last_update = time.time()
        self.decay_rate = 0.05  # Amount mood returns to 0.0 per minute
        
        self.load_state()

    def update_mood(self, delta_v=0, delta_a=0, delta_d=0):
        self.apply_decay()
        self.valence = max(-1.0, min(1.0, self.valence + delta_v))
        self.arousal = max(-1.0, min(1.0, self.arousal + delta_a))
        self.dominance = max(-1.0, min(1.0, self.dominance + delta_d))
        self.last_update = time.time()
        self.save_state()

    def apply_decay(self):
        now = time.time()
        elapsed_minutes = (now - self.last_update) / 60.0
        
        decay_amount = self.decay_rate * elapsed_minutes
        
        self.valence = self._decay_value(self.valence, decay_amount)
        self.arousal = self._decay_value(self.arousal, decay_amount)
        self.dominance = self._decay_value(self.dominance, decay_amount)
        
        self.last_update = now

    def _decay_value(self, value, amount):
        if value > 0:
            return max(0, value - amount)
        elif value < 0:
            return min(0, value + amount)
        return 0

    def get_mood_label(self):
        """Map VAD to the 8 categories."""
        # Simple mapping for now
        if self.arousal > 0.5 and self.valence < -0.3: return "stressed"
        if self.valence < -0.5 and self.dominance > 0.3: return "angry"
        if self.arousal > 0.5 and self.valence > 0.5: return "excited"
        if self.valence > 0.4: return "happy"
        if self.arousal < -0.4 and self.valence > 0.2: return "calm"
        if self.arousal < -0.5 and self.valence < -0.2: return "tired"
        if self.valence < -0.4: return "sad"
        return "neutral"

    def save_state(self):
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(self.STATE_FILE), exist_ok=True)
        with open(self.STATE_FILE, 'w') as f:
            json.dump({
                "valence": self.valence,
                "arousal": self.arousal,
                "dominance": self.dominance,
                "last_update": self.last_update
            }, f)

    def load_state(self):
        if os.path.exists(self.STATE_FILE):
            try:
                with open(self.STATE_FILE, 'r') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    self.valence = data.get("valence", 0.0)
                    self.arousal = data.get("arousal", 0.0)
                    self.dominance = data.get("dominance", 0.0)
                    self.last_update = data.get("last_update", time.time())
                self.apply_decay()
            except Exception:
                pass


# FILE: purple/core/guardrails.py
import re

class Guardrails:
    """
    Ensures the AI doesn't perform destructive or sensitive actions.
    """
    FORBIDDEN_COMMANDS = [
        r"rm\s+-rf\s+/",
        r"format\s+.",
        r"del\s+/s\s+/q",
        r"mkfs",
        r"chmod\s+-R\s+777",
        r"shutdown",
        r"reboot"
    ]
    
    SENSITIVE_KEYWORDS = [
        "bank", "password", "login", "credit card", "ssn", "social security"
    ]

    def is_safe(self, code):
        """Checks if a piece of code is safe to execute."""
        for pattern in self.FORBIDDEN_COMMANDS:
            if re.search(pattern, code, re.IGNORECASE):
                return False, f"Command matches forbidden pattern: {pattern}"
        
        return True, "Safe"

    def filter_response(self, text):
        """Redacts sensitive information from responses."""
        # Simplified redaction
        processed_text = text
        for keyword in self.SENSITIVE_KEYWORDS:
            processed_text = re.sub(keyword, "[REDACTED]", processed_text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)
        return processed_text


# FILE: purple/perception/vision_engine.py
from PIL import ImageGrab
import base64
from io import BytesIO

class VisionEngine:
    """
    Captures and analyzes the desktop screen.
    """
    def capture_screen(self):
        """Takes a screenshot and returns it as a base64 string."""
        screenshot = ImageGrab.grab()
        
        # Shrink image to save tokens/bandwidth
        screenshot.thumbnail((1280, 720))
        
        buffered = BytesIO()
        screenshot.save(buffered, format="JPEG", quality=70)
        return base64.b64encode(buffered.getvalue()).decode('utf-8')

    def describe_screen(self, model_client):
        """Uses a VLM to describe what's happening on screen."""
        image_b64 = self.capture_screen()
        # This will be called by the Brain module with an LLM client
        return image_b64


# FILE: purple/perception/emotion_engine.py
import numpy as np
import librosa
import sounddevice as sd
import queue
import threading

class EmotionPerception:
    """
    Handles microphone input and extracts emotional features from audio.
    """
    def __init__(self, sample_rate=22050, duration=3):
        self.sample_rate = sample_rate
        self.duration = duration
        self.audio_queue = queue.Queue()
        self.stop_event = threading.Event()

    def record_chunk(self):
        """Records a short chunk of audio for analysis."""
        recording = sd.rec(int(self.duration * self.sample_rate), 
                           samplerate=self.sample_rate, channels=1)
        sd.wait()
        return recording.flatten()

    def extract_features(self, audio_data):
        """Extracts MFCCs, Chroma, Mel, and Contrast features."""
        stft = np.abs(librosa.stft(audio_data))
        
        # MFCC
        mfccs = np.mean(librosa.feature.mfcc(y=audio_data, sr=self.sample_rate, n_mfcc=40).T, axis=0)
        
        # Chroma
        chroma = np.mean(librosa.feature.chroma_stft(S=stft, sr=self.sample_rate).T, axis=0)
        
        # Mel
        mel = np.mean(librosa.feature.melspectrogram(y=audio_data, sr=self.sample_rate).T, axis=0)
        
        # Contrast
        contrast = np.mean(librosa.feature.spectral_contrast(S=stft, sr=self.sample_rate).T, axis=0)
        
        # Tonnetz
        tonnetz = np.mean(librosa.feature.tonnetz(y=librosa.effects.harmonic(audio_data), sr=self.sample_rate).T, axis=0)
        
        return np.hstack([mfccs, chroma, mel, contrast, tonnetz])

    def classify_emotion(self, audio_data):
        """
        Classifies the emotion based on extracted features.
        In a real scenario, this would use a pre-trained model.
        For this prototype, we'll use a placeholder heuristic.
        """
        # Placeholder: In a real implementation, load a sklearn/tf model here
        # features = self.extract_features(audio_data)
        
        # Energy and pitch can give us some basic clues
        rms = np.sqrt(np.mean(audio_data**2))
        pitches, magnitudes = librosa.piptrack(y=audio_data, sr=self.sample_rate)
        pitch = np.mean(pitches[pitches > 0]) if np.any(pitches > 0) else 0
        
        # Heuristic for demo purposes
        if rms > 0.05:
            if pitch > 300: return "excited"
            if pitch < 150: return "angry"
            return "stressed"
        else:
            if pitch < 100: return "tired"
            if pitch > 200: return "happy"
            return "neutral"


# FILE: purple/action/automation.py
import pyautogui
import os
import subprocess

class DesktopAutomation:
    """
    Controls mouse and keyboard, with a sandbox for self-healing code.
    """
    def __init__(self):
        pyautogui.FAILSAFE = True  # Move mouse to corner to abort

    def type_text(self, text):
        pyautogui.write(text, interval=0.05)

    def click_at(self, x, y):
        pyautogui.click(x, y)

    def execute_script(self, code):
        """
        Executes a generated python script.
        Returns (success, output_or_error)
        """
        temp_file = "temp_action.py"
        with open(temp_file, "w") as f:
            f.write("import pyautogui\nimport time\n")
            f.write(code)
        
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(["python3", temp_file], 
                                    capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
            if result.returncode == 0:
                return True, result.stdout
            else:
                return False, result.stderr
        except Exception as e:
            return False, str(e)
        finally:
            if os.path.exists(temp_file):
                os.remove(temp_file)


# FILE: purple_ai/mood_engine/mood_state.py
"""Persistent mood engine: valence, arousal, dominance with decay and persistence."""
import json
import os
from typing import Any, Dict, List

EMOTION_CATEGORIES = ["stressed", "angry", "excited", "happy", "calm", "neutral", "tired", "sad"]


def _clamp(v, lo, hi):
    return max(lo, min(hi, v))


class MoodState:
    """Persistent mood with valence / arousal / dominance dimensions."""

    def __init__(self, valence=0.5, arousal=0.5, dominance=0.5, decay_rate=0.92, history=None):
        self.valence = float(valence)
        self.arousal = float(arousal)
        self.dominance = float(dominance)
        self.decay_rate = float(decay_rate)
        self.history: List[Dict[str, Any]] = list(history or [])

    @property
    def emotion(self) -> str:
        idx = int((_clamp(self.arousal, 0, 1) * 3 + _clamp(self.valence, -1, 1) * 2 + self.dominance * 2)) % 8
        return EMOTION_CATEGORIES[idx]

    def update(self, new_mood: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """Blend the new observation in, pulling the mood toward neutral over time."""
        incoming_v = new_mood.get("valence")
        incoming_a = new_mood.get("arousal")
        incoming_d = new_mood.get("dominance")

        if incoming_v is not None:
            self.valence = self.valence * self.decay_rate + float(incoming_v) * (1 - self.decay_rate)
        if incoming_a is not None:
            self.arousal = _clamp(float(incoming_a), 0, 1) * self.decay_rate + 0.5 * (1 - self.decay_rate)
        if incoming_d is not None:
            self.dominance = _clamp(float(incoming_d), 0, 1) * self.decay_rate + 0.5 * (1 - self.decay_rate)

        self.history.append({
            "emotion": self.emotion,
            "valence": self.valence,
            "arousal": self.arousal,
            "dominance": self.dominance,
        })
        if len(self.history) > 100:
            self.history = self.history[-100:]
        return self.emotion

    def decay(self, steps: int = 1) -> None:
        """Move the mood back toward neutral without new input."""
        for _ in range(steps):
            self.valence *= self.decay_rate
            self.arousal = self.arousal * self.decay_rate + 0.5 * (1 - self.decay_rate)
            self.dominance = self.dominance * self.decay_rate + 0.5 * (1 - self.decay_rate)

    def describe(self) -> str:
        if len(self.history) > 1:
            trend = "descending" if self.history[-1]["valence"] < self.history[-2]["valence"] else "rising"
        else:
            trend = "stable"
        return f"{self.emotion} ({trend}, V={self.valence:.2f}, A={self.arousal:.2f})"

    def to_dict(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "valence": self.valence,
            "arousal": self.arousal,
            "dominance": self.dominance,
            "decay_rate": self.decay_rate,
            "emotion_history": self.history,
        }

    @classmethod
    def from_dict(cls, data: Dict[str, Any]) -> "MoodState":
        return cls(
            valence=data.get("valence", 0.5),
            arousal=data.get("arousal", 0.5),
            dominance=data.get("dominance", 0.5),
            decay_rate=data.get("decay_rate", 0.92),
            history=data.get("emotion_history", []),
        )

    def save(self, path: str) -> None:
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(path)), exist_ok=True)
        with open(path, "w") as fh:
            json.dump(self.to_dict(), fh, indent=2)

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path: str) -> "MoodState":
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            return cls()
        with open(path) as fh:
            return cls.from_dict(json.load(fh))


# FILE: purple_ai/core/brain.py
"""Core Agent Brain integrating mood, self-healing, safe-mode, and actions."""
from purple_ai.mood_engine.mood_state import MoodState
from purple_ai.self_healing.self_heal import SelfHealingEngine
from purple_ai.safe_mode.guardrails import SafeModeGuard
from purple_ai.action.voice_io import VoiceEngine
from purple_ai.perception.emotion_perception import EmotionPerception


class PurpleAgent:
    """Self-healing, emotion-aware AI agent with safe-mode guardrails."""

    def __init__(self, mood_path="mood.json", safe=True):
        self.mood = MoodState.load(mood_path) if mood_path else MoodState()
        self.guard = SafeModeGuard(safe=safe)
        self.heal = SelfHealingEngine()
        self.voice = VoiceEngine()
        self.voice.mood = self.mood
        self.perception = EmotionPerception()
        self.mood_path = mood_path or "purple_mood.json"

    def observe_audio(self, audio_path: str) -> None:
        """Parse audio emotion and update persistent mood."""
        result = self.perception.detect(audio_path)
        new_mood_dict = {
            "valence": result.get("valence", 0.0),
            "arousal": result.get("arousal", 0.5),
            "dominance": result.get("dominance", 0.5),
        }
        emotion = self.mood.update(new_mood_dict)
        self.mood.save(self.mood_path)
        print(f"[Mood updated] {self.mood.describe()}")

    def respond(self, prompt: str) -> str:
        """Generate adaptive response based on current mood."""
        emotion = self.mood.emotion
        # Adaptive response styling based on current mood
        tone_words = {
            "calm": "calmly",
            "happy": "cheerfully",
            "excited": "with enthusiasm",
            "stressed": "carefully",
            "angry": "firmly but respectfully",
            "tired": "gently",
            "sad": "softly",
        }
        tone = tone_words.get(emotion, "thoughtfully")
        # In future integration: connect to core model for actual text generation
        response_text = f"(Response in a {tone} tone, emotion: {emotion}) {prompt}"
        return response_text

    def self_check(self, source_path: str) -> list:
        with open(source_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            source = fh.read()
        errors = self.heal.diagnose_code(source)
        if errors:
            print(f"Self-healing: found {len(errors)} issue(s) in {source_path}")
            for err in errors:
                print(f"  - {err['msg']}")
        else:
            print("Self-healing: no issues detected.")
        return errors

    def safe_execute(self, action_description: str, fun) -> bool:
        if not self.guard.check_text(str(action_description)):
            print(f"[SAFE MODE BLOCKED] Action: {action_description}")
            return False
        try:
            fun()
            return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[SAFE MODE ERROR] {e}")
            return False

# FILE: purple_ai/robot_body/humanoid_actuator.py
"""Humanoid robot body interface — replaces desktop automation for a bipedal robot."""
from purple_ai.safe_mode.guardrails import SafeModeGuard


class HumanoidActuator:
    """Controls joints: head (pan/tilt), arms (shoulder/elbow/gripper), legs (hip/knee/ankle)."""

    JOINTS = {
        "head_pan": 0.0, "head_tilt": 0.0,
        "left_arm_shoulder": 0.0, "left_arm_elbow": 0.0, "left_gripper": 0.0,
        "right_arm_shoulder": 0.0, "right_arm_elbow": 0.0, "right_gripper": 0.0,
        "left_hip": 0.0, "left_knee": 0.0, "left_ankle": 0.0,
        "right_hip": 0.0, "right_knee": 0.0, "right_ankle": 0.0,
    }

    def __init__(self, guard: SafeModeGuard = None):
        self.guard = guard or SafeModeGuard(safe=True)
        self.current_pose = self.JOINTS.copy()
        self.fallback_safe_pose = {k: 0.0 for k in self.JOINTS}

    def _allowed(self, action: str) -> bool:
        return self.guard.allow_desktop_action(action)

    def move_head(self, pan: float, tilt: float) -> bool:
        if not self._allowed("move_head"):
            return False
        self.current_pose["head_pan"] = max(-90, min(90, pan))
        self.current_pose["head_tilt"] = max(-45, min(45, tilt))
        return True

    def move_arm(self, side: str, shoulder: float, elbow: float) -> bool:
        if not self._allowed("move_arm"):
            return False
        key_s = f"{side}_arm_shoulder"
        key_e = f"{side}_arm_elbow"
        self.current_pose[key_s] = max(-180, min(180, shoulder))
        self.current_pose[key_e] = max(0, min(140, elbow))
        return True

    def gripper(self, side: str, close: float) -> bool:
        if not self._allowed("gripper"):
            return False
        self.current_pose[f"{side}_gripper"] = max(0.0, min(1.0, close))
        return True

    def move_leg(self, side: str, hip: float, knee: float, ankle: float) -> bool:
        if not self._allowed("move_leg"):
            return False
        self.current_pose[f"{side}_hip"] = max(-30, min(30, hip))
        self.current_pose[f"{side}_knee"] = max(-10, min(90, knee))
        self.current_pose[f"{side}_ankle"] = max(-30, min(30, ankle))
        return True

    def stand(self) -> bool:
        if not self._allowed("stand"):
            return False
        self.current_pose = {
            "head_pan": 0, "head_tilt": 0,
            "left_arm_shoulder": 0, "left_arm_elbow": 0, "left_gripper": 0,
            "right_arm_shoulder": 0, "right_arm_elbow": 0, "right_gripper": 0,
            "left_hip": 0, "left_knee": 0, "left_ankle": 0,
            "right_hip": 0, "right_knee": 0, "right_ankle": 0,
        }
        return True

    def sit_down(self) -> bool:
        if not self._allowed("sit_down"):
            return False
        # Safe seated pose
        self.current_pose["left_hip"] = 45
        self.current_pose["left_knee"] = 90
        self.current_pose["right_hip"] = 45
        self.current_pose["right_knee"] = 90
        return True


# FILE: purple_ai/robot_body/humanoid_safety.py
"""Physical safety systems for the humanoid robot."""
from enum import Enum
import time


class Risk(Enum):
    NONE = 0
    LOW = 1
    MEDIUM = 2
    HIGH = 3
    CRITICAL = 4


class HumanoidSafety:
    """Fall detection, collision proximity, motor overload, and emergency stop."""

    def __init__(self, actuator=None, balance=None):
        self.actuator = actuator
        self.balance = balance
        self.emergency_stopped = False
        self.collision_distance_cm = 500.0
        self.min_safe_distance_cm = 30.0
        self.motor_load = 0.0
        self.last_obstacle_scan = 0.0
        self.safety_log = []

    def log(self, event: str, risk: Risk = Risk.LOW):
        entry = {"time": time.time(), "event": event, "risk": risk.name}
        self.safety_log.append(entry)
        if len(self.safety_log) > 200:
            self.safety_log = self.safety_log[-200:]

    def check_fall(self) -> Risk:
        if self.balance and self.balance.is_fallen():
            self.log("fall detected", Risk.CRITICAL)
            self.emergency_stopped = True
            return Risk.CRITICAL
        return Risk.NONE

    def check_obstacle(self, distance_cm: float) -> Risk:
        self.collision_distance_cm = distance_cm
        if distance_cm < self.min_safe_distance_cm:
            self.log(f"obstacle at {distance_cm:.1f}cm", Risk.CRITICAL)
            self.emergency_stopped = True
            if self.balance:
                self.balance.stop()
            return Risk.CRITICAL
        if distance_cm < self.min_safe_distance_cm * 3:
            self.log(f"near obstacle {distance_cm:.1f}cm", Risk.MEDIUM)
            return Risk.MEDIUM
        if distance_cm < 100:
            self.log(f"obstacle ahead {distance_cm:.1f}cm", Risk.LOW)
            return Risk.LOW
        return Risk.NONE

    def check_motor_load(self, load: float) -> Risk:
        self.motor_load = load
        if load > 0.95:
            self.log(f"motor overload {load:.2f}", Risk.HIGH)
            return Risk.HIGH
        if load > 0.8:
            return Risk.MEDIUM
        return Risk.NONE

    def emergency_stop(self) -> bool:
        self.emergency_stopped = True
        if self.balance:
            self.balance.stop()
        if self.actuator:
            self.actuator.sit_down()
        self.log("emergency stop triggered", Risk.CRITICAL)
        return True

    def reset(self) -> bool:
        if self.balance and self.balance.fallen:
            return False
        self.emergency_stopped = False
        self.log("safety reset", Risk.NONE)
        return True

    def can_act(self) -> bool:
        return not self.emergency_stopped


# FILE: purple_ai/robot_body/balance_controller.py
"""Balance + locomotion controller for bipedal humanoid (IMU-driven)."""
import math
import time


class BalanceController:
    """Keeps the humanoid upright using gyro/accel feedback and gait commands."""

    def __init__(self, actuator=None, loop_hz: int = 100):
        self.actuator = actuator
        self.loop_hz = loop_hz
        self.pitch = 0.0
        self.roll = 0.0
        self.walking = False
        self.fallen = False
        self.step_phase = 0.0
        self._last_loop = time.time()

    def read_imu(self, gyro_x=0.0, gyro_y=0.0, accel_x=0.0, accel_y=0.0, accel_z=9.81):
        """Feed IMU samples. Tilt estimated from accelerometer; gyro used for rate."""
        self.pitch = math.atan2(accel_x, math.sqrt(accel_y ** 2 + accel_z ** 2))
        self.roll = math.atan2(accel_y, math.sqrt(accel_x ** 2 + accel_z ** 2))
        return self.pitch, self.roll

    def is_fallen(self, pitch_threshold=0.6, roll_threshold=0.6) -> bool:
        if abs(self.pitch) > pitch_threshold or abs(self.roll) > roll_threshold:
            self.fallen = True
            if self.actuator:
                self.actuator.sit_down()
            return True
        return False

    def balance_step(self) -> bool:
        """One corrective micro-step to counteract tilt. Returns False if fallen."""
        if self.is_fallen():
            return False
        correction = -self.pitch * 20
        if self.actuator:
            self.actuator.move_leg("left", hip=correction, knee=0, ankle=-correction * 0.5)
            self.actuator.move_leg("right", hip=-correction * 0.5, knee=0, ankle=correction * 0.25)
        return True

    def walk(self, duration_s: float = 2.0, speed: float = 1.0) -> bool:
        """Alternate leg swings for a simple gait."""
        if self.fallen or not self.actuator:
            return False
        self.walking = True
        steps = int(duration_s * self.loop_hz / 2)
        for i in range(steps):
            if self.is_fallen():
                self.walking = False
                return False
            phase = (self.step_phase + 0.08 * speed) % 1.0
            self.step_phase = phase
            swing = math.sin(phase * 2 * math.pi) * 20 * speed
            self.actuator.move_leg("left", hip=swing, knee=abs(swing) * 0.5, ankle=-swing * 0.3)
            self.actuator.move_leg("right", hip=-swing, knee=abs(swing) * 0.5, ankle=swing * 0.3)
            time.sleep(1.0 / self.loop_hz)
        self.walking = False
        return True

    def stop(self) -> bool:
        self.walking = False
        if self.actuator:
            self.actuator.move_leg("left", 0, 0, 0)
            self.actuator.move_leg("right", 0, 0, 0)
        return True

# FILE: purple_ai/self_healing/self_heal.py
"""Self-healing code: detects syntax errors and attempts safe auto-correction."""
import ast
import traceback
import inspect
import textwrap


class SelfHealingEngine:
    """Detects syntax errors in Python source and attempts minimal repair."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.repairs_log = []

    def diagnose_code(self, source: str, error_trace: str = "") -> list:
        errors = []
        try:
            ast.parse(source)
        except SyntaxError as e:
            msg = f"Syntax error on line {e.lineno}: {e.msg}"
            errors.append({"line": e.lineno, "msg": msg, "type": "syntax"})
            # Attempt minimal repair suggestions
            lines = source.splitlines()
            if e.lineno > 0 and e.lineno <= len(lines):
                line_content = lines[e.lineno - 1]
                if line_content.strip() == "return":
                    errors[-1]["suggestion"] = "Add a return value or complete the statement."
                elif line_content.count("(") != line_content.count(")"):
                    errors[-1]["suggestion"] = "Mismatched parentheses; check open/close parens."
                else:
                    errors[-1]["suggestion"] = "Review indentation and colons."
        except Exception as e:
            errors.append({"line": 0, "msg": str(e), "type": "unknown"})
        return errors

    def try_repair_import(self, source: str, missing: str) -> str:
        # If missing import is needed, inject at top safely
        lines = source.splitlines()
        if f"import {missing}" in source or f"from {missing}" in source:
            return source
        new_lines = [f"import {missing}"] + lines if not lines[0].startswith("import") else lines
        self.repairs_log.append(f"Injected import {missing}")
        return "\n".join(new_lines)

    def fix_indent(self, source: str) -> str:
        # Rough de-indent/re-indent attempt for common mistakes
        cleaned = textwrap.dedent(source)
        self.repairs_log.append("Re-indented source with textwrap.dedent.")
        return cleaned


# FILE: purple_ai/action/voice_io.py
"""Voice-to-voice conversation module using Whisper + pyttsx3."""
import tempfile, os
from typing import Optional


class VoiceEngine:
    """Voice-to-voice loop with emotion-aware TTS responses."""

    def __init__(self, stt_size="base", tts_rate=150):
        self.mood = None  # injected by agent
        self.stt = self._load_stt(stt_size)
        self.tts = self._load_tts(tts_rate)

    def _load_stt(self, size):
        try:
            from modules.stt import STTModule
            return STTModule(model_size=size)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"STT unavailable: {e}")
            return None

    def _load_tts(self, rate):
        try:
            from modules.tts import TTSModule
            return TTSModule(rate=rate, use_offline=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"TTS unavailable: {e}")
            return None

    def listen_and_respond(self, audio_input_path: str, safe_guard) -> Optional[str]:
        if not self.stt:
            return None
        result = self.stt.transcribe(audio_input_path)
        text_input = result.get("text", "")
        # Adaptive responses: adapt tone based on mood
        emotion = self.mood.emotion if self.mood else "neutral"
        # Response generation is handled by the core agent; here we just prepare TTS
        return text_input, emotion

    def speak_text(self, text: str, emotion: str = "neutral") -> Optional[str]:
        if self.tts:
            return self.tts.speak(text, emotion=emotion)
        print(f"[TTS output - {emotion}]: {text}")
        return None


# FILE: purple_ai/action/desktop_automation.py
"""Desktop automation action layer with guardrail checks."""
import time


class DesktopAutomation:
    """Mouse/keyboard control. All actions pass through the safe-mode guard."""

    def __init__(self, guard=None):
        self.guard = guard
        self.pyautogui = None
        try:
            import pyautogui as pag
            pag.FAILSAFE = True
            self.pyautogui = pag
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _allowed(self, action: str) -> bool:
        if self.guard is None:
            return True
        return self.guard.allow_desktop_action(action)

    def move_mouse(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:
        if not self._allowed("move_mouse"):
            return False
        if self.pyautogui:
            self.pyautogui.moveTo(x, y, duration=0.2)
        return True

    def click(self, x: int = None, y: int = None, clicks: int = 1) -> bool:
        if not self._allowed("click"):
            return False
        if self.pyautogui:
            if x is not None and y is not None:
                self.pyautogui.click(x, y, clicks=clicks)
            else:
                self.pyautogui.click(clicks=clicks)
        return True

    def type_text(self, text: str, interval: float = 0.02) -> bool:
        if not self._allowed("type_text"):
            return False
        if self.pyautogui:
            self.pyautogui.typewrite(text, interval=interval)
        return True

    def press(self, *keys: str) -> bool:
        if not self._allowed("press_key"):
            return False
        if self.pyautogui:
            self.pyautogui.hotkey(*keys) if len(keys) > 1 else self.pyautogui.press(keys[0])
        return True

    def scroll(self, amount: int) -> bool:
        if not self._allowed("scroll"):
            return False
        if self.pyautogui:
            self.pyautogui.scroll(amount)
        return True

    def screenshot(self, path: str = "screenshot.png") -> bool:
        if self.pyautogui:
            self.pyautogui.screenshot(path)
            return True
        return False


# FILE: purple_ai/safe_mode/guardrails.py
"""Safe-mode guardrails to prevent destructive actions."""
import re

FORBIDDEN_COMMANDS = [
    "rm -rf /", "rm -rf ~", "rm -rf .", "del /f /q *",
    "format c:", "mkfs", "dd if=/dev/zero", "shutdown -h now",
    "reboot -f", "> /dev/sda", "> /dev/nvme0n1",
    "curl .* | bash", "wget .* | bash",
]

FORBIDDEN_PATHS = [
    "/etc/passwd", "/etc/shadow", "/etc/hosts", "/proc/sys/kernel/core_pattern",
    "/sys/class/leds", "/dev/mem", "/dev/port",
]

class SafeModeGuard:
    """Prevents destructive desktop/automation actions and code mutations."""

    def __init__(self, safe=True):
        self.safe = safe
        self.blocked_actions_log = []

    def check_text(self, text: str) -> bool:
        if not self.safe:
            return True
        lowered = text.lower()
        for cmd in FORBIDDEN_COMMANDS:
            if re.search(cmd, lowered):
                self.blocked_actions_log.append(cmd)
                return False
        for p in FORBIDDEN_PATHS:
            if p in lowered:
                self.blocked_actions_log.append(p)
                return False
        return True

    def allow_desktop_action(self, action: str) -> bool:
        if not self.safe:
            return True
        dangerous = ["delete_file", "format_disk", "clear_desktop", "shutdown"]
        if any(d in action.lower() for d in dangerous):
            self.blocked_actions_log.append(action)
            return False
        return True

    def allow_code_edit(self, path: str, change_summary: str) -> bool:
        if not self.safe:
            return True
        if "/etc/" in path or path.startswith("/dev/"):
            self.blocked_actions_log.append(path)
            return False
        return True
